## 1280033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec128-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9デルガドは鍛冶の腕だけでなく、<line-break>戦士としても一流なんだよ。<line-break>若いころは世界中を冒険していたみたいだしな。<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29重い武具を扱うには<line-break>それなりの筋力が必要になってくる。<line-break>武具も人を選ぶってことさ。おぼえておけよ。<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49ＳＴＲを鍛えれば、それだけ重い装備が<line-break>できるようになる。前衛で戦うのなら<line-break>しっかり鍛えとけよ。<end><if><value:$2C><greater-equal>%50あんたは強いな。<line-break>ほんとに、大したもんだよ。<line-break>ドワーフなら王になれたかもしれないな。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9Dergado isn't only a master smith. He's also a first-rate warrior. Apparently, he traveled the world as an adventurer in his youth.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29You need the muscle to handle heavy arms and armor. Equipment chooses its bearer, too. Remember that.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49The more you train your ＳＴＲ, the heavier the equipment you can use. If you fight on the front line, make sure you train it well.<end><if><value:$2C><greater-equal>%50You're strong. Truly impressive. Had you been a Dwarf, you might have become king.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9델가도는 대장장이 솜씨뿐 아니라 전사로서도 일류야. 젊었을 때는 세계 곳곳을 모험했던 모양이고.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29무거운 무구를 다루려면 그만한 근력이 필요해. 무구도 사람을 고른다는 거지. 기억해 둬.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49STR을 단련하면 그만큼 무거운 장비를 착용할 수 있어. 전위에서 싸울 거라면 확실히 단련해 둬.<end><if><value:$2C><greater-equal>%50당신 강하군. 정말 대단해. 드워프였다면 왕이 됐을지도 모르겠어.<end>"

## 1280034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec128-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9魔法しか効かないモンスターもいる。<line-break>魔法が使えるメンバーが、パーティーに１人は<line-break>いたほうがいいだろうな。<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29戦闘中の移動コマンドは重要だぞ。<line-break>武器が届かないときは前衛や飛び出し位置に<line-break>移動すればいいのさ。<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49貴重なアイテムを無駄使いするなよ。<line-break>道具屋で売ってない物は貴重品だと思って、<line-break>大切に、ここぞって時に使うんだ。<end><if><value:$2C><greater-equal>%50恵みの陽光みたいなアイテムは、<line-break>本当に必要な時に使うんだぞ。<line-break>無駄遣いは許されないんだ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9Some monsters can only be harmed by magic. You should have at least one party member who can cast spells.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29The Move command is important in battle. If your weapon can't reach, move to the front line or the forward position.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49Don't waste valuable items. Treat anything that isn't sold in item shops as a rarity, and save it for when you truly need it.<end><if><value:$2C><greater-equal>%50Items like Blessing Sunlight should only be used when you truly need them. Don't waste them.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9마법만 통하는 몬스터도 있어. 마법을 쓸 수 있는 동료가 파티에 한 명쯤은 있는 게 좋겠지.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29전투 중 이동 명령은 중요해. 무기가 닿지 않을 때는 전위나 돌출 위치로 이동하면 돼.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49귀한 아이템을 낭비하지 마. 도구점에서 팔지 않는 물건은 귀중품이라 생각하고 꼭 필요할 때 써.<end><if><value:$2C><greater-equal>%50은혜의 햇빛 같은 아이템은 정말 필요할 때 써야 해. 낭비하면 안 돼.<end>"

## 1280035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec128-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9弓を扱える仲間がいれば便利だろうな。<line-break>空の敵だろうが、後列に位置している敵だろうが<line-break>気にせず戦えるからな。<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29リルビー族はマヒや毒の影響を受けないんだ。<line-break>パーティーに１人いれば、全員がマヒになって<line-break>全滅なんてことを回避できるぜ。<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49未開の森の先には、選ばれた者の集う場所が<line-break>あるって話だ。あんたくらいの強さなら、<line-break>その場所へ行けるんじゃないのか。<end><if><value:$2C><greater-equal>%50西の海に闇の門の島があるそうだよ。<line-break>そこはすべての災いを生み出す恐ろしい島<line-break>だと聞いてるぜ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9A companion who can use a bow would be useful. You could fight flying enemies and enemies in the rear rank without worrying about reach.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29Lilubees are unaffected by Paralysis and poison. Having one in the party can prevent everyone from being paralyzed and wiped out.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49They say a gathering place for the chosen lies beyond the Uncharted Forest. At your strength, perhaps you can reach it.<end><if><value:$2C><greater-equal>%50They say the Island of the Dark Gate lies in the western sea. I've heard it's a dreadful island where every calamity is born.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9활을 다룰 수 있는 동료가 있으면 편리하겠지. 하늘의 적이든 후열에 있는 적이든 신경 쓰지 않고 싸울 수 있으니까.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29릴비족은 마비나 독의 영향을 받지 않아. 파티에 한 명 있으면 전원이 마비되어 전멸하는 일은 피할 수 있지.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49미개의 숲 너머에는 선택받은 자들이 모이는 곳이 있다는 이야기야. 네 정도 강함이라면 그곳에 갈 수 있지 않을까.<end><if><value:$2C><greater-equal>%50서쪽 바다에 어둠의 문의 섬이 있다고 해. 모든 재앙을 낳는 무서운 섬이라고 들었어.<end>"

## 1280036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec128-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9戦闘中のスピードは、敏捷さＡＧＩと　　　<line-break>装備品の重さによって変化するんだ。<line-break>そいつを忘れると痛い目にあうぜ。<line-break>例え重装備でも、敵の集中攻撃をくらえば<line-break>大ダメージを受ける可能性があるだろ。<line-break>パーティー内での攻撃順位は確認しとけよ。<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29仲間はたくさんできたか？<line-break>まだ、３人以上いないなら問題だぞ。<line-break>世界中の町を隅々まで歩いてみな。<line-break>必ず、運命が仲間を引き寄せてくれるから。<line-break>もちろん、困っているやつを見捨ててたり<line-break>したらダメだぜ。<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49ゼグナ鉱山では、たまに錬剛石が拾えるぜ。<line-break>拾って何ケ月かたつと、また拾えることも<line-break>あるから、見つけたら迷わず拾っときな。<end><if><value:$2C><greater-equal>%50西の果てに半島があって、<line-break>そこに古城が建っているそうだ。<line-break>なんでも、船でしか行けない場所らしいな。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9Battle speed depends on AGI and equipment weight. Heavy armor won't stop focused attacks, so always check party attack order.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29Do you have three companions? If not, explore every town. Fate draws allies to you, unless you abandon those in need.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49Zegna Mine sometimes yields Tempering Stones. More may appear months after collection, so never pass one by.<end><if><value:$2C><greater-equal>%50At the world's far western edge is a peninsula with an Old Castle, reachable only by ship.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9전투 중 속도는 민첩 AGI와 장비 무게로 변해. 그걸 잊으면 큰코다쳐. 중장비라도 적의 집중 공격을 받으면 큰 피해를 입을 수 있잖아. 파티 내 공격 순서는 확인해 둬.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29동료는 많이 생겼나? 아직 셋 이상 없다면 문제야. 세계 곳곳의 마을을 구석구석 돌아봐. 분명 운명이 동료를 끌어당겨 줄 테니까. 물론 곤란한 사람을 외면하면 안 돼.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49제그나 광산에서는 가끔 연강석을 주울 수 있어. 주운 뒤 몇 달 지나면 또 생기기도 하니까 발견하면 망설이지 말고 챙겨 둬.<end><if><value:$2C><greater-equal>%50서쪽 끝에 반도가 있고 거기에 고성이 있다더라. 배로만 갈 수 있는 곳이라는군.<end>"

## 1280037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec128-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9ソウルを宿していたときに、<line-break>あまり使わなかったスキルは、<line-break>無理に習得しなくてもいいだろう。<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29宝箱は無理に開けずに、解除に自信がつくまで<line-break>とっておくのもひとつの手だぜ。<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49西の海に、しぶきの群島があるそうだ。<line-break>そこに向かって生きて帰った者は<line-break>いないっていうから危険な所なんだろうな。<end><if><value:$2C><greater-equal>%50エンシャントの中には<line-break>魔王バロルの廃城があって、<line-break>今でも闇の力が強いんだってよ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9You don't have to learn every skill you barely used while bearing a Soul.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29You needn't force open every treasure chest. Leaving one until you're more confident at disarming it is also an option.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49They say the Spray Archipelago lies in the western sea. No one who has set out for it has ever returned alive, so it must be a dangerous place.<end><if><value:$2C><greater-equal>%50Within Ancient stands the Ruined Castle of Balor, Demon King. They say the power of darkness remains strong there even now.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9소울을 깃들였을 때 별로 쓰지 않았던 스킬은 억지로 익히지 않아도 될 거야.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29보물상자는 무리해서 열지 말고 해제에 자신이 생길 때까지 남겨 두는 것도 방법이야.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49서쪽 바다에 물보라 군도가 있다더군. 거기로 갔다가 살아 돌아온 사람이 없다니 위험한 곳이겠지.<end><if><value:$2C><greater-equal>%50엔샨트 안에는 마왕 발로르의 폐성이 있는데 지금도 어둠의 힘이 강하대.<end>"

## 1280038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec128-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルーマスの葉を使った、高級なお茶さ。<line-break>貴族たちが、噂話をしながら飲むのはこれだぜ。<line-break>そうそう、噂話と言えば…<end>"
EN: "A fine tea made from Rumas leaves. This is what nobles drink while exchanging gossip. Speaking of gossip...<end>"
KO: "루마스 잎을 쓴 고급 차야. 귀족들이 소문을 나누며 마시는 게 바로 이거지. 그래, 소문이라고 하니….<end>"

## 1280039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec128-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50猫屋敷に住んでいる賢者様は、<line-break>勇者ネメアと一緒に魔王バロルを倒した<line-break>仲間の１人らしいぜ。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%119ザギヴ将軍はライラネート神殿のとなりにある<line-break>墓地をよく訪れるそうだよ。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%150乙女の鏡に強そうなモンスターが<line-break>住み着いたらしいよ。<line-break>もう、誰か、退治してくれたかな？<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%151ここで飲んでるお客さんが、あんたのことを<line-break>話してるのを見ると、なんだか自分の事<line-break>みたいにうれしくなってしまってね。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50Apparently, the sage who lives at Cat Manor was one of the companions who defeated Balor, Demon King, alongside Hero Nemea.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%119They say General Zagiv often visits the cemetery beside Lailanate Temple.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%150Apparently, a formidable monster has taken up residence in Maiden's Mirror. I wonder if anyone has defeated it yet.<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%151Whenever I hear the customers drinking here talk about you, I can't help feeling happy, as if they were talking about me.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50고양이 저택에 사는 현자님은 용사 네메아와 함께 마왕 발로르를 쓰러뜨린 동료 중 한 명이라더군.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%119자기브 장군은 라이라네이트 신전 옆 묘지를 자주 찾는다고 해.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%150소녀의 거울에 강해 보이는 몬스터가 자리 잡았다더군. 벌써 누가 퇴치했으려나?<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%151여기서 술 마시는 손님들이 네 이야기를 하는 걸 보면 왠지 내 일처럼 기뻐져.<end>"

## 1280040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec128-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50エリエナイ公の弟エストは考古学者として<line-break>闇の神器とかいう魔法のアイテムを<line-break>研究しているそうだな。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%119自称未来の勇者ガルドランの奴、<line-break>どうもフェルムちゃんにほれたみたいでな、<line-break>しょっちゅうやって来るんだ。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%149城塞都市跡に恐ろしい魔物が住み着いて、<line-break>うっかり奥に入り込んだ駆け出し冒険者が<line-break>何人か犠牲になってるそうだよ。<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%150駆け出しの吟遊詩人が何人か訪ねてきてね、<line-break>大陸一の冒険者<value:$28>の冒険談を<line-break>知っていたら教えてくださいってさ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50Est, Duke Elienai's younger brother, is an archaeologist studying magical Artifacts of Darkness.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%119Self-styled future Hero Girldlan has fallen for little Ferme. He's always here now.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%149A fearsome monster inhabits the Fortress City Ruins. Several novices who ventured too deep fell victim to it.<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%150Novice bards asked me for tales of <value:$28>, the continent's greatest adventurer.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50엘리에나이 공의 동생 에스트는 고고학자로서 어둠의 신기라는 마법 아이템을 연구한다더군.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%119자칭 미래의 용사 갈드란 녀석, 아무래도 페름에게 반한 것 같아서 자주 찾아와.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%149성새도시 터에 무서운 마물이 눌러앉아서, 멋모르고 안쪽으로 들어간 풋내기 모험자 몇 명이 희생됐다고 해.<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%150풋내기 음유시인 몇 명이 찾아와서는 대륙 제일의 모험자 <value:$28>의 모험담을 알면 들려 달라더군.<end>"

## 1280041

## 1280042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec128-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%25あんたの噂を全然聞かないぜ。<line-break>もっとがんばってくれよ。<line-break>期待しているんだからな。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50城塞都市跡にフゴーが欲しがっている<line-break>珍しい品があるらしいよ。<line-break>機会があったら探してみるといい。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%75風の巫女エアは有名だけど、<line-break>火、水、土の巫女ってのもいるらしいね。<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%76フゴーは珍しい物だけじゃなくて有名人の<line-break>持ち物も欲しがるらしいよ。<line-break>あんたのも狙われてるんじゃないのかい？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%25I haven't heard a single rumor about you. Try a little harder. I'm counting on you.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50Apparently, there's a rare item in the Fortress City Ruins that Hugo wants. You should look for it if you get the chance.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%75Er is known as the Priestess of Wind, and apparently there are also a Priestess of Fire, a Priestess of Water, and a Priestess of Earth.<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%76Apparently, Hugo doesn't only want rare things. He also collects the possessions of famous people. Perhaps he's after something of yours.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%25네 소문은 전혀 안 들리는데. 좀 더 힘내 줘. 기대하고 있으니까.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50성채도시 유적에 휴고가 원하는 희귀한 물건이 있다더군. 기회가 되면 찾아봐.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%75바람의 무녀 에아는 유명하지만 불, 물, 땅의 무녀도 있다는 모양이야.<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%76휴고는 희귀한 물건뿐 아니라 유명인의 물건도 탐낸다더군. 네 물건도 노리는 거 아니야?<end>"

## 1280043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec128-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%45ロティの娘クリュセイスは、<line-break>ロセン王家の血をひいているらしいよ。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%79ネメアの父親は魔人だっていう<line-break>バカげた噂があるんだが、<line-break>信じる奴はいるのかね？<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%119火山岩地帯にやっかいな魔物が住み着いた<line-break>らしいな。退治をギルドに頼みに行くって、<line-break>そこでお茶を飲んでた神官が言ってたよ。<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%120闇の神器をすべて集めると破壊神ウルグが<line-break>復活するという伝説があるんだが、<line-break>この伝説には続きがあるらしいよ。<line-break>復活した破壊神を倒すことができれば、<line-break>破壊神の力を自分のものにできるってのが、<line-break>その続きなんだが、そんなの無理だよなぁ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%45Khryuseis, Lottie's daughter, is said to have Rocen royal blood.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%79An absurd rumor says Nemea's father is a Demon. Who believes that?<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%119A troublesome monster settled in the Volcanic Rock Zone. A priest taking tea here planned to ask the Guild to exterminate it.<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%120Legend says the Artifacts of Darkness revive Ulugh, God of Destruction; whoever defeats the revived god claims its power. Impossible, surely.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%45로티의 딸 크류세이스는 로센 왕가의 피를 이었다는군.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%79네메아의 아버지가 마인이라는 황당한 소문이 있는데, 그걸 믿는 사람이 있을까?<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%119화산암 지대에 성가신 마물이 자리 잡았다더군. 거기서 차를 마시던 신관이 길드에 퇴치를 의뢰하러 간다고 하더라고.<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%120어둠의 신기를 모두 모으면 파괴신 울그가 부활한대. 그 울그를 쓰러뜨리면 힘을 얻는다는 전설도 있어. 가능할까?<end>"

## 1280044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec128-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50その昔、ここにはある高名な神官がいてね、<line-break>命をかけて邪竜を封印したそうだよ。<line-break>今じゃ、そんな偉大な神官はいなくなったな。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100昔、この地から産出した聖光石は、<line-break>世界を支配するだけの力を持っていたそうだ。<line-break>今じゃ、かけらぐらいしか採れないがね。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%130この地には破壊神ウルグに従った邪竜が<line-break>封印されているらしいよ。<line-break>それが本当だと大変なことになるな。<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%131古の樹海にはエルフの聖地があるらしよ。<line-break>もちろん、普通の人間はそこまで<line-break>入り込めはしないらしいけどな。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50Long ago, a renowned priest lived here and is said to have risked death to seal away an Evil Dragon. There aren't any great priests like that anymore.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100They say the Holy Light Stone once mined here held enough power to rule the world. These days, only fragments can be mined.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%130Apparently, an Evil Dragon that served Ulugh, the God of Destruction, is sealed away in this land. If that's true, it could mean serious trouble.<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%131They say there's an Elf sanctuary in the Ancient Forest. Ordinary humans can't make it that far in, of course.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50옛날 이곳에는 이름 높은 신관이 있어 목숨을 걸고 사룡을 봉인했다고 해. 지금은 그런 위대한 신관이 없지.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100옛날 이 땅에서 산출된 성광석은 세계를 지배할 정도의 힘을 지녔다고 해. 지금은 조각 정도밖에 캐지 못하지만.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%130이 땅에는 파괴신 울그를 따랐던 사룡이 봉인되어 있다더군. 그게 사실이면 큰일이야.<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%131고대의 수해에는 엘프의 성지가 있다더군. 물론 평범한 인간은 그 안까지 들어갈 수 없다지만.<end>"

## 1280049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec128-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%49ウルカーンの長老シェムハザは、<line-break>コーンス族らしいぞ。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100竜骨の砂漠には、ラドラスの財宝が眠っている<line-break>らしいぞ。探してみるだけの価値は<line-break>あるんじゃないかな。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%130大陸の東に炎竜山があるのは知ってるか？<line-break>あそこに、鍛冶の名人が住んでいるらしいぞ。<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%131名工デルガドは知っているだろ？<line-break>実は鎧を鍛えるのは苦手らしくてな。<line-break>自分の鎧も他人に鍛えてもらっているらしいな。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%49Shemhaza, elder of Ulkarne, is apparently one of the Konsu.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100Ladras's treasure lies buried in Dragonbone Desert. It may be worth seeking.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%130Did you know Flame Dragon Mountain is in the continent's east? A master smith lives there.<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%131You know master craftsman Dergado? Armor alone defeats him; he even has someone else make his own.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%49울칸의 장로 셈하자는 콘스족이라더군.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100용골의 사막에는 라드라스의 보물이 잠들어 있다더라. 찾아볼 가치는 있지 않을까.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%130대륙 동쪽에 염룡산이 있는 건 알고 있나? 거기에 대장장이 명인이 산다더군.<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%131명공 델가도는 알지? 사실 갑옷 단련은 서툴다더군. 자기 갑옷도 남에게 단련받는 모양이야.<end>"

## 1280050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec128-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%49各地の街道にしゃべるゴブリンが<line-break>現れるらしいんだ。<line-break>自分たちのことをゴブゴブ団とか呼ぶらしいよ。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%80紺碧の洞窟に住み着いた魔物のせいで<line-break>コロル石の採掘ができないらしいよ。<line-break>なんでも、かなり強い魔物だってね。<line-break>ギルドに退治の依頼が来たらしいが<line-break>どうなったかな？<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%139この町に住んでいたコーンス族のナッジの<line-break>おじいさんの話なんだがな。<line-break>実は、魔道アカデミーの教授だったらしいよ。<line-break>あのジラークに魔法を教えたのも、<line-break>ナッジのおじいさんだって話だぜ。<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%140吟遊詩人のレルラ＝ロントンを知ってるかい？<line-break>あいつには生き別れになった娘がいるらしいよ。<line-break>けっこう意外な話だろ？<line-break>５年も家族を無視して旅してたら、奥さんも娘も<line-break>行方不明になっちまったらしい。<line-break>もっとも、家族を捜す気はないみたいだけど。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%49Talking Goblins called the Gob-Gob Gang have appeared on roads across the land.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%80A powerful monster in Azure Cave halted Korol Stone mining. The Guild was asked to exterminate it. I wonder what happened.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%139Nadge's grandfather, a Konsu once living here, taught at the Magic Academy and even taught Zsilak magic.<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%140Bard Relra-Lonton has an estranged daughter. After he ignored his family to travel for five years, his wife and daughter vanished. He seems unconcerned.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%49각지 가도에 말하는 고블린이 나타난다더라. 자기들을 고브고브단이라고 부른다나 봐.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%80감청의 동굴에 자리 잡은 마물 때문에 코롤석 채굴을 못 하고 있대. 꽤 강한 마물이라던데. 길드에 퇴치 의뢰가 들어왔다는데 어떻게 됐으려나?<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%139이 마을에 살던 콘스족 나지의 할아버지 이야기인데 말이야. 사실 마도 아카데미 교수였다고 해. 그 지라크에게 마법을 가르친 것도 나지의 할아버지라더군.<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%140렐라=론톤에겐 생이별한 딸이 있대. 5년간 가족을 두고 떠돌다 아내와 딸이 사라졌다더군. 그런데 찾을 생각도 없나 봐.<end>"

## 1280051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec128-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%49宿屋の別館を魔法で破壊した女の子…、<line-break>修理費を返すまで宿屋でただ働きさせられる<line-break>ことになったけど、まだ働いているのかな。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100ワッシャー海賊のキャプテン・カンダカンは、<line-break>どうやら死んだらしいよ。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%130ワッシャー海賊のキャプテン・カンダカンには、<line-break>ヒルダリアって名の一人娘がいたはずだが、<line-break>親の跡を継いでキャプテンをしてるのかな？<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%131宿屋の別館を破壊したユーリスって娘は、<line-break>魔道アカデミーでも有名な問題児らしいね。<line-break>建物の破壊なんてのは序の口らしいよ。<line-break>魔法で掃除しようとして歴代名誉教授像の頭を<line-break>ことごとく破壊したり、カゼをひいた友人に<line-break>マヒ薬をのませたりと悪行ざんまいだってな。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%49The girl who magically wrecked the inn annex had to work there unpaid for repairs. I wonder if she still does.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100Captain Kandakan of the Washer Pirates is reportedly dead.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%130Washer Pirate Captain Kandakan had one daughter, Hildaria. Did she succeed her parent as captain?<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%131Inn-wrecker Julls' Academy misdeeds: tried magic cleaning but blew every honorary-professor statue's head off; gave a friend with a cold a paralysis drug.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%49여관 별관을 마법으로 부순 여자애… 수리비를 갚을 때까지 여관에서 공짜로 일하게 됐다는데 아직도 일하고 있으려나.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100와셔 해적의 캡틴 칸다칸은 아무래도 죽은 모양이야.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%130와셔 해적의 캡틴 칸다칸에게 힐다리아라는 외동딸이 있었을 텐데 부모 뒤를 이어 캡틴을 하고 있을까?<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%131유리스는 마도 아카데미의 문제아래. 여관 별관을 부수고 교수상 머리를 날렸으며, 감기 든 친구에겐 마비약까지 먹였다더군.<end>"

## 1280052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec128-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50エリス王妃は、リベルダムの武器商人ロティや<line-break>同じく武器商人のアンティノと組んで、<line-break>ペウダを操っているみたいだよ。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%75リューガ家のレムオンは二刀流の使い手だ。<line-break>確か剣聖レーグもそうだよな。<line-break>　<line-break>２人ともダブルブレードというスキルを<line-break>習得しているそうだよ。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%129ゼネテスは盾の扱いがどうしようもなく<line-break>下手らしいぜ。ま、盾と片手剣を持った姿は<line-break>似合わないからかまわないと思うがな。<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%130エリス王妃の料理はうまいらしいぜ。<line-break>もっとも実際に食べたわけじゃないから、<line-break>どのくらいうまいのかは知らないけどな。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50Queen Eris joined Liberdam arms dealers Lottie and Antino to manipulate Peuda.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%75House Ryuga's Lemghon and Sword Saint Reig both practice Dual Wielding and know Double Blade.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%129Xenetes is hopeless with shields. He'd look wrong with a shield and one-handed sword anyway, so no matter.<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%130Queen Eris is said to be a fine cook. I've never tasted her food, so I can't say how good.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50에리스 왕비는 리벨덤의 무기상 로티와 같은 무기상인 안티노와 손잡고 페우다를 조종하는 것 같아.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%75류가 가문의 레무온은 이도류 사용자야. 검성 레그도 그랬던 것 같은데. 둘 다 더블 블레이드라는 스킬을 익혔다고 해.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%129제네테스는 방패를 다루는 솜씨가 정말 형편없다더라. 뭐, 방패와 한손검을 든 모습은 어울리지 않으니 상관없다고 생각하지만.<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%130에리스 왕비의 요리는 맛있다더라. 물론 실제로 먹어 본 건 아니라 얼마나 맛있는지는 모르지만.<end>"

## 1280053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec128-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50盾を装備できるのは騎士として<line-break>認められた者だけなんだそうだよ。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100ドワーフ王国の王ジンガは、<line-break>若いころに闘士として、<line-break>闘技場で活躍していたそうだよ。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%120フゴーに仕えているダイダロは、<line-break>闘技場で優勝したこともある、<line-break>なかなかの戦士らしいぜ。<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%121フゴーとフゴー夫人の愛の物語を<line-break>最後まで聞いた人間は、恐怖のあまりに<line-break>狂ってしまうらしいぜ。恐い話だよな。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50Apparently, only those recognized as knights are permitted to equip shields.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100They say King Jinga of the Dwarf Kingdom made quite a name for himself as an arena fighter in his youth.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%120Daedalo, who serves Hugo, is apparently quite a warrior. He's even won the arena tournament before.<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%121They say anyone who hears the entire love story of Hugo and Mrs. Hugo goes mad with terror. Frightening, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%50방패를 장비할 수 있는 건 기사로 인정받은 자뿐이라더군.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100드워프 왕국의 왕 징가는 젊었을 때 투사로서 투기장에서 활약했다더군.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%120휴고를 섬기는 다이다로는 투기장에서 우승한 적도 있는 꽤 뛰어난 전사라던데.<end><if><value:$2A><greater-equal>%121휴고와 휴고 부인의 사랑 이야기를 끝까지 들은 사람은 공포 때문에 미쳐 버린다더군. 무서운 이야기지.<end>"

## 1290000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかげ様で無事着くことができました。<line-break>これが報酬です。ありがとう。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I arrived safely. Here's your reward. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "덕분에 무사히 도착했습니다. 이게 보수입니다. 고마워요.<end>"

## 1290001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかげ様で命拾いしました。<line-break>これが報酬です。ありがとう。<end>"
EN: "You saved my life. Here's your reward. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "덕분에 목숨을 건졌습니다. 이것이 보수입니다. 고맙습니다.<end>"

## 1290002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご苦労様。<line-break>これが報酬だよ。<end>"
EN: "Good work. Here's your reward.<end>"
KO: "수고했어. 이게 보상이야.<end>"

## 1290003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、さようなら。<line-break>お元気で。<end>"
EN: "Well then, goodbye. Take care.<end>"
KO: "그럼 안녕히. 건강하세요.<end>"

## 1290004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "配達ご苦労様。<line-break>これが報酬だよ。<end>"
EN: "Good work on the delivery. Here's your reward.<end>"
KO: "배달 수고했어. 이게 보상이야.<end>"

## 1290005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく無事に戻ってこられたな。　　<line-break>本当にご苦労様。<line-break><line-break>さあ、これが怪物退治の報酬だ。<line-break>受け取ってくれ。<end>"
EN: "I'm glad you made it back safely. You did well. Now, here's your reward for defeating the monster. Take it.<end>"
KO: "무사히 돌아왔구나. 정말 수고했어. 자, 이게 괴물 퇴치 보수다. 받아 줘.<end>"

## 1290006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっ！　お帰り。<line-break>その表情からすると首尾は上々のようだな。<line-break>どれどれ………。<line-break>うん、確かに依頼の品だ。<line-break>じゃあ、この品物と引き換えに、<line-break>報酬を受け取ってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Oh! Welcome back. Judging by that look on your face, it seems things went well. Let's see... Yes, this is definitely the item we requested. In exchange for it, take your reward.<end>"
KO: "오! 돌아왔군. 그 표정을 보니 일이 잘 풀린 모양이야. 어디 보자………. 그래, 분명 의뢰한 물건이군. 그럼 이 물건과 맞바꿔 보수를 받아가게.<end>"

## 1290007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "次は期限を守ってくれよ。<end>"
EN: "Next time, make sure you meet the deadline.<end>"
KO: "다음엔 기한을 지켜 줘.<end>"

## 1290008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっとついた………。<line-break>期限がすぎてしまったので<line-break>報酬は半分です。<end>"
EN: "Made it at last... But the deadline has passed, so the reward is only half.<end>"
KO: "겨우 도착했다……. 기한이 지나 버렸으니 보수는 절반입니다.<end>"

## 1290009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは…、さようなら。<end>"
EN: "Well then... Goodbye.<end>"
KO: "그럼… 안녕.<end>"

## 1290010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたに護衛してもらいたいのは<line-break>こちらの方だ。<end>"
EN: "This is the person we'd like you to escort.<end>"
KO: "오히려 내가 네게 호위를 부탁하고 싶은 상황이야.<end>"

## 1290011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "長い道のりですが、<line-break>よろしくお願いします。<end>"
EN: "It's a long journey, but I hope I can count on you.<end>"
KO: "긴 여정이지만 잘 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 1290012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたがたは…、<line-break>助けに来てくれたのですね。<line-break><line-break>わたしを近くの街まで<line-break>連れて帰ってください。<end>"
EN: "You... you've come to help me, haven't you? Please take me back to a nearby town.<end>"
KO: "여러분은… 구하러 와 주셨군요. 저를 가까운 도시까지 데려다 주세요.<end>"

## 1290013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "道中、危険なモンスターや賊どもに<line-break>遭遇することもある、十分に気をつけてくれ。<line-break><line-break>お客さんを戦闘にまきこまないよう、<line-break>くれぐれも慎重に行動してくれよ。<end>"
EN: "You may encounter dangerous monsters or bandits along the way, so keep your wits about you. Above all, be careful not to let the client get caught up in any fighting.<end>"
KO: "도중에 위험한 몬스터나 도적을 만날 수도 있으니 충분히 조심해 줘. 손님을 전투에 휘말리게 하지 않도록 신중하게 행동해.<end>"

## 1290014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よお、<value:$28>。<line-break>ちょうどいいところに来てくれた。<line-break><line-break>実はな、お前さんを見込んで<line-break>ひとつ頼みがあるんだ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. You've come at just the right time. There's something I'd like to ask of you. I think you're just the person for the job.<end>"
KO: "여어, <value:$28>. 마침 잘 왔어. 사실 자네를 믿고 부탁 하나 하려던 참이야.<end>"

## 1290015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$3C><equal>1やあ、あんた。<line-break>仕事を探してるんだろ。　<line-break><line-break>実はな、お前さんを<line-break>腕のいい冒険者と見込んで<line-break>ひとつ頼みがあるんだ。<end>やあ、あんたたち。<line-break>仕事を探してるんだろ。　<line-break><line-break>実はな、お前さんたちを<line-break>腕のいい冒険者と見込んで<line-break>ひとつ頼みがあるんだ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$3C><equal>1Hello there. You're looking for work, aren't you? As it happens, I have a favor to ask. You strike me as a capable adventurer.<end>Hello there. You're looking for work, aren't you? As it happens, I have a favor to ask. You strike me as capable adventurers.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$3C><equal>1안녕, 자네. 일거리를 찾고 있지? 사실 말이야, 자네를 솜씨 좋은 모험자라고 보고 부탁 하나가 있어.<end>안녕, 자네들. 일거리를 찾고 있지? 사실 말이야, 자네들을 솜씨 좋은 모험자라고 보고 부탁 하나가 있어.<end>"

## 1290016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "数日前から<value:$15>が<line-break><value:$17>の辺りで行方知れずになっていて<line-break>捜索願いが出ているんだ。<line-break>依頼主は<value:$16>。<line-break>成功報酬は<value:$1A>ギア。<line-break>タイムリミットは今日から<value:$1B>日間だ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$15> went missing near <value:$17> several days ago, and a search request has been filed. Client: <value:$16>. Reward upon success: <value:$1A> Gea. Deadline: <value:$1B> days from today.<end>"
KO: "며칠 전부터 <value:$15>이(가) <value:$17> 부근에서 행방불명되어 수색 의뢰가 들어왔다. 의뢰인은 <value:$16>. 성공 보수는 <value:$1A>기어. 제한 시간은 오늘부터 <value:$1B>일이다.<end>"

## 1290017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうだ、やってくれるかい？<end>"
EN: "So, will you do it?<end>"
KO: "어때, 해주겠어?<end>"

## 1290018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうこなくっちゃ！<line-break>人の命がかかってるんだ。<line-break>期日厳守で頼んだよ。<end>"
EN: "That's what I wanted to hear! Human life is at stake, so make sure you meet the deadline.<end>"
KO: "그래야지! 사람 목숨이 걸려 있어. 기한은 꼭 지켜 달라고.<end>"

## 1290019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…、残念だが仕方ない。<line-break>また今度、別の機会があったら<line-break>その時は頼むよ。<end>"
EN: "I see... That's a shame, but so be it. If another opportunity comes up, I'll ask you again.<end>"
KO: "그런가… 아쉽지만 어쩔 수 없지. 다음에 다른 기회가 생기면 그때 부탁할게.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec129-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1300002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すみませんでした<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "죄송했습니다<end>"

## 1300003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何様のつもりだ<end>"
EN: "Who do you think you are?<end>"
KO: "네가 뭐라도 된 줄 알아?<end>"

## 1300004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　ならば、　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　無礼なマネは慎むことだ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Then see that you refrain from such insolence.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 무례한 행동은 삼가도록 해라.<end>"

## 1300005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　愚かな。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　身の程を思い知るがよい。<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Fool. Know your place.<end>"
KO: "어리석군. 분수를 깨닫도록 해라.<end>"

## 1300006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "翔王様！<end>"
EN: "Soaring King!<end>"
KO: "비상왕님!<end>"

## 1300007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　エアか…？　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　我が小さき娘か…？<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Er...? My little daughter...?<end>"
KO: "에아인가…? 나의 작은 딸인가…?<end>"

## 1300008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　見よ、我は死ぬ…。　　　　　　<line-break>　　　見よ、無限のソウルを持つ者を…。<line-break>　　　　　汝すら、すべてを見ること<line-break>　　　　　　　あたわざる者を…。<end>"
EN: "Behold, I die... Behold the Bearer of the Infinite Soul... one whom even you cannot see in full...<end>"
KO: "보아라, 나는 죽는다…. 보아라, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자를…. 그대조차 모든 것을 볼 수 없는 자를….<end>"

## 1300009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　希望は…ある…。　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　どんなところにも<line-break>　　　　　　絶望さえしなければ<line-break>　　　　　　　希望はあるのだ…。<end>"
EN: "There is... hope... Wherever you may be, so long as you do not despair, there is hope...<end>"
KO: "희망은… 있다…. 어디에든 절망하지만 않는다면 희망은 있는 것이다…<end>"

## 1300010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　風の巫女よ…風になれ。　　　　　<line-break>　　　お前の母たちが逃れたいと願っていた<line-break>　　　　　地獄のくびきを逃れよ…。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Priestess of Wind... become the wind. Escape the yoke of hell from which your mothers longed to break free...<end>"
KO: "바람의 무녀여… 바람이 되어라. 네 어머니들이 벗어나길 바라던 지옥의 굴레에서 벗어나라….<end>"

## 1300011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　この者ならば、　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　お前を縛る鎖をも<line-break>　　　　　軽々と引きちぎるだろう…。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "This one could easily tear apart even the chains that bind you...<end>"
KO: "이자라면 너를 묶는 사슬조차 가볍게 끊어 버리겠지….<end>"

## 1300012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　我は翔王と名乗りながら、　　　　　<line-break>　　　　すべてが自由にならなかった。<line-break>　　　　この巨大な翼も竜王の束縛を<line-break>　　　　振り切ることはできなかった。<end>"
EN: "Though I called myself the Soaring King, I was never wholly free. Even these enormous wings could not shake off the Dragon King's bonds.<end>"
KO: "나는 상왕이라 자칭하면서도 모든 것이 자유롭지는 못했다. 이 거대한 날개도 용왕의 속박을 떨쳐내지 못했다.<end>"

## 1300013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　だが、死に臨んで我は自由だ。　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　風よ、小さき娘よ…<line-break>　　　　お前も…自由に、なる…のだ…。<end>"
EN: "But now, facing death, I am free. O wind, my little daughter... you too... shall be free...<end>"
KO: "하지만 죽음을 앞둔 나는 자유롭다. 바람이여, 작은 딸이여… 너도… 자유로워지는… 것이다….<end>"

## 1300014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "翔王…様…。<end>"
EN: "Soaring King... my lord...<end>"
KO: "상왕…님….<end>"

## 1300015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…出ていって…くれぬか？<end>"
EN: "…Could you… leave?<end>"
KO: "…나가 주지 않겠나?<end>"

## 1300016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おのれ…。よくも…。<end>"
EN: "Damn you... How dare you...<end>"
KO: "이놈…. 감히….<end>"

## 1300017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ば、バカな…。<line-break>未来が…読めなかっ…た…？<end>"
EN: "I-impossible... I couldn't... read the future...?<end>"
KO: "마, 말도 안 돼…. 미래를… 읽지 못했…다고…?<end>"

## 1300018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう！<line-break>なんせさあ。翔王と<line-break>竜王でさえ一目置くエアが相手だろう？<line-break>どうしようかって思ってたんだ。<line-break>おかげで助かったよ。<line-break>うふふ、じゃ、もらってくね！<end>"
EN: "Thank you! I mean, you were up against Er―someone even the Soaring King and the Dragon King take seriously, right? I didn't know what I was going to do. You really saved me. Hee-hee! Well, I'll be taking it, then!<end>"
KO: "고마워! 상대가 상왕과 용왕마저도 인정하는 에아잖아? 어떻게 해야 하나 고민했어. 덕분에 살았네. 우후후, 그럼 받아 갈게!<end>"

## 1300019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "母よ…。<end>"
EN: "Mother...<end>"
KO: "어머니여….<end>"

## 1300020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ。　　　　　<line-break>　　　あなたが来るのを待っていました。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　私は風の精霊神プレシオーネ。<line-break>　　もしもあなたに、私の期待にそうだけの<line-break>　　　　　力があれば、私はあなたに<line-break>　　　　　最高の風を授けましょう。<end>"
EN: "Bearer of the Infinite Soul, I have been waiting for you. I am Pressione, spirit god of the wind. If your power proves equal to my expectations, I shall bestow upon you the supreme wind.<end>"
KO: "무한의 소울을 지닌 자여. 당신이 오기를 기다리고 있었습니다. 저는 바람의 정령신 프레시오네. 당신에게 제 기대에 부응할 만한 힘이 있다면, 최고의 바람을 내려드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 1300021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　この奥に潜み　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　あのお方の力を封じている<line-break>　　　　　　憎きアビスの息の根を<line-break>　　　　　　見事止めてくるのです。<end>"
EN: "Go into the depths, where the loathsome Abyss lurks and seals away the power of the exalted one, and put an end to it.<end>"
KO: "이 안쪽에 숨어 그분의 힘을 봉인하고 있는 증오스러운 어비스의 숨통을 훌륭히 끊고 오너라.<end>"

## 1300022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やってみます<end>"
EN: "I'll give it a try.<end>"
KO: "해 보겠습니다<end>"

## 1300023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "興味ないです<end>"
EN: "I'm not interested.<end>"
KO: "관심 없습니다<end>"

## 1300024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　そうですか。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　では、私はあなたを<line-break>　　　　封印の扉へと運びましょう。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　そこで、清き風の光に身を投じ<line-break>　　　　　　　その先にある地で<line-break>　　　　巨人アビスに挑んでください。<end>"
EN: "I see. Then I shall carry you to the sealed door. There, cast yourself into the pure light of the wind, then challenge the giant Abyss in the land beyond.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까. 그럼 제가 당신을 봉인의 문으로 옮겨 드리겠습니다. 그곳에서 맑은 바람의 빛에 몸을 맡기고 그 너머의 땅에서 거인 어비스에게 도전하세요.<end>"

## 1300025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　いいでしょう。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　アビスの餌食となってしまっては<line-break>　　　　　　意味がありません。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　アビスを引き裂くだけの<line-break>　　　　　　　力を付けたなら、<line-break>　　　　もう一度来るとよいでしょう。<end>"
EN: "Very well. It would serve no purpose for you to fall prey to Abyss. Return once you have gained the strength to tear Abyss apart.<end>"
KO: "좋습니다. 아비스의 먹이가 되어 버리면 아무 의미가 없지요. 아비스를 찢어 버릴 만큼의 힘을 기른 뒤 다시 찾아오도록 하세요.<end>"

## 1300026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　何を戸惑っているのです。　　　　　<line-break>　　　風の力が欲しくはないのですか？<end>"
EN: "Why do you hesitate? Do you not desire the power of the wind?<end>"
KO: "무엇을 망설이는 겁니까. 바람의 힘이 필요하지 않은 겁니까?<end>"

## 1300027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力が欲しい<end>"
EN: "I want that power.<end>"
KO: "힘이 필요해<end>"

## 1300028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今はやめておく<end>"
EN: "Not right now.<end>"
KO: "지금은 그만둔다<end>"

## 1300029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　では、お行きなさい。　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　アビスを倒したとき、あなたに<line-break>　　　　　最高の風を授けましょう。<end>"
EN: "Then go. When you have defeated Abyss, I shall grant you the supreme wind.<end>"
KO: "그럼 가십시오. 아비스를 쓰러뜨렸을 때 당신에게 최고의 바람을 내려 드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 1300030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　いいでしょう。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　アビスの餌食となってしまっては<line-break>　　　　　　意味がありません。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　アビスを引き裂くだけの<line-break>　　　　　　　力を付けたなら、<line-break>　　　　もう一度来るとよいでしょう。<end>"
EN: "Very well. There would be no point if you were to fall prey to Abyss. Return once you have gained enough strength to tear Abyss apart.<end>"
KO: "좋습니다. 아비스의 먹이가 되어 버리면 아무 의미가 없지요. 아비스를 찢어 버릴 만큼의 힘을 기른 뒤 다시 찾아오도록 하세요.<end>"

## 1300031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪しき風に送られて<line-break>深淵をのぞきに来たか？<line-break>風よりも軽き生命の人の子よ…。<line-break>ここにあるは、<line-break>真なる闇と破壊…。<line-break>触れることは許されぬ。<end>"
EN: "Has an evil wind borne you here to gaze into the abyss? O child of man, whose life is lighter than the wind... Here lie true darkness and destruction. None may touch them.<end>"
KO: "악한 바람에 떠밀려 심연을 들여다보러 왔느냐? 바람보다 가벼운 생명을 지닌 인간의 아이여… 여기에 있는 것은 진정한 어둠과 파괴다. 손대는 것은 허락되지 않는다.<end>"

## 1300032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゆえに、何人をも近寄らせぬ。<line-break>バイアス様の力をいただきし<line-break>この土の巨人、アビスがな…！<end>"
EN: "Therefore, I allow none to draw near. For I am Abyss, the giant of earth, granted the power of Lord Vyas!<end>"
KO: "그러므로 누구도 가까이 오지 못하게 한다. 바이어스님의 힘을 받은 이 흙의 거인, 아비스가…!<end>"

## 1300033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカな…！　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>バイアス様より…いただいた<line-break>この…体が…。<line-break>人の子よ…<line-break>己の罪をいずれ…知るがよい…<line-break>そして、絶望とともに悔いるがよい…。<end>"
EN: "Impossible...! This body... bestowed upon me by Lord Vyas... Child of man... one day you shall know your sin... and rue it in despair.<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼…! 바이아스 님께… 받은 이… 몸이…. 인간의 자식이여… 언젠가 자신의 죄를… 알게 될 것이다…. 그리고 절망과 함께 후회하라….<end>"

## 1300034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　よくやりました、　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ……。<end>"
EN: "Well done, Bearer of the Infinite Soul...<end>"
KO: "잘했습니다, 무한의 소울을 가진 자여…….<end>"

## 1300035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　よくぞ、アビスを倒しました。　　　　<line-break>　　　約束どおり、あなたに最高の風を<line-break>　　　　　　　授けましょう。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　あなたの魔道の力が極限まで高まり<line-break>　　　　　内なる風がまきあがる時<line-break>　　　　この最高の風はあなたを包み<line-break>　　　　仇なす者を切り刻むでしょう。<end>"
EN: "You have done well to defeat Abyss. As promised, I shall bestow upon you the supreme wind. When your magical power reaches its utmost and the wind within you rises, this supreme wind will envelop you and cut all who oppose you to pieces.<end>"
KO: "훌륭히 어비스를 쓰러뜨렸습니다. 약속대로 당신에게 최고의 바람을 내리겠습니다. 당신의 마도의 힘이 극한까지 높아져 내면의 바람이 일어날 때, 이 최고의 바람이 당신을 감싸고 적대하는 자를 갈기갈기 찢을 것입니다.<end>"

## 1300036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風の最高位の魔法が解放された。<end>"
EN: "The highest-level wind spell has been unlocked.<end>"
KO: "바람의 최고위 마법이 해방되었다.<end>"

## 1300037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　さぁ、お行きなさい。　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ。<end>"
EN: "Now go, Bearer of the Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "자, 가십시오. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여.<end>"

## 1300038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　待っていました、<value:$28>。<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ。　　　　　<line-break>　　　アビスに打ち勝つだけの力をつけ、　　　<line-break>　　　　　再びここを訪れたのですね。　　　　<end>"
EN: "I have been waiting for you, <value:$28>, Bearer of the Infinite Soul. You have gained the strength to overcome Abyss and returned here once more.<end>"
KO: "기다리고 있었습니다, <value:$28>. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여. 어비스를 이길 만큼 힘을 길러 다시 이곳을 찾아왔군요.<end>"

## 1300039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その通りです<end>"
EN: "That's right.<end>"
KO: "그 말씀대로입니다<end>"

## 1300040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec130-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなわけじゃ…<end>"
EN: "Not exactly...<end>"
KO: "그런 건 아니야…<end>"

## 1310001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec131-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>より多くの道標を望むか。<line-break>ならば、聞かせてやろう。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. Do you seek further guidance? Then I shall grant it.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 더 많은 이정표를 원하나. 그렇다면 들려주지.<end>"

## 1310002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec131-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくぞ来た、<value:$28>よ。<end>"
EN: "Welcome, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "잘 왔다, <value:$28>여.<end>"

## 1310003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec131-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "またも、進むべき道に迷い<line-break>わらわの言葉を求めるか。<line-break>ならば、聞かせてやろう。<end>"
EN: "Have you lost your way once more and come seeking my counsel? Then I shall grant it.<end>"
KO: "또 나아갈 길을 잃고 나의 말을 구하느냐. 그렇다면 들려주마.<end>"

## 1310004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec131-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わらわはエア。<line-break>このエルズの竜の座で<line-break>すべてを見る者なり。<end>"
EN: "I am Er, she who sees all from the Dragon's Seat here in Elz.<end>"
KO: "나는 에아. 이 엘즈의 용의 자리에서 모든 것을 보는 자이니라.<end>"

## 1310005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec131-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いかがした？<line-break>わらわがそなたの名を知っておるのが<line-break>それほど、驚きか？<line-break>わらわは天地千年を見通す。<line-break>そなたの名を知ることぐらい、<line-break>たやすきことよ。<end>"
EN: "What is it? Are you so surprised that I know your name? My sight spans heaven and earth across a thousand years. Knowing your name is a simple matter.<end>"
KO: "왜 그러느냐? 내가 그대의 이름을 아는 것이 그리 놀라운가? 나는 천지 천년을 내다본다. 그대 이름을 아는 정도는 쉬운 일이지.<end>"

## 1310006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec131-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、そなたは道標を求め<line-break>わらわを訪ねたのであろう？<line-break>ならば、聞かせてやろう。<end>"
EN: "Now, you came to me seeking guidance, did you not? Then I shall grant it.<end>"
KO: "자, 너는 길잡이를 찾아 나를 찾아온 것이겠지? 그렇다면 들려주마.<end>"

## 1310007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec131-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの南東にある賢者の森。<line-break>選ばれた者のみに見える館が<line-break>汝を迎え入れるであろう。<line-break>そなたの真の旅は<line-break>そこから始まるであろう…。<end>"
EN: "In Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient, a manor visible only to the chosen shall welcome you. There your true journey shall begin...<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽의 현자의 숲. 선택받은 자에게만 보이는 저택이 그대를 맞이할 것이다. 그대의 진정한 여행은 그곳에서 시작되리라….<end>"

## 1310008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec131-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "汝、かつての栄華の跡なる地より<line-break>常なる世界を越え<line-break>闇の淵へと迷い込まん。<line-break>そこで汝は<line-break>若き獅子の雄々しさと<line-break>その背負いたる宿業を見るであろう。<end>"
EN: "From a land marked by the remnants of former glory, you shall pass beyond the ordinary world and stray into the abyss of darkness. There you shall witness the valor of a young lion and the fateful burden borne upon those shoulders.<end>"
KO: "그대는 옛 영화의 흔적이 남은 땅에서 일상의 세계를 넘어 어둠의 심연으로 헤매어 들 것이다. 거기서 젊은 사자의 용맹과 그가 짊어진 숙업을 보게 되리라.<end>"

## 1310009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec131-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無限のソウルを持つ者は、<line-break>汝だけにあらず。<line-break>今ひとつの魂が無限の輝きを宿す。<line-break>千年樹としんきろう、ふたつを<line-break>結びし古の道にて<line-break>汝とその魂は出会うであろう。<end>"
EN: "You are not the only Bearer of the Infinite Soul. Another soul holds that same infinite radiance. On the ancient road linking the Thousand-Year Tree and the Mirage, you and that soul shall meet.<end>"
KO: "무한의 소울을 지닌 자는 너만이 아니다. 또 하나의 영혼이 무한의 빛을 품고 있다. 천년수와 신기루, 둘을 잇는 옛길에서 너와 그 영혼은 만나게 되리라.<end>"

## 1310010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec131-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "汝と対をなす無限の魂も<line-break>その生きる道を旅の中に見た。<line-break>その周りには、３つの魂あり。<line-break>今からそう遠くはないいつか、<line-break>都市にて仕事をなさんとする時<line-break>汝らは再会するであろう。<end>"
EN: "The Infinite Soul paired with yours has also found its path in life through its journey. Three souls surround it. Before long, when you set out to undertake a task in a city, you shall be reunited.<end>"
KO: "그대와 짝을 이루는 무한의 혼 또한 살아갈 길을 여행 속에서 보았다. 그 주위에는 세 개의 혼이 있다. 그리 멀지 않은 어느 날, 도시에서 일을 하려 할 때 그대들은 다시 만나게 되리라.<end>"

## 1310011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec131-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "青き死神、　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>東の暗愚王の業に怒り<line-break>裁きの鎌を振るわんとす。<line-break>汝、死神の力になりたくば、<line-break>閉ざされし国の門を叩くがよい。<end>"
EN: "The Blue Reaper, enraged by the deeds of the foolish king in the east, prepares to swing the scythe of judgment. If you would lend the Reaper your strength, knock upon the gates of the closed realm.<end>"
KO: "푸른 사신이여, 동쪽 암우왕의 업에 분노하여 심판의 낫을 휘두르려 한다. 그대가 사신의 힘이 되고자 한다면 닫힌 나라의 문을 두드려라.<end>"

## 1310012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec131-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールにふたりの王女あり。<line-break>ひとりは光、<line-break>今ひとりは闇をまとう。<line-break>今この大地には戦乱の兆しあり。<line-break>彼女らに会うべき想いあらば<line-break>急ぎ彼の国へと向かうがよい。<end>"
EN: "There are two princesses in Rostorl. One is light; the other is clad in darkness. The shadow of war now hangs over this land. If you feel compelled to meet them, make haste for that realm.<end>"
KO: "로스톨에는 두 왕녀가 있다. 하나는 빛, 다른 하나는 어둠을 두른다. 지금 이 대지에는 전란의 조짐이 있다. 그녀들을 만나야 할 뜻이 있다면 서둘러 그 나라로 향하라.<end>"

## 1310013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec131-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この大陸が揺らいでおる。<line-break>ふたつの大いなる力の交錯じゃ。<line-break>　<line-break>汝に守るべき縁あらば、<line-break>その者と道を共にするがよい。<line-break>汝が、生きる力となろう。<end>"
EN: "This continent is trembling as two great powers collide. If there is someone you are bound to protect, walk the same path with that person. That person shall become the strength that sustains you.<end>"
KO: "이 대륙이 흔들리고 있다. 두 거대한 힘이 교차하는구나. 네게 지켜야 할 인연이 있다면 그자와 길을 함께하라. 그것이 네가 살아갈 힘이 될 것이다.<end>"

## 1310014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec131-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自由と欲望とで築かれし町、　　　　<line-break>やがて死神の吐息を受けん。<line-break>その、滅びの運命がゆえに。<line-break>しかし、富裕と孤独とをまといし娘が<line-break>死神の鎌に逆らおうとしておる。<line-break>牙を共にするも面白かろう。<end>"
EN: "A city built on freedom and desire shall soon feel the Reaper's breath, for destruction is its fate. Yet a young woman cloaked in wealth and solitude seeks to defy the Reaper's scythe. It may prove interesting to bare your fangs beside her.<end>"
KO: "자유와 욕망으로 세워진 도시, 머지않아 사신의 숨결을 받으리라. 그 멸망의 운명 때문에. 하지만 부와 고독을 두른 아가씨가 사신의 낫에 맞서려 하고 있다. 함께 이를 드러내는 것도 재미있겠군.<end>"

## 1310015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec131-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いにしえの日より<line-break>大いなる力のために<line-break>血を連ねし４人の巫女あり。<line-break>やがて巫女らは<line-break>東方の魔道士にかどわかされ<line-break>空舞う恐怖の鍵となろう。<end>"
EN: "Since ancient days, there have been four priestesses bound by blood for the sake of a great power. In time, they shall be carried off by a sorcerer from the east and become the key to a terror that soars through the skies.<end>"
KO: "태고부터 위대한 힘을 위해 피를 이은 네 명의 무녀가 있었다. 이윽고 무녀들은 동방의 마도사에게 현혹되어 하늘을 나는 공포의 열쇠가 되리라.<end>"

## 1310016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec131-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今そなたに示すべき道は<line-break>このようなものじゃ。<line-break>進むがよい。<value:$28>よ。<end>"
EN: "That is all the guidance I have for you now. Go forth, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "지금 그대에게 보일 길은 이와 같다. 나아가거라, <value:$28>여.<end>"

## 1320001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec132-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（あれが…、空中都市…。<line-break>　もしかしたらあの中には<line-break>　彼女がいたのかもしれない…。）<end>"
EN: "(That's... the Floating City... Maybe she was in there...)<end>"
KO: "(저게… 공중도시…. 어쩌면 그 안에 그녀가 있었을지도 몰라….)<end>"

## 1320002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec132-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　小さき者よ…<line-break>　　　　　　　エアの心を乱すな…。<line-break>　　　　　２度とここを訪れるでない。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Little one... Do not disturb Er's heart. Never return here again.<end>"
KO: "작은 자여… 에아의 마음을 어지럽히지 마라…. 두 번 다시 이곳을 찾지 마라.<end>"

## 1320003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec132-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　エアは世界の均衡を守るべき装置。　　　<line-break>　　　　　　強大な力を持ちながら、　　　　　<line-break>　　　　希望なきゆえに野心を抱かず、　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　絶望ゆえに従順…。　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Er is a device meant to preserve the Balance of the World. Though she possesses immense power, without hope she harbors no ambition, and in despair remains obedient...<end>"
KO: "에아는 세계의 균형을 지키기 위한 장치. 강대한 힘을 가졌지만 희망이 없기에 야심을 품지 않고, 절망하기에 순종한다….<end>"

## 1320004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec132-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　竜王様はエアという道具を　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　手放すおつもりはない…。<line-break>　　汝にエアの手を引いて、希望の地へ<line-break>　　　連れて行かせるわけにはゆかぬ…。　　<end>"
EN: "The Dragon King has no intention of relinquishing the tool called Er... I cannot allow you to take Er by the hand and lead her to a land of hope...<end>"
KO: "용왕님은 에아라는 도구를 놓아줄 생각이 없다…. 네가 에아의 손을 잡고 희망의 땅으로 데려가게 둘 수는 없다….<end>"

## 1320005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec132-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　汝、小さき者よ…、　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　次に我の姿を見るときは、<line-break>　　　我の息吹に身を焼かれるときだと思え。<end>"
EN: "You, little one... Know that the next time you behold me will be when my breath sears your flesh.<end>"
KO: "그대, 작은 자여…. 다음에 나의 모습을 보게 될 때는 내 숨결에 몸이 불타는 때라 생각하라.<end>"

## 1320006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec132-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　愚かなり…。<line-break>　　　　我が警告を無視するとは…。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Foolish... To think you would ignore my warning...<end>"
KO: "어리석군…. 내 경고를 무시하다니….<end>"

## 1320007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec132-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　竜はいかなる時も<line-break>　　　　　真なる言葉しかはかぬこと、<line-break>　　　　その身をもって思い知るがよい。　　　<end>"
EN: "Learn firsthand that a dragon always speaks nothing but the truth.<end>"
KO: "용은 어느 때든 진실한 말만 한다는 것을 그 몸으로 똑똑히 깨달아라.<end>"

## 1320008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec132-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　エアか…？<line-break>　　　　　　我が小さき娘か…？　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Er...? My little daughter...?<end>"
KO: "에아인가…? 나의 작은 딸인가…?<end>"

## 1320009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec132-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　見よ、我は死ぬ。<line-break>　　　見よ、無限のソウルを持つ者を…。　　　<line-break>　　　　汝にすら、すべてを見ること<line-break>　　　　　　あたわざる者を…。<end>"
EN: "Behold, I die. Behold the Bearer of the Infinite Soul... one whom even you cannot see in full...<end>"
KO: "보아라, 나는 죽는다. 보아라, 무한의 소울을 가진 자를…. 그대조차 모든 것을 볼 수 없는 자를….<end>"

## 1320010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec132-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　希望は…ある…。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　どんなところにも<line-break>　　　　　　絶望さえしなければ<line-break>　　　　　　　希望はあるのだ。<end>"
EN: "There is... hope. Hope can be found anywhere, so long as you do not despair.<end>"
KO: "희망은… 있다…. 어디에라도 절망하지만 않는다면 희망은 있는 것이다.<end>"

## 1320011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec132-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　風の巫女よ…風になれ。<line-break>　　　　　　　お前の母たちが<line-break>　　　　　　逃れたいと願っていた<line-break>　　　　　地獄のくびきから逃れよ…。　　　　<end>"
EN: "Priestess of Wind... become the wind. Escape the yoke of hell from which your mothers longed to be free...<end>"
KO: "바람의 무녀여… 바람이 되어라. 네 어머니들이 벗어나고자 했던 지옥의 굴레에서 벗어나라….<end>"

## 1320012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec132-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　この者ならば、　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　お前を縛る鎖をも<line-break>　　　　　軽々と引きちぎるだろう…。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "This one could easily tear apart even the chains that bind you...<end>"
KO: "이자라면 너를 묶는 사슬조차 가볍게 끊어 버리겠지….<end>"

## 1320013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec132-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　我は翔王と名乗りながら、　　　　　<line-break>　　　　すべてが自由にならなかった。<line-break>　　　　この巨大な翼も竜王の束縛を<line-break>　　　　振り切ることはできなかった。<end>"
EN: "Though I called myself the Soaring King, I was never wholly free. Even these enormous wings could not shake off the Dragon King's bonds.<end>"
KO: "나는 상왕이라 자칭하면서도 모든 것이 자유롭지는 못했다. 이 거대한 날개도 용왕의 속박을 떨쳐내지 못했다.<end>"

## 1320014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec132-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　だが、死に臨んで我は自由だ。　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　風よ、小さき娘よ…。<line-break>　　　　お前も…自由に、なるのだ…。<end>"
EN: "Yet now, as death approaches, I am free. O wind, my little daughter... You too... shall be free...<end>"
KO: "하지만 죽음을 앞둔 나는 자유롭다. 바람이여, 작은 딸이여…. 너도… 자유로워지는 것이다….<end>"

## 1320015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec132-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "翔王…様…。<end>"
EN: "Soaring King... my lord...<end>"
KO: "상왕…님….<end>"

## 1320016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec132-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>12…竜の強さはあんなものじゃない。<line-break>翔王はお前さんに<line-break>倒してもらいたかったんだ…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>20死に急ぐか…翔王…。<line-break>…全力のお前と戦いたかった。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>8なんとも…悲しい魂ですね。<line-break>このような形でしか、あなたに<line-break>運命を託せなかったのですから。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>16…高潔な魂は束縛を拒む。<line-break>死を望み、本気を出さぬか、翔王…。<line-break>　<line-break>私は全力で戦った。<line-break>真に強き者への礼儀だ…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>11<value:$28>…。<line-break>竜の本来の強さはこの程度じゃないわ。<line-break>彼…あなたに倒されたがっていたのね。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>3竜の強さはこんなものではない。<line-break><value:$28>よ、<line-break>翔王はお主に倒されたかったんじゃな。 <end>………………。 <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>12A dragon is far stronger than that. The Soaring King wanted to lose to you...<end><if><value:$01><equal>20So eager to die, Soaring King...? I wanted to fight you at your full strength.<end><if><value:$01><equal>8What a sorrowful soul. There was no other way to entrust destiny to you.<end><if><value:$01><equal>16A noble soul refuses to be bound. Do you seek death and refuse to fight in earnest, Soaring King...? I fought with all my strength. It is the courtesy owed to the truly strong...<end><if><value:$01><equal>11<value:$28>... That was nowhere near a dragon's true strength. The Soaring King... wanted to lose to you.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3A dragon is far stronger than that. <value:$28>, the Soaring King wanted to lose to you.<end>...<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>12…용의 강함은 저 정도가 아니야. 상왕은 네게 쓰러지고 싶었던 거다…<end><if><value:$01><equal>20죽음을 재촉하는가… 상왕… 나는 전력을 다한 너와 싸우고 싶었다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>8참으로… 슬픈 영혼이군요. 이런 방식으로밖에 당신에게 운명을 맡길 수 없었으니까요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>16…고결한 영혼은 속박을 거부한다. 죽음을 바라며 전력을 내지 않는가, 상왕… 나는 전력으로 싸웠다. 진정 강한 자에 대한 예의다…<end><if><value:$01><equal>11<value:$28>… 용의 본래 힘은 이 정도가 아니야. 그… 당신에게 쓰러지고 싶었던 거네.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3용의 강함은 이런 정도가 아니다. <value:$28>여, 상왕은 자네에게 쓰러지고 싶었던 게로군.<end>……………….<end>"

## 1320017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec132-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…出ていって…くれぬか？<end>"
EN: "…Could you… leave?<end>"
KO: "…나가 주지 않겠나?<end>"
