## 1330001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　エアは<line-break>　　　　　　　　虚無の子に<line-break>　　　　　さらわれてしまった…。　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "The Child of the Void has taken Er…<end>"
KO: "에아는 허무의 아이에게 납치당하고 말았다….<end>"

## 1330002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ。　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　エアは竜骨の砂漠だ。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　エアを<line-break>　　　　　　　　救うのだ。<end>"
EN: "Bearer of the Infinite Soul. Er is in the Dragonbone Desert. Save her.<end>"
KO: "무한의 소울을 지닌 자여. 에아는 용골의 사막에 있다. 에아를 구하라.<end>"

## 1330003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　エアは死んでしまった…。　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　もはや、我が未来のために<line-break>　　　　　　　守るべきものは<line-break>　　　　　　なくなってしまった…。<end>"
EN: "Er is dead... There is nothing left for me to protect for the sake of my future...<end>"
KO: "에아는 죽었다… 이제 내 미래를 위해 지켜야 할 것은 아무것도 남지 않았다…<end>"

## 1330004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ、　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　我に見せてはくれぬか？<line-break>　　　　　　そなたのその力を。<line-break>　　　　　　　未来への希望を。<end>"
EN: "Bearer of the Infinite Soul, will you not show me? Show me that power of yours. Show me hope for the future.<end>"
KO: "무한의 소울을 지닌 자여, 내게 보여 주지 않겠느냐? 그 힘을. 미래를 향한 희망을.<end>"

## 1330005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "期待に応えてみせます<end>"
EN: "I'll live up to your expectations.<end>"
KO: "기대에 부응하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1330006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたも希望なのです<end>"
EN: "You are hope, too.<end>"
KO: "당신도 희망입니다.<end>"

## 1330007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　では、ゆくぞ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者、<line-break>　　　　　いや、<value:$28>よ。<end>"
EN: "Then let us begin. Bearer of the Infinite Soul―no, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 간다. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자, 아니, <value:$28>여.<end>"

## 1330008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　見せてもらったぞ。　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　感じさせてもらったぞ。<line-break>　　　そなたの持つ、大いなる可能性を。<line-break>　　　　希望は、まだあるということを。<end>"
EN: "I have seen it. I have felt it: the great potential within you, and the truth that hope still remains.<end>"
KO: "보았다. 느꼈다. 네가 지닌 커다란 가능성을. 아직 희망은 남아 있다는 것을.<end>"

## 1330009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　我は、すでに古く、朽ちゆく存在。　　　<line-break>　　　　そなたらの未来には要らぬもの。<line-break>　　　　　　　　さらばだ。<line-break>　　　小さく、弱く、強く、優しき者よ。<end>"
EN: "I am already ancient, a being slowly crumbling away. Your future has no need of me. Farewell, you who are small and weak, yet strong and kind.<end>"
KO: "나는 이미 낡고 썩어 가는 존재. 너희의 미래에는 필요 없는 것. 잘 있어라. 작고 약하며, 강하고 다정한 자여.<end>"

## 1330010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は<line-break>竜殺しの称号を手に入れた。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> earned the title of Dragonslayer.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>은 용살자의 칭호를 손에 넣었다.<end>"

## 1330011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　この我が……希望……か。　　　　　<line-break>　　　　不思議なものだ。汝が言うと<line-break>　　　いかにもという感じがしてくる。<end>"
EN: "That I... am hope...? A strange thing. When you say it, somehow it begins to feel true.<end>"
KO: "이 내가…… 희망……인가. 신기하군. 그대가 말하니 정말 그럴듯하게 들리는구나.<end>"

## 1330012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　ならば、我も在りつづけよう。　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　やがて新しき希望が<line-break>　　　　この地に生まれるのを待ち、<line-break>　　　　そして、守り育んでゆこう。<end>"
EN: "Then I, too, shall endure. I will wait for a new hope to be born in this land, and I shall protect and nurture it.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 나 또한 계속 존재하리라. 머지않아 새로운 희망이 이 땅에 태어나길 기다리며, 그리고 지켜 키워 가리라.<end>"

## 1330013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　では、さらばだ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ。<line-break>　　　　　我はまだ見ぬ希望を待つ。<line-break>　　　　汝は、希望をもたらしにゆけ。<end>"
EN: "Then farewell, Bearer of the Infinite Soul. I shall await a hope as yet unseen. Go forth and bring hope.<end>"
KO: "그럼 잘 있어라. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여. 나는 아직 보지 못한 희망을 기다리겠다. 그대는 희망을 가져오러 가라.<end>"

## 1330014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>。<line-break>本当にそなたが参るとは…。<line-break>いや、なんでもない。こちらの話じゃ。<line-break>今日はそなたに<line-break>聞いてもらいたいことがあったのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "…<value:$28>. To think you truly came... No, never mind. It was merely a thought of my own. There was something I wanted you to hear today.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>. 정말 네가 올 줄은…. 아니, 아무것도 아니다. 이쪽 이야기다. 오늘은 네게 들려주고 싶은 이야기가 있었다.<end>"

## 1330015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "風の巫女は、魔法によって　　<line-break>その命の連続を築いてきた。<line-break>　<line-break>わかりやすく言えば、<line-break>次の巫女となる自分の娘を<line-break>魔法で生み出してきたのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "The Priestesses of Wind have used magic to perpetuate their line. Put simply, each has used magic to bring forth her own daughter, who would become the next priestess.<end>"
KO: "바람의 무녀는 마법으로 생명의 연속을 이어 왔다. 쉽게 말하면 다음 무녀가 될 자신의 딸을 마법으로 낳아 온 것이다.<end>"

## 1330016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自らの魔力を守るために　　　　<line-break>他者との血の交わりを嫌い<line-break>自然の理に背いてな……。<line-break>ゆえに風の巫女は、<line-break>普通の人間の半分も生きられぬ。<end>"
EN: "To preserve their own magical power, they shunned the mingling of their blood with another's and defied the laws of nature. Thus, a Priestess of Wind cannot live even half as long as an ordinary human.<end>"
KO: "자신의 마력을 지키기 위해 타인과 피를 섞는 걸 꺼리고 자연의 이치를 거슬렀지…. 그래서 바람의 무녀는 평범한 인간의 절반도 살지 못한다.<end>"

## 1330017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わらわは絶望した。　　　　　　　　<line-break>この、悲しき営みに。<line-break>　<line-break>子を残すために魔力を使い果たし<line-break>おぞましくくずれ落ちる<line-break>歴代の風の巫女と、自分の運命に…。<end>"
EN: "I despaired at this sorrowful cycle―at the fate of every Priestess of Wind who had come before me, spending all her magic to leave behind a child, only to collapse into a hideous ruin... and at the fate awaiting me.<end>"
KO: "나는 절망했다. 이 슬픈 반복에. 아이를 남기기 위해 마력을 다 써버리고 흉측하게 무너져 내리는 역대 바람의 무녀와 나 자신의 운명에….<end>"

## 1330018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それゆえ、わらわは　　　　　<line-break>シャリの言葉に惹かれた。<line-break>　<line-break>運命を断ち切る力であれば、<line-break>それがたとえ絶望であっても<line-break>すがりたいと思ったのじゃ…。<end>"
EN: "That was why Shalli's words drew me in. If there was a power that could sever the bonds of fate, I wanted to cling to it―even if that power was despair itself.<end>"
KO: "그래서 나는 샤리의 말에 끌렸다. 운명을 끊을 힘이라면 설령 그것이 절망이라 해도 매달리고 싶었던 것이다…<end>"

## 1330019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最後まで聞くのじゃ…。<end>"
EN: "Hear me out to the end.<end>"
KO: "끝까지 듣거라….<end>"

## 1330020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、そなたが、<line-break>絶望のはざまにしか道を見出せなかった<line-break>わらわを救ってくれた。<line-break>わらわは決めた。<line-break>魔法で子を産むのはよそう、<line-break>運命にあらがおう、と。<end>"
EN: "But you saved me, when I could see no path except one through despair. And so I made my choice. I would not bear a child through magic. I would defy fate.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그대가 절망 사이에서밖에 길을 찾지 못했던 나를 구해 주었다. 나는 결심했다. 마법으로 아이를 낳는 것은 그만두고 운명에 맞서겠다고.<end>"

## 1330021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "運命はひとつではない。<line-break>それは可能性のひとつにすぎない…。<end>"
EN: "There is not just one fate. It is only one possibility among many.<end>"
KO: "운명은 하나가 아니다. 그것은 가능성 중 하나일 뿐….<end>"

## 1330022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "運命と絶望があるのではなく、　　　<line-break>可能性と希望がある…。<line-break>　<line-break>そう、そなたが教えてくれたからな。<end>"
EN: "It is not fate and despair that lie before us, but possibility and hope. You taught me that.<end>"
KO: "운명과 절망이 있는 게 아니라 가능성과 희망이 있다…. 그래, 그대가 가르쳐 주었으니까.<end>"

## 1330023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わらわは、今日、　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>わらわの決心を<line-break>そなたに聞いてもらいたかった。<line-break>それゆえ、わらわは<line-break>そなたがここを訪れることを望み、<line-break>そして、今日、そなたはここへ参った。<end>"
EN: "Today, I wanted you to hear the choice I had made. And so I wished that you would visit this place―and today, you came.<end>"
KO: "나는 오늘 내 결심을 그대에게 들려주고 싶었다. 그래서 그대가 이곳을 찾아오길 바랐고, 오늘 그대는 이곳에 왔다.<end>"

## 1330024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…願いが、…かのうたのじゃ。<line-break>希望とは、つまり<line-break>こういうことなのじゃな…。<end>"
EN: "My wish... has come true. So this is what hope means.<end>"
KO: "…소원이… 이루어진 게야. 희망이란, 결국 이런 것이었구나….<end>"

## 1330025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱ、ちょっと殺風景だよ。<line-break>僕にとっては翔王が死んでてくれて<line-break>好都合なんだけどさ。<end>"
EN: "Still, it does feel a little bare. Though having the Soaring King dead works out rather well for me.<end>"
KO: "역시 좀 썰렁하네. 나로서는 쇼왕이 죽어 있는 편이 편하긴 하지만.<end>"

## 1330026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリか…。抵抗はせぬ。<line-break>わらわを、ラドラスへ<line-break>連れて行くがよい…。<end>"
EN: "Shalli... I shall not resist. Take me to Ladras.<end>"
KO: "샤리인가…. 저항하지 않겠다. 나를 라드라스로 데려가라….<end>"

## 1330027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おとなしく僕に連れ去られ、<line-break>空中都市を復活させる。<line-break>　<line-break>それが、君に見えた運命から<line-break>君が選んだ道なんだね？<end>"
EN: "You'll let me carry you off without a fight and restore the Floating City. So that's the path you chose based on the destiny you saw?<end>"
KO: "얌전히 내게 붙잡혀 공중도시를 부활시킨다. 그게 네가 본 운명에서 네가 고른 길이구나?<end>"

## 1330029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>君の力で見える運命ってさ、<line-break>たったひとつっきりなの？<line-break>いろんな運命っていうかさ、<line-break>例えば君が僕に抵抗したくなるような、<line-break>そんな運命は見えなかったの？<end>"
EN: "Tell me, is there only one destiny your power lets you see? Aren't there others? One where you feel like resisting me, for instance. Didn't you see anything like that?<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 네 힘으로 보이는 운명은 딱 하나뿐이야? 여러 운명이라든가, 예를 들면 네가 나에게 저항하고 싶어지는 그런 운명은 보이지 않았어?<end>"

## 1330030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わらわに見える運命にも<line-break>いくらかの、ぶれやねじれはある。<line-break>　<line-break>しかし、それらがたどりつく<line-break>運命の帰結点は、ひとつじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Even the destinies I see contain some variation and distortion. Yet they all arrive at the same final outcome.<end>"
KO: "내게 보이는 운명에도 어느 정도 흔들림과 뒤틀림은 있다. 하지만 그것들이 도달하는 운명의 종착점은 하나다.<end>"

## 1330031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーん。ね、じゃあさ、<line-break>運命がたったひとつに<line-break>帰結する点って、いつのことなの？<end>"
EN: "Hmm. Then tell me: when exactly do all those destinies converge on a single outcome?<end>"
KO: "흐음. 그럼 말이야, 운명이 단 하나로 귀결되는 때라는 건 언제야?<end>"

## 1330032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは………。<end>"
EN: "That is...<end>"
KO: "그건……….<end>"

## 1330033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕が答えてあげよっか？　　　<line-break>運命なんて本当は<line-break>帰結してないんでしょ？<line-break>君がこうしているのは、<line-break>君が、それを望んでいるから。<line-break>そうでしょ？<end>"
EN: "Want me to answer for you? Destiny doesn't really converge at all, does it? You're doing this because it's what you want. Isn't that right?<end>"
KO: "내가 대답해 줄까? 운명은 사실 귀결된 게 아니지? 네가 이렇게 있는 건 네가 그걸 원하기 때문이야. 그렇지?<end>"

## 1330034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "空中都市を浮かばせて、　　　　<line-break>運命から逃げたいんでしょ？<line-break>君が見た、君の運命から。<line-break>先代までの風の巫女みたいに<line-break>むごたらしい姿で朽ちていく<line-break>自分と、その娘たちの運命から！<end>"
EN: "You want to raise the Floating City and escape destiny, don't you? The destiny you saw for yourself: you and your daughters rotting away in some hideous state, just like every Priestess of Wind before you!<end>"
KO: "공중도시를 띄워서 운명에서 도망치고 싶은 거지? 네가 본 네 운명에서. 선대 바람의 무녀들처럼 처참한 모습으로 스러져 가는 자신과 그 딸들의 운명에서!<end>"

## 1330035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言うな！<end>"
EN: "Say no more!<end>"
KO: "말하지 마!<end>"

## 1330036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかったよ。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>僕は君が来てくれるなら<line-break>それでいいんだ。<line-break>じゃあ、行こうか。<line-break>君を救ってくれる絶望に向かって。<end>"
EN: "All right. As long as you come with me, that's enough. Shall we go, then? Toward the despair that will save you.<end>"
KO: "알았어. 난 네가 와 주기만 하면 돼. 그럼 가자. 너를 구원해 줄 절망을 향해.<end>"

## 1330037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリ！<end>"
EN: "Shalli!<end>"
KO: "샤리!<end>"

## 1330038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、地の巫女エステルちゃん！<line-break>ごめんね。今は君に構ってられない。<line-break>僕の代わりにこいつがお相手するよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't little Esther, the Priestess of Earth! Sorry, but I don't have time for you right now. This one will keep you company in my place.<end>"
KO: "어라, 땅의 무녀 에스텔! 미안하지만 지금은 너한테 신경 쓸 틈이 없어. 대신 이 녀석이 상대해 줄 거야.<end>"

## 1330039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリ、いったい何を企んでおる！<line-break>イークレムン殿をどうした！？<end>"
EN: "Shalli, what in blazes are you plotting? What have you done with Lady Iiklmn?!<end>"
KO: "샤리, 대체 무슨 속셈이냐! 이크레문 님에게 무슨 짓을 한 거냐!?<end>"

## 1330040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イークレムン殿…ねえ？　<line-break>彼女なら無事だよ。<line-break>　<line-break>ねぇ、お爺ちゃん、<line-break>元気が余ってるんなら、<line-break>こいつの相手でもしててよ。<end>"
EN: "Lady Iiklmn, you say? She's safe. And Grandpa, if you've got that much energy to spare, why don't you keep this one busy?<end>"
KO: "이크레문 님… 말이야? 그녀라면 무사해. 있잖아, 할아버지. 힘이 남아돌면 이 녀석 상대나 해 줘.<end>"

## 1330041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やぁ、　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>じゃないか？　<line-break>　<line-break>僕と君は、<line-break>今、同じ感覚を共有しているよ。<line-break>おいおい、またこいつかよ、ってね。<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't <value:$28>. You and I are feeling exactly the same thing right now: “Oh, come on. This thing again?”<end>"
KO: "야, <value:$28>잖아? 지금 너와 나는 같은 기분을 공유하고 있어. '어이, 또 이 녀석이야?' 하는 기분 말이야.<end>"

## 1330042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、ダメだよん、君。<line-break>順番はちゃんと守ってよ。<line-break>まずは僕がエアと話すの。<line-break>その間、退屈だろうから、<line-break>こいつとでも遊んでてよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh-heh, no cutting in line. I get to talk to Er first. You'd only be bored while you wait, so go play with this one.<end>"
KO: "후후, 안 돼, 자네. 순서는 제대로 지켜야지. 먼저 내가 에아와 이야기할 거야. 그동안 심심할 테니 이 녀석하고라도 놀고 있어.<end>"

## 1330043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無限のソウルを持つ者の未来は<line-break>エアにも見えないらしいね。<line-break>　<line-break>フフ、そうだ、こうしよう！<line-break>君がこいつに勝ったら、<line-break>今日は引き上げようじゃない？<end>"
EN: "It seems even Er can't see the future of the Bearer of the Infinite Soul. Heh-heh, I know! Let's do this: if you beat this one, why don't I call it a day and leave?<end>"
KO: "내가 대답해 줄까? 무한의 소울을 가진 자의 미래는 에아에게도 보이지 않는 모양이네. 후후, 좋아. 네가 이 녀석을 이기면 오늘은 물러가 주지.<end>"

## 1330044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、約束だ。<line-break>今日は引き上げるよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh-heh, you have my word. I'll leave for today.<end>"
KO: "후후, 약속이다. 오늘은 이만 물러가지.<end>"

## 1330045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、ねえ、エア、いいの？<line-break>君自身の望みは、僕と一緒に<line-break>行くことなんじゃないのかい？<end>"
EN: "But Er, are you sure? Isn't going with me what you truly want?<end>"
KO: "그런데 에아, 괜찮아? 네가 정말 원하는 건 나와 함께 가는 것 아니야?<end>"

## 1330046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エア。君には選ぶ権利がある。<line-break>僕と来て、空中都市で<line-break>すべての絶望に終止符を打つか。<line-break>それとも、彼らと共に行き、<line-break>結局、新しい絶望を味わうか。<end>"
EN: "Er, the choice is yours. Come with me and put an end to all despair in the Floating City, or go with them and eventually suffer a new despair.<end>"
KO: "에아. 너에게는 선택할 권리가 있어. 나와 함께 공중도시로 가서 모든 절망에 종지부를 찍을 것인가, 아니면 저들과 함께 가서 결국 새로운 절망을 맛볼 것인가.<end>"

## 1330047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクたちは絶望なんかしない。<line-break>絶望をはねのける希望のために<line-break>戦いぬいてみせる！！<end>"
EN: "We won't give in to despair! We'll fight to the very end for the hope that can drive despair away!!<end>"
KO: "우리는 절망하지 않아. 절망을 떨쳐내는 희망을 위해 끝까지 싸울 거야!!<end>"

## 1330048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わらわは…。<end>"
EN: "I...<end>"
KO: "이 몸은….<end>"

## 1330049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが答え？<line-break>愚かすぎるよ。<end>"
EN: "Is that your answer? How utterly foolish.<end>"
KO: "그게 답이야? 너무 어리석군.<end>"

## 1330050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愚かなもんか！！<line-break>暗い運命なんか信じないで、<line-break>明るい希望に賭けるんだよ！<end>"
EN: "It's not foolish!! We won't believe in some grim destiny. We'll bet on a brighter hope!<end>"
KO: "어리석기는 무슨!! 어두운 운명 따위 믿지 말고 밝은 희망에 거는 거야!<end>"

## 1330051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クスクス、希望だって？<line-break>エア、本気？<line-break>君らしくもない行動だよ？<end>"
EN: "Heh-heh. Hope, you say? Are you serious, Er? This isn't like you at all.<end>"
KO: "후훗, 희망이라고? 에아, 진심이야? 너답지 않은 행동인데?<end>"

## 1330052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うるさいわ。<line-break>わらわはそなたに従わぬ。<line-break>それだけじゃ。去れ！<end>"
EN: "Silence. I shall not obey you. That is all. Begone!<end>"
KO: "시끄럽다. 나는 그대에게 따르지 않는다. 그것뿐이다. 물러가라!<end>"

## 1330053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーあ、僕も嫌われたもんだね。<line-break>まあいいや。<line-break>僕もちょっと忙しくなってきたし。<line-break>また今度、誘いに来るよ。<line-break>じゃあね！　バイバーイ！<end>"
EN: "Oh dear, you really do hate me now. Never mind. I'm getting a little busy myself. I'll come back and ask you again sometime. Bye-bye!<end>"
KO: "아아, 나도 미움받았네. 뭐 됐어. 나도 좀 바빠졌으니까. 다음에 또 권유하러 올게. 그럼 안녕! 바이바이!<end>"

## 1330054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エア、シャリのねらいはなんなの？<line-break>空中都市って？<end>"
EN: "Er, what is Shalli after? And what's this Floating City?<end>"
KO: "에아, 샤리의 목적은 뭐야? 공중도시라니?<end>"

## 1330055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…フフ、そなたは知らぬのか？<line-break>空中都市とは、ラドラスそのもの…。<line-break>竜王をも倒せる、空の魔道要塞よ。<line-break>かつて、魔道王国が滅ぼされしとき、<line-break>魔道王国の人々は、空中都市が<line-break>他者の手に落ちることを恐れた。<end>"
EN: "Heh... You do not know? The Floating City is Ladras itself: a sorcerous fortress in the sky, powerful enough to defeat even the Dragon King. When the Magic Kingdom fell long ago, its people feared that the Floating City would fall into the hands of others.<end>"
KO: "…후후, 그대는 모르는가? 공중도시란 라드라스 그 자체…. 용왕조차 쓰러뜨릴 수 있는 하늘의 마도요새다. 과거 마도왕국이 멸망했을 때, 사람들은 공중도시가 타인의 손에 넘어가는 것을 두려워했다.<end>"

## 1330056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこで、そなたの祖先、地のズゥは<line-break>魔法で王国の地を砂漠に変え、<line-break>そこに空中都市を沈めたのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "And so your ancestor, Zuu of Earth, used magic to turn the kingdom's lands into a desert and sank the Floating City beneath the sands.<end>"
KO: "그래서 네 조상, 대지의 즈는 마법으로 왕국의 땅을 사막으로 바꾸고 거기에 공중도시를 가라앉힌 것이다.<end>"

## 1330057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その伝説は知ってる。　　　　　　　　<line-break>ボクたち地水風火の<line-break>４人の巫女の子孫がそろったとき…<line-break>ラドラスの魔道技術、<line-break>…つまり、空中都市が<line-break>復活するんだよね。<end>"
EN: "I know that legend. When all four descendants of the priestesses of earth, water, wind, and fire come together, Ladras's arcane technology―the Floating City―will be restored, right?<end>"
KO: "그 전설은 알아. 우리 지수풍화 네 무녀의 후손이 모두 모였을 때… 라드라스의 마도기술, 즉 공중도시가 부활하는 거지.<end>"

## 1330058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…シャリのねらいはわからない。<line-break>けれど、奴に空中都市は渡せない。<line-break>あそこには、ボクの仲間がいるもの。<end>"
EN: "I don't know what Shalli is after. But we can't let him have the Floating City. My friends are in there.<end>"
KO: "…샤리의 목적은 모르겠어. 하지만 녀석에게 공중도시를 넘길 순 없어. 거기엔 내 동료들이 있으니까.<end>"

## 1330059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エア、キミなら、水の巫女と火の巫女が<line-break>囚われている所が、わかるよね。<end>"
EN: "Er, you know where the Priestess of Water and the Priestess of Fire are being held, don't you?<end>"
KO: "에아, 너라면 물의 무녀와 불의 무녀가 갇힌 곳을 알고 있지?<end>"

## 1330060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ラドラスの、制御の間じゃ。<line-break>しかし、それを知ってどうする？<end>"
EN: "In Ladras's control chamber. But what will you do with that knowledge?<end>"
KO: "라드라스의 제어실이다. 하지만 그걸 알아서 어쩌려는 게냐?<end>"

## 1330061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの、いつもは動かない<line-break>柱の向こうか……。じゃあ、<line-break>エア、ボクたちをそこへ飛ばせる？<end>"
EN: "Behind that pillar that never moves... Then, Er, can you take us there?<end>"
KO: "저 평소엔 움직이지 않는 기둥 너머인가…. 그럼 에아, 우리를 거기로 보내 줄 수 있어?<end>"

## 1330062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わらわなら可能じゃ。<line-break>しかし、それを聞いてどうする？<end>"
EN: "I can. But why do you ask?<end>"
KO: "나라면 가능하다. 하지만 그걸 들어서 어쩌려는 게냐?<end>"

## 1330063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "決まってるでしょ。<line-break>助けに行く。<line-break>そして、今のラドラスを守る。<end>"
EN: "Isn't it obvious? We're going to rescue them―and protect Ladras as it is now.<end>"
KO: "당연하잖아. 구하러 간다. 그리고 지금의 라드라스를 지킨다.<end>"

## 1330064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愚かな…。<line-break>そなたらは運命に抗えると思うのか？<end>"
EN: "Fools... Do you believe you can defy fate?<end>"
KO: "어리석군…. 너희는 운명에 맞설 수 있다고 생각하나?<end>"

## 1330065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんで、まだ見たこともない未来を<line-break>運命だってあきらめちゃうの？<line-break>　<line-break>どうして、自分の意志と希望で<line-break>前に進むことを愚かだって言うの？<line-break>ねえ、エア！<end>"
EN: "Why resign yourself to a future you haven't even seen and call it fate? Why call it foolish to move forward, driven by your own will and hope? Er!<end>"
KO: "왜 아직 보지도 못한 미래를 운명이라며 포기하는 거야? 왜 자기 의지와 희망으로 앞으로 나아가는 걸 어리석다고 해? 말해 봐, 에아!<end>"

## 1330066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わらわは天地千年を見通す…。<line-break>すべては運命じゃ。<end>"
EN: "My sight spans a thousand years of heaven and earth... All is fate.<end>"
KO: "나는 천년의 세월을 내다본다…. 모든 것은 운명이다.<end>"

## 1330067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "違う。運命なんかあるものか！　　<line-break>キミが見ているのは可能性だ。<line-break>運命じゃない。<line-break>だから、ボクが教えてやる。<line-break>未来は決まってなんかいないって。<line-break>ボクがキミに希望を見せてやる！<end>"
EN: "No. There is no such thing as fate! What you're seeing is a possibility, not fate. So I'll prove it to you: the future isn't already decided. I'll show you hope!<end>"
KO: "아니야. 운명 같은 게 어디 있어! 네가 보는 건 가능성이야. 운명이 아니라고. 그러니까 내가 알려 줄게. 미래는 정해져 있지 않다는 걸. 내가 네게 희망을 보여 주겠어!<end>"

## 1330068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ……そなたとでは話にならんな。<line-break><value:$28>よ、どうする？<line-break>そなたも行くなどと申すのか？<end>"
EN: "Heh... There is no reasoning with you. What will you do, <value:$28>? Do you mean to go as well?<end>"
KO: "후후…… 그대와는 이야기가 안 되는군.<value:$28>여, 어떻게 할 텐가? 그대도 가겠다는 건가?<end>"

## 1330069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行く<end>"
EN: "I'll go.<end>"
KO: "간다<end>"

## 1330070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行かない<end>"
EN: "I won't go.<end>"
KO: "안 간다<end>"

## 1330071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…愚かな。<line-break>どこまでも、運命を受け入れぬか。<line-break>　<line-break>………………。<line-break>よかろう、ならば送ってやる。<line-break>運命の待つ、制御の間へとな。<end>"
EN: "Foolish... So you will never accept fate. ... Very well, then. I shall send you to the control chamber, where fate awaits.<end>"
KO: "…어리석군. 끝까지 운명을 받아들이지 않는가. ………………. 좋다. 그렇다면 보내 주마. 운명이 기다리는 제어실로.<end>"

## 1330072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見せてもらうぞ。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>そなたらの言う、希望とやらを。<line-break>　<line-break>シャリを追い返した時点で<line-break>不本意ながら、そなたらに賭けたことに<line-break>なったのじゃからな。<end>"
EN: "Then let me see this \"hope\" of yours. By driving Shalli away, I have already cast my lot in with you, however unwillingly.<end>"
KO: "보여 다오. 너희가 말하는 그 희망이라는 것을. 샤리를 쫓아낸 시점에서 내키지는 않았으나 너희에게 걸어 버린 셈이니 말이다.<end>"

## 1330073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！<line-break>…そんな！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! No...!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! …그럴 수가!<end>"

## 1330074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何も聞きたくないよ、<line-break><value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "I don't want to hear it, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "아무것도 듣고 싶지 않아, <value:$28>….<end>"

## 1330075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キミがパーティーのリーダーなんだ。<line-break>ボクも、キミの決定には…従う。<line-break>　<line-break>…じゃ。<end>"
EN: "You're the leader of the party. I'll... abide by your decision. Well... then.<end>"
KO: "네가 파티의 리더야. 나도 네 결정에는… 따를게. …그럼.<end>"

## 1330076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エア！！<line-break>そんな………。<end>"
EN: "Er!! No...<end>"
KO: "에아!! 그럴 수가……….<end>"

## 1330077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱり、そうでしょ？　　　　　　<line-break>もう、変な意地はるのはよそうよ。<line-break>　<line-break>それじゃ、彼女もお望みのことだし、<line-break>僕たちは行くことにするよ。<line-break>じゃあね。バイバーイ！<end>"
EN: "I knew it. Enough of this silly stubbornness. Well, since this is what she wants too, we'll be on our way. Bye-bye!<end>"
KO: "역시 그렇지? 이제 이상한 고집은 그만 부리자. 그럼 그녀도 원하고 있으니 우리는 가 볼게. 그럼 안녕. 바이바이!<end>"

## 1330078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なーんちゃって！<line-break>うっそだよー！！　アハハ！！<end>"
EN: "Just kidding! It was a lie!! Ah-ha-ha!<end>"
KO: "…라고 할 줄 알았지! 거짓말이야!! 아하하!!<end>"

## 1330079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "約束だ。<line-break>じゃ、彼女はもらっていくよん。<end>"
EN: "You have my word. Well then, I'll be taking her with me.<end>"
KO: "약속이다. 그럼, 그 여자는 데려갈게.<end>"

## 1330080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break>わらわは……………。<line-break>　<line-break>去れ。<line-break>今はそなたらと<line-break>話しとうない。<end>"
EN: "...I... Leave. I do not wish to speak with any of you now.<end>"
KO: "………………. 짐은……………. 물러가라. 지금은 그대들과 이야기하고 싶지 않다.<end>"

## 1330081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "水の巫女イークレムンと　　<line-break>火の巫女フレアは<line-break>シャリの手に落ちた。<line-break>すべては時間の問題じゃ…。<line-break>運命は変えられぬ。<end>"
EN: "The Priestess of Water Iiklmn and the Priestess of Fire Flare have fallen into Shalli's hands. The rest is only a matter of time... Fate cannot be changed.<end>"
KO: "물의 무녀 이크레문과 불의 무녀 플레어는 샤리의 손에 넘어갔다. 모든 것은 시간문제다…. 운명은 바꿀 수 없다.<end>"

## 1330082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　ロストールとディンガル　　　　　<line-break>　　　ふたつの大国が大陸を揺り動かす中、　　<line-break>　　　　　　　いにしえの力もまた　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　その大いなる眠りから放たれた。　　　<end>"
EN: "As the two great powers of Rostorl and Dyneskal shook the continent, an ancient power was also released from its long slumber.<end>"
KO: "로스톨과 딘갈, 두 대국이 대륙을 뒤흔드는 가운데 고대의 힘도 긴 잠에서 풀려났다.<end>"

## 1330083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　砂漠にある都市ラドラスが　　　　　<line-break>　　　　その魔道国家時代の機能を発動。　　　<line-break>　　　　攻撃力を持った空中都市として　　　　<line-break>　　　　　空へと飛び立ったのである。　　　　<end>"
EN: "The desert city of Ladras activated systems dating back to its days as a sorcerous state and rose into the sky as a Floating City with the power to attack.<end>"
KO: "사막의 도시 라드라스가 마도국가 시대의 기능을 발동했다. 공격력을 지닌 공중도시로 하늘에 떠오른 것이다.<end>"

## 1330084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この驚異的な事件は、大陸中に出没する　　　　<line-break>魔道士シャリの手によるものであり、　　　　　<line-break>これが彼の数々の行動の　　<line-break>目的のひとつでもあった。　<end>"
EN: "This astonishing event was the work of the mage Shalli, who had been appearing throughout the continent. It was also one of the objectives behind his many actions.<end>"
KO: "이 놀라운 사건은 대륙 각지에 출몰하는 마도사 샤리의 손에 의한 것으로, 그의 여러 행동 목적 중 하나이기도 했다.<end>"

## 1330085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　しかし、眠りから目覚めた竜王が介入。　<line-break>　　　　　 太古の超兵器、空中都市は、　 　　<line-break>　　　その秘めた破壊の力を振るうことなく　　<line-break>　　　　　　再び地へと落ちていった。　　　　<end>"
EN: "But the Dragon King, awakened from slumber, intervened. The Floating City, an ancient superweapon, fell back to earth without ever unleashing the destructive power hidden within it.<end>"
KO: "하지만 잠에서 깨어난 용왕이 개입했다. 태고의 초병기 공중도시는 숨겨진 파괴력을 휘두르지 못한 채 다시 대지로 추락했다.<end>"

## 1330086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　この事件は、ラドラスの民をのぞく　　<line-break>　　　多くの人々にとっては、どこか遠くから　<line-break>　　　届く轟音としてしか認識されなかった。　<line-break>　大いなる力は、歴史の闇へと葬られたのである。<end>"
EN: "For many people other than the inhabitants of Ladras, the event registered as nothing more than a distant roar. A great power was buried in the darkness of history.<end>"
KO: "이 사건은 라드라스 주민을 제외한 많은 사람들에게 어딘가 먼 곳에서 들려온 굉음 정도로만 인식되었다. 거대한 힘은 역사의 어둠 속에 묻혔다.<end>"

## 1330087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　そして、いにしえの力を今に受け継いだ　<line-break>　　　巫女と呼ばれる４人の少女たちも、　　<line-break>　　　　　空中都市と運命を共にし、　　　　<line-break>　　　そのはかない命を空に散らした…。　　<end>"
EN: "And the four girls known as priestesses, who had inherited the ancient power and carried it into the present age, shared the Floating City's fate, their fleeting lives scattered across the sky…<end>"
KO: "그리고 고대의 힘을 지금까지 이어받은, 무녀라 불리는 네 소녀도 공중도시와 운명을 함께해 그 덧없는 목숨을 하늘에 흩뿌렸다….<end>"

## 1330088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　どうした。無限のソウルを持つ者よ。　　<line-break>　　　　道に迷い、道標を乞いに来たか。<end>"
EN: "What is it, Bearer of the Infinite Soul? Have you lost your way and come seeking guidance?<end>"
KO: "왜 그러지. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여. 길을 잃고 길잡이를 구하러 왔나.<end>"

## 1330089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　しかし、エアはもはやおらぬ。　　　　<line-break>　　　汝が、自らの力で進むべき道を探し、<line-break>　　　　　　　進んでゆくのだ。<line-break>　　　希望を、世にもたらすものとしてな。<end>"
EN: "But Er is no longer here. You must use your own strength to find the path you should take, then walk it―as one who brings hope to the world.<end>"
KO: "하지만 에아는 이제 없다. 네 힘으로 나아갈 길을 찾아 전진하라. 세상에 희망을 가져오는 자로서.<end>"

## 1330090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "制御の間は　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>この通路を奥へと進んでいった<line-break>その先にあるはずだ。<line-break>たぶん、他の巫女たちも<line-break>そこにいるはずだよ。<line-break>さあ、急ごう。<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "The control room should be farther down this passage. The other priestesses are probably there too. Come on, <value:$28>. Let's hurry.<end>"
KO: "제어실은 이 통로를 안쪽으로 계속 가면 그 끝에 있을 거야. 아마 다른 무녀들도 거기 있을 거고. 자, 서두르자. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1330091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この柱はきっと、ラドラスの　　<line-break>中の方につながってる奴だよ。<line-break>　<line-break>今は、そっちへ行く時間はない。<line-break>みんなのことが気になるけど、<line-break>ボクたちは急がなきゃ。<end>"
EN: "This pillar must lead farther inside Ladras. We don't have time to go that way now. I'm worried about everyone, but we have to hurry.<end>"
KO: "이 기둥은 분명 라드라스 안쪽으로 이어지는 거야. 지금은 그쪽으로 갈 시간이 없어. 모두가 걱정되지만 우리는 서둘러야 해.<end>"

## 1330092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーもう、エアのしわざだね？　　<line-break>せっかくここには来れないように<line-break>柱をいじっておいたのに。<line-break>まあ、でも、なんとなく<line-break>君はここまで来るんじゃないかって<line-break>思ってたよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, come on. This is Er's doing, isn't it? I went to all that trouble tampering with the pillars to keep you from getting here. Still, I had a feeling you'd make it this far.<end>"
KO: "아, 정말. 에아 짓이지? 모처럼 여기 못 오게 기둥을 손봐 뒀는데. 뭐, 그래도 왠지 너라면 여기까지 올 것 같긴 했어.<end>"

## 1330093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君に紹介しようかな。　　　　　<line-break>彼はレムゲドン。大昔に<line-break>この空中都市を作った人さ。<line-break>彼も僕と同じで、この空中都市を<line-break>浮上させたいんだ。だから、<line-break>君とちょっと話がしたいってさ。<end>"
EN: "Let me introduce you. His name is Remgedon. He built this Floating City ages ago. Like me, he wants to make the city rise. So he says he'd like to talk to you.<end>"
KO: "소개해 줄까? 그는 렘게돈. 아주 오래전에 이 공중도시를 만든 사람이야. 그도 나처럼 이 공중도시를 띄우고 싶어 하지. 그래서 너와 조금 얘기하고 싶대.<end>"

## 1330094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、あとは当事者同士で。<line-break>邪魔者は消えるとしますか。<end>"
EN: "Well, I'll leave the two of you to it. Time for me to make myself scarce.<end>"
KO: "그럼 나머지는 당사자끼리. 방해꾼은 사라져 줄까.<end>"

## 1330095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは…？<end>"
EN: "Who are you...?<end>"
KO: "당신은…?<end>"

## 1330096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君を助けに来たんだよ。<line-break>君、イークレムン、だよね？<end>"
EN: "I came to rescue you. You're Iiklmn, right?<end>"
KO: "널 구하러 왔어. 너, 이크레문 맞지?<end>"

## 1330097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は、はい…。<line-break>あなたたちは…？<end>"
EN: "Y-yes... Who are you?<end>"
KO: "네, 네…. 당신들은…?<end>"

## 1330098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクはエステル。<line-break>君とおんなじ巫女、地の巫女だよ。<line-break>　<line-break>で、こっちが<value:$28>。<line-break>ま、冒険者、かな。<end>"
EN: "I'm Esther. I'm a priestess, just like you. The Priestess of Earth. And this is <value:$28>. Well, an adventurer, I guess.<end>"
KO: "나는 에스텔. 너와 같은 무녀, 땅의 무녀야. 그리고 이쪽이 <value:$28>. 뭐, 모험자랄까.<end>"

## 1330099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그렇군요. 에스텔 씨, <value:$28>님, 정말 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1330100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1330101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1330102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご無事そうで何よりです。<end>"

## 1330103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "네….<end>"

## 1330104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのー…。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>なんだか、ボクたち<line-break>入っていきづらいんですけど…。<end>"
EN: "Um... It's kind of hard for us to step in here...<end>"
KO: "저기…. 왠지 우리, 들어가기가 좀 힘든데….<end>"

## 1330105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、す、すいません。<line-break>あなたは…私と同じ、巫女ですね。<end>"
EN: "Oh! S-sorry. You're a priestess, like me, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "아, 죄송합니다. 당신은… 저와 같은 무녀군요.<end>"

## 1330106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん。地の巫女。<line-break>エステルっていうんだ。よろしく。<end>"
EN: "Yeah. The Priestess of Earth. My name's Esther. Nice to meet you.<end>"
KO: "응. 땅의 무녀. 에스텔이라고 해. 잘 부탁해.<end>"

## 1330107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エステルさん、<line-break>それに、<value:$28>さん、<line-break>本当に、ありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "Esther, and <value:$28>... thank you both so very much.<end>"
KO: "에스텔 씨, 그리고 <value:$28>님, 정말 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1330108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん……！<line-break>きっと、きっと来てくれると<line-break>思っていました…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...! I was sure... I was sure you'd come.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨……! 분명, 분명 와 줄 거라고 생각했어요….<end>"

## 1330109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん……？<line-break>助けに来て…くださったのですか？<line-break>　<line-break>ありがとう…ございます……。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...? Did you come to rescue me? Thank you... so much.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님……? 구하러… 와 주신 건가요? 감사…합니다…….<end>"

## 1330110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですが、どうしましょう…。<line-break>ここをこのままにしておくと、<line-break>いずれまた大変なことに……。<end>"
EN: "But what should we do now...? If we leave this place as it is, sooner or later something terrible will happen again...<end>"
KO: "하지만 어떻게 하죠…. 여길 이대로 두면 언젠가 다시 큰일이…….<end>"

## 1330111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここを、動かなくしよう。<line-break>二度と使えないように。<end>"
EN: "Let's shut this place down, so it can never be used again.<end>"
KO: "여기를 움직이지 못하게 하자. 다시는 쓸 수 없게.<end>"

## 1330112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは！！<line-break>…これを壊すことは…<line-break>動かなくするということは…。<line-break>私たち巫女が今まで<line-break>存在しつづけてきた意味が、<line-break>理由がなくなってしまいます。<end>"
EN: "But...! If we destroy this... if we shut it down... then there'll be no meaning, no reason for us priestesses to have gone on existing all this time.<end>"
KO: "그건!! …이걸 부순다는 건… 움직이지 않게 한다는 건…. 우리 무녀가 지금까지 존재해 온 의미와 이유가 없어져 버립니다.<end>"

## 1330113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、この空中都市がありつづけると<line-break>いつかきっと、悲しみが生まれます。<line-break>大切な命が、失われるでしょう。<line-break>それよりは、今、この空中都市を<line-break>永久に封じた方が、世界にとって<line-break>いいのではないでしょうか。<end>"
EN: "But if the Floating City remains, sooner or later it will bring sorrow. Precious lives will be lost. Wouldn't it be better for the world if we sealed it away forever, here and now?<end>"
KO: "하지만 이 공중도시가 계속 존재하면 언젠가 분명 슬픔이 생길 겁니다. 소중한 생명이 잃어버리겠죠. 그보다는 지금 이 공중도시를 영원히 봉인하는 편이 세계를 위해 더 낫지 않을까요.<end>"

## 1330114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクもイークレムンと同じ気持ちだよ。<line-break>悲しみや絶望を生むために<line-break>ボクたちはいるんじゃない。<line-break>８００年、ボクたち巫女の存在が<line-break>これのためにあったというのなら、<line-break>今日で、それを終わりにしよう。<end>"
EN: "I feel the same way as Iiklmn. We don't exist to bring grief and despair. If this is what we priestesses have existed for these past eight hundred years, then let's bring it to an end today.<end>"
KO: "나도 이크렘과 같은 마음이야. 슬픔과 절망을 만들기 위해 우리가 존재하는 건 아니야. 800년 동안 우리 무녀의 존재가 이것을 위해 있었다면, 오늘로 그것을 끝내자.<end>"

## 1330115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、イークレムンとフレアは　　<line-break>自分たちの制御球の所に行って。<line-break>　<line-break>で、ボクが、せーの！　って言ったら<line-break>みんなで一緒に、壊れろって祈って。<line-break>いい？　じゃ、始めるよ。<end>"
EN: "All right. Iiklmn, Flare, go to your control orbs. When I shout, “Now!” we'll all pray together for it to break. Ready? Then let's begin.<end>"
KO: "그럼 이클렘과 플레어는 각자 제어구 쪽으로 가. 그리고 내가 하나, 둘! 하면 다 같이 부서져라 하고 빌어. 알겠지? 그럼 시작할게.<end>"

## 1330116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "せーの！！<end>"
EN: "Now!!<end>"
KO: "하나, 둘!!<end>"

## 1330117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…あれ？<line-break>この揺れって…<line-break>うまくいったってことなのかな？　<line-break>なんだか、予想外だけど…、<line-break>うわああああ、<value:$28>、<line-break>どうしよう！？<end>"
EN: "H-huh? This shaking... does it mean it worked? This isn't quite what I expected... Aaaaaah! <value:$28>, what do we do?!<end>"
KO: "어… 어라? 이 흔들림은… 잘됐다는 뜻인가? 뭔가 예상 밖인데…, 우와아아, <value:$28>, 어떡하지!?<end>"

## 1330118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここ…<line-break>壊れてしまうのでしょうか…？<line-break>　<line-break>そんな…<line-break>せっかく…。<end>"
EN: "Is this place... going to collapse? No... after all that...<end>"
KO: "여기… 부서져 버리는 걸까요…? 그런… 모처럼….<end>"

## 1330119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "役目が終われば<line-break>ここにいる理由もない。<line-break>　<line-break>そして<line-break>存在の意味もない。<line-break>だから、これでいい…。<end>"
EN: "Once the duty is over, there will be no reason to remain here. And no meaning in existing. So this is fine...<end>"
KO: "역할이 끝나면 여기 있을 이유도 없다. 그리고 존재의 의미도 없다. 그러니 이걸로 됐어….<end>"

## 1330120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くだらぬことを申すな。<line-break>そなたらは希望のために<line-break>戦うのであろう？<end>"
EN: "Do not speak such nonsense. You fight for hope, do you not?<end>"
KO: "시답잖은 소리 마라. 너희는 희망을 위해 싸우는 것 아니냐?<end>"

## 1330121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わらわがそなたらを<line-break>飛ばしてやる。<line-break>心を、空にするのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "I shall send you away from here. Empty your minds.<end>"
KO: "내가 너희를 날려 보내 주마. 마음을 비우거라.<end>"

## 1330122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは…？<end>"
EN: "Who are you...?<end>"
KO: "당신은…?<end>"

## 1330123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君を助けに来たんだよ。<line-break>君、フレア、だよね？<end>"
EN: "I came to help you. You're Flare, right?<end>"
KO: "널 구하러 왔어. 너, 플레어지?<end>"

## 1330124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい。<end>"
EN: "Yes.<end>"
KO: "네.<end>"

## 1330125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクはエステル。<line-break>君とおんなじ巫女、地の巫女だよ。<line-break>　<line-break>で、こっちが<value:$28>。<line-break>ま、冒険者、かな。<end>"
EN: "I'm Esther. I'm a priestess just like you―the Priestess of Earth. And this is <value:$28>. Well, an adventurer, I guess.<end>"
KO: "나는 에스텔. 너와 같은 무녀, 땅의 무녀야. 그리고 이쪽이 <value:$28>. 뭐, 모험자랄까.<end>"

## 1330126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<end>"
EN: "I see.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까.<end>"

## 1330127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>……！<line-break>こうなるとは、思っていました。<line-break>きっと、ここへ来るだろうと…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...! I thought it might come to this. I knew you would come here...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>……! 이렇게 될 줄 알았습니다. 분명 여기로 올 거라고….<end>"

## 1330128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>あなたも、ここへと<line-break>みちびかれたのですか…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Were you guided here as well...?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 당신도 이곳으로 이끌린 건가요….<end>"

## 1330129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、この絶望の都市で<line-break>何をしようというのですか？<end>"
EN: "But what do you intend to do here, in this city of despair?<end>"
KO: "하지만 이 절망의 도시에서 무엇을 하려는 겁니까?<end>"

## 1330130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、本当に<line-break>空中都市の復活を止めるとは…。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>無限のソウルの行く先は<line-break>このわらわにも見えんということか…。<end>"
EN: "And yet, you truly did prevent the Floating City's resurrection... So even I cannot see where the Infinite Soul is headed...<end>"
KO: "하지만 정말 공중도시의 부활을 막아내다니…. 무한의 소울이 향할 곳은 나조차 볼 수 없다는 건가….<end>"

## 1330131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "違うよ、エア。　　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>が特別だから<line-break>こうなったんじゃないよ。<line-break>イークレムンやフレアと一緒に<line-break>みんなで希望を持って頑張った。<line-break>だから、こうなったんだよ。<end>"
EN: "No, Er. This didn't happen just because <value:$28> is special. All of us held on to hope and gave it everything we had, alongside Iiklmn and Flare. That's why things turned out this way.<end>"
KO: "아니야, 에아. <value:$28>가 특별해서 이렇게 된 게 아니야. 이크렘과 플레어와 함께 모두가 희망을 품고 노력했어. 그래서 이렇게 된 거야.<end>"

## 1330132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そしてエア、キミが来てくれたから<line-break>今、ボクたちはこうしていられる。<line-break>ありがとう。<end>"
EN: "And we're here now because you came, Er. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "그리고 에아, 네가 와 줬기에 지금 우리는 이렇게 있을 수 있어. 고마워.<end>"

## 1330133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なにを、気楽なことを…。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>他の巫女らは、本来いるべき地に<line-break>送り飛ばしておいた。<line-break>そなたらも、旅に戻るがよい。<end>"
EN: "How blithely you say that... I have sent the other priestesses back to where they belong. You should return to your journey as well.<end>"
KO: "뭘 그렇게 태평하게…. 다른 무녀들은 원래 있어야 할 곳으로 보내 두었다. 너희도 여행으로 돌아가거라.<end>"

## 1330134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん、そうするよ。<line-break>ありがとう、エア。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, we will. Thank you, Er.<end>"
KO: "응, 그렇게 할게. 고마워, 에아.<end>"

## 1330135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "希望、か……。<end>"
EN: "Hope, huh...<end>"
KO: "희망, 인가…….<end>"

## 1330136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>より多くの道標を望むか。<line-break>ならば、聞かせてやろう。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. Do you wish for more signs to guide you? Then I shall tell you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 더 많은 이정표를 원하나. 그렇다면 들려주지.<end>"

## 1330137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か。<line-break>どうした？　道に迷い<line-break>わらわが見る可能性を頼りに来たか。<line-break>ならば、聞かせよう。<line-break>わらわに見える、そなたの未来、<line-break>そこにある希望の断片を。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. What is it? Have you lost your way and come to rely on the possibilities I can see? Then listen, and I shall tell you of the future I see for you, and the fragments of hope that lie within it.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가. 무슨 일이지? 길을 잃고 내가 보는 가능성에 기대러 왔느냐. 그렇다면 들려주마. 내게 보이는 그대의 미래, 그 안에 있는 희망의 조각을.<end>"

## 1330138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死神の鎌は<line-break>あまねく大陸をなぎ払おうとしておる。<line-break>　<line-break>その鎌の切っ先にあるは<line-break>革新か、あるいは悲しみか。<line-break>汝の目で、それを見定めるがよい。<end>"
EN: "Death's scythe is poised to sweep across the whole continent. Does innovation lie at its point, or sorrow? See for yourself, and judge.<end>"
KO: "사신의 낫은 대륙 전역을 베어 넘기려 한다. 그 낫끝에 있는 것이 혁신인지 슬픔인지 네 눈으로 확인하라.<end>"

## 1330139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死神の鎌はその輝きを増し、<line-break>最後の王国をなぎ払おうとしておる。<line-break>相対し得るは剣狼の牙のみ。<line-break>その影で、獅子は闇と戦っておる。<line-break>汝、いずれに与すか。<line-break>道は分かれ、未来もまた分かたれる。<end>"
EN: "Death's scythe shines ever brighter, poised to sweep away the last kingdom. Only the fangs of the Sword Wolf can stand against it. In its shadow, the lion battles the darkness. Which will you side with? The paths divide, and so too does the future.<end>"
KO: "사신의 낫은 더욱 빛나며 마지막 왕국을 쓸어버리려 하고 있다. 맞설 수 있는 것은 검랑의 이빨뿐. 그 그림자에서 사자는 어둠과 싸우고 있지. 그대는 어느 쪽에 설 것인가. 길은 갈리고, 미래 또한 갈라진다.<end>"

## 1330140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すべては、混沌を抱え　　　　　　　　　<line-break>すべては、終焉へと向かっておる。<line-break>混沌ゆえに、その終焉の先も見えぬ。<line-break>無限の魂の輝きが<line-break>新たなる世界を照らすであろう。<line-break>いまだ、定まらぬ未来という世界を…。<end>"
EN: "All things harbor chaos. All things move toward their end. And because of that chaos, even what lies beyond the end cannot be seen. The radiance of the Infinite Soul shall illuminate a new world: the future, not yet determined…<end>"
KO: "모든 것은 혼돈을 품고, 모든 것은 종언을 향하고 있다. 혼돈 때문에 그 종언 너머조차 보이지 않는다. 무한한 영혼의 빛이 새로운 세계를 비추겠지. 아직 정해지지 않은 미래라는 세계를….<end>"

## 1330141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わらわが今聞かせたのは　　<line-break>今の世界の断片にすぎぬ。<line-break>　<line-break>そこに希望をもたらすも<line-break>それに絶望を感じるも<line-break>すべては、そなた次第じゃ。<end>"
EN: "What I have told you is but a fragment of the world as it now stands. Whether you bring hope to it or find despair there, all rests with you.<end>"
KO: "내가 지금 들려준 것은 지금 세계의 단편에 지나지 않는다. 거기에 희망을 가져오는 것도, 절망을 느끼는 것도 모두 그대에게 달렸다.<end>"

## 1330142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このような未来があったとはな…。<line-break>そなたの持つ可能性は、わらわにさえ<line-break>見通すことができんようだな……。<end>"
EN: "To think a future such as this was possible... It seems even I cannot see the full extent of your potential...<end>"
KO: "이런 미래가 있었다니…. 그대가 가진 가능성은 나조차도 내다볼 수 없는 모양이구나…….<end>"

## 1330152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この大都会エンシャントに、<line-break>人っ子ひとりおらんのが<line-break>ご不審のようじゃな。<line-break>ソウルリープにかけられたのじゃよ。<line-break>老いも若きも男も女も…<line-break>この街の住民、全員がな。<end>"
EN: "You wonder why not a single soul remains in the great city of Ancient. They were subjected to Soul Leap. Young and old, men and women... every last person in the city.<end>"
KO: "이 대도시 엔샨트에 사람 하나 없는 게 이상한 모양이구나. 소울 리프에 걸린 거다. 노인도 젊은이도 남자도 여자도… 이 도시 주민 전부가 말이지.<end>"

## 1330153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしはゾフォル。<line-break>かつて魔王バロルに仕え、<line-break>妖術宰相と呼ばれた魔道士じゃ。<end>"
EN: "I am Zofor, a sorcerer who once served Balor, Demon King, and was known as the Sorcerous Chancellor.<end>"
KO: "나는 조포르. 한때 마왕 발로르를 섬기며 요술재상이라 불렸던 마도사다.<end>"

## 1330154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "円卓騎士をはじめとする魔人、<line-break>いきのよい怪物どもや、凶暴な邪竜、<line-break>そして、偉大なる破壊神。<line-break>それらを召喚するためには、<line-break>それこそ人知を超える莫大な<line-break>魔道エネルギーが必要となるのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Demons such as the Knights of the Round Table, the liveliest of monsters, ferocious Evil Dragons, and the great God of Destruction―to summon them requires a truly immense amount of magical energy, beyond human understanding.<end>"
KO: "원탁기사를 비롯한 마인, 기세 좋은 괴물들과 흉포한 사룡, 그리고 위대한 파괴신. 그것들을 소환하려면 그야말로 인간의 지혜를 넘어서는 막대한 마도 에너지가 필요하단 말이지.<end>"

## 1330155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クック…。<line-break>大望を成し遂げるためには、<line-break>どうすればよいと思う…？<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... What do you suppose must be done to fulfill such a grand ambition?<end>"
KO: "크크…. 큰 뜻을 이루려면 어떻게 해야 한다고 생각하나…?<end>"

## 1330156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人知を超える、とは言ったがの、<line-break>これを実現させることができるのは、<line-break>実は、人のソウルの力なのじゃ。<line-break>人間のソウルを吸い取り、<line-break>その魔道力を抽出する。<line-break>その法こそ、ソウルリープ。<end>"
EN: "I called it beyond human comprehension, but in truth, human Souls themselves can provide the necessary power. Drain the Souls from human beings and extract their magical energy. That is the art of Soul Leap.<end>"
KO: "인지를 초월한다고는 했지만, 사실 이것을 실현할 수 있는 건 인간의 소울의 힘이다. 인간의 소울을 빨아들여 그 마도력을 추출한다. 그 방법이 바로 소울 리프다.<end>"

## 1330157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この町に住まっておった人間、<line-break>そのすべてのソウルは、破壊神ウルグ様の<line-break>崇高なる祭壇にささげられた。<line-break>喜べ、破壊神復活の時は近いぞ。<line-break>フォフォフォ…。<end>"
EN: "Every Soul belonging to those who once lived in this city has been offered upon the exalted altar of Lord Ulugh, the God of Destruction. Rejoice, for the hour of the God's resurrection draws near. Hoh hoh hoh...<end>"
KO: "이 마을에 살던 인간들의 모든 소울은 파괴신 울그 님의 숭고한 제단에 바쳐졌다. 기뻐하라, 파괴신 부활의 때가 가깝다. 포포포….<end>"

## 1330158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうじゃのう。さしものわしにも、<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主の<line-break>未来は見えぬ。じゃが…<end>"
EN: "Indeed. Even I cannot see the future of the bearer of the Infinite Soul. And yet...<end>"
KO: "그렇구나. 아무리 나라도 무한의 소울을 지닌 자의 미래는 보이지 않는다. 하지만….<end>"

## 1330159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それだけのことじゃ。<line-break>たとえ無限のソウルを持つとも、<line-break>運命の波には逆らえぬ。<end>"
EN: "That is all. Even the bearer of the Infinite Soul cannot resist the tides of fate.<end>"
KO: "그뿐이다. 무한의 소울을 지녔다 해도 운명의 파도에는 거스를 수 없다.<end>"

## 1330160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう、無謀だとわかりきっていて、<line-break>なお挑もうというのかの？<line-break> <line-break>面白い。来い。追ってこい。<line-break>わしは、エンシャント城内、<line-break>謁見の間に待っておるぞ。<end>"
EN: "Oh? You know full well how reckless this is, yet you would still challenge me? How amusing. Come, then. Follow me. I shall await you in the audience chamber within Ancient Castle Interior.<end>"
KO: "호오, 무모하다는 걸 뻔히 알면서도 도전하겠다는 건가? 재미있군. 와라. 쫓아와라. 나는 엔샨트 성 안, 알현실에서 기다리겠다.<end>"

## 1330161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしか、お主か…<line-break>そこで知れるじゃろう。<line-break> <line-break>フォッフォッフォ…！<end>"
EN: "Whether it shall be you or me... we will learn the answer there. Hoh hoh hoh!<end>"
KO: "나인가, 그대인가… 거기서 알게 되겠지. 포호호호…!<end>"

## 1330162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人のいる気配が…<line-break>ソウルの息づきが感じられない。<line-break>まさか、ソウルリープ…！？<end>"
EN: "There's no sign of anyone... I can't feel the pulse of a single Soul. Could this be Soul Leap?!<end>"
KO: "사람이 있는 기척이… 소울의 숨결이 느껴지지 않아. 설마, 소울 리프…!?<end>"

## 1330163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "円卓騎士や邪竜、破壊神…<line-break>それらを復活させるには、<line-break>莫大な魔道エネルギーが要る。<end>"
EN: "The Knights of the Round Table, the Evil Dragons, the God of Destruction... Resurrecting them would require an enormous amount of magical energy.<end>"
KO: "원탁기사나 사룡, 파괴신…. 그것들을 부활시키려면 막대한 마도 에너지가 필요해.<end>"

## 1330164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのために、<line-break>人からソウルを吸い集め、<line-break>魔道力を抽出する技が編み出された。<line-break>邪法と呼べるその技こそが、<line-break>ソウルリープ。<line-break>つまり…<end>"
EN: "So an art was devised to drain Souls from human beings and extract their magical energy. That wicked art is Soul Leap. Which means...<end>"
KO: "그 때문에 사람에게서 소울을 빨아 모아 마도력을 추출하는 기술이 만들어졌다. 사법이라 부를 만한 그 기술이 바로 소울 리프. 즉….<end>"

## 1330165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つまり、この帝都に暮らしておった<line-break>人間のソウルは、すべてがすべて、<line-break> <line-break>破壊神ウルグ様復活の祭壇に<line-break>ささげられたというわけじゃよ。<end>"
EN: "It means every Soul belonging to the people who lived in this imperial capital was offered upon the altar for the resurrection of Lord Ulugh, the God of Destruction.<end>"
KO: "즉, 이 제도에 살고 있던 인간의 소울은 모조리 파괴신 울그 님 부활의 제단에 바쳐졌다는 게야.<end>"

## 1330166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ムダよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>これは幻像にすぎない…。<line-break>実体はおそらく、城の中よ。<end>"
EN: "It's no use, <value:$28>. This is only an apparition... The real body is probably somewhere inside the castle.<end>"
KO: "소용없어, <value:$28>. 이건 환상에 불과해…. 본체는 아마 성 안에 있을 거야.<end>"

## 1330167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくぞ戻ってきたな、ザギヴよ。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>おぬしが皇帝となり、<line-break>破壊神が復活するは、<line-break>やはり運命だったのじゃよ。<end>"
EN: "Welcome back, Zagiv. It was fate after all that you would become emperor and the God of Destruction would rise again.<end>"
KO: "잘 돌아왔구나, 자기브여. 네가 황제가 되고 파괴신이 부활하는 건 역시 운명이었던 게야.<end>"

## 1330168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はその運命と闘うために来た！<line-break>私は、皇帝にはならないわ！<end>"
EN: "I came here to fight that fate! I will not become emperor!<end>"
KO: "나는 그 운명과 싸우기 위해 왔어! 난 황제가 되지 않을 거야!<end>"

## 1330169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほどな。<line-break>お主の剣となり盾となるのが、<line-break><value:$28>というわけだ。<line-break>確かに、さしものわしにも、<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主の未来は見えぬ。<line-break>じゃがな、ザギヴよ…。<end>"
EN: "Ah, so <value:$28> is to be your sword and shield. It is true that even I cannot see the future of the bearer of the Infinite Soul. But, Zagiv...<end>"
KO: "그렇군. 네 검이자 방패가 되는 게 <value:$28>이라는 거구나. 확실히 나라도 무한의 소울을 지닌 자의 미래는 볼 수 없다. 하지만 자기브여….<end>"

## 1330170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おぬしが見ておる希望という名の霧、<line-break>その向こうにあるものが、果たして、<line-break>本当におぬしの望んでおるものかの？<end>"
EN: "Beyond that mist you call hope, will you truly find what you desire?<end>"
KO: "그대가 보고 있는 희망이라는 이름의 안개, 그 너머에 있는 것이 과연 정말 그대가 원하는 것일까?<end>"

## 1330171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "望んでいるか、なんて！<line-break>私はそれを…その霧に腕を差し入れ、<line-break>未来をつかみ取りに、ここへ来たのよ！<end>"
EN: "Whether that's what I want?! I came here to reach into that mist and seize the future!<end>"
KO: "원하냐고!? 나는 그걸… 그 안개 속에 팔을 뻗어 미래를 움켜쥐려고 여기 온 거야!<end>"

## 1330172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フォフォ…言うではないか。<line-break>ならば、城の謁見の間で、<line-break>楽しみに待たせてもらおうかの。<end>"
EN: "Hoh hoh... Bold words. Then I shall look forward to receiving you in the castle's audience chamber.<end>"
KO: "호호… 제법 말하는구나. 그렇다면 성의 알현실에서 기대하며 기다리도록 하지.<end>"

## 1330173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "運命と対決し、その闇の中から<line-break>未来をつかみとるために、<line-break>私はここへ来た。<line-break>行きましょう、<value:$28>。<line-break>エンシャント城内へ…！<end>"
EN: "I came here to confront fate and wrest the future from its darkness. Let's go, <value:$28>. Into the Ancient Castle Interior...!<end>"
KO: "운명과 맞서 그 어둠 속에서 미래를 붙잡기 위해 저는 여기에 왔습니다. 갑시다, <value:$28>. 엔샨트 성 안으로…!<end>"

## 1330174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（エンシャント城内へ行かなければ…）<end>"
EN: "(I have to reach the Ancient Castle Interior...)<end>"
KO: "(엔샨트 성 안으로 가야 해…)<end>"

## 1330175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気づいておるかの？<line-break>ここは謁見の間…お前が皇帝となり、<line-break>預言を成就させるにはよい場所じゃ。<line-break>運命には逆らえぬ。<line-break>どうじゃ、ザギヴ。<line-break>あきらめはついたかの？<end>"
EN: "Do you realize where you are? This is the audience chamber, a fitting place for you to become emperor and fulfill the prophecy. None can defy fate. Well, Zagiv? Have you resigned yourself to it?<end>"
KO: "눈치챘느냐? 여기는 알현실…. 네가 황제가 되어 예언을 성취하기 좋은 곳이지. 운명에는 거스를 수 없다. 어떠냐, 자기브. 이제 포기할 마음이 들었나?<end>"

## 1330176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "運命なんて、存在しないわ。<line-break>存在するのだとすれば、<line-break>それは、変えるためにあるものよ。<end>"
EN: "There is no such thing as fate. And if there is, it exists to be changed.<end>"
KO: "운명 같은 건 존재하지 않아. 만약 존재한다면 바꾸기 위해 있는 거야.<end>"

## 1330177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フォフォ…。<line-break>つまらぬ希望にすがりおって。<line-break>ならば、お前の言葉を借りて言おう。<line-break>希望など、存在しはしない。<line-break>存在するのだとすれば、<line-break>それは、人を惑わすためにあるものよ。<end>"
EN: "Ho ho... Still clinging to that worthless hope. Then let me borrow your words: there is no such thing as hope. And if there is, it exists only to lead people astray.<end>"
KO: "호호…. 시답잖은 희망에 매달리는군. 그렇다면 네 말을 빌려 말해 주마. 희망 따위는 존재하지 않는다. 존재한다면 인간을 현혹하기 위해 존재할 뿐이지.<end>"

## 1330178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少々厳しくしつけてやらねば<line-break>ならぬようじゃな。<line-break>この妖術宰相ゾフォルがの！<end>"
EN: "It seems you need a sterner lesson―and I, Zofor, the Sorcerous Chancellor, shall give it to you!<end>"
KO: "조금 엄하게 가르쳐 줘야겠구나. 이 요술재상 조포르가 말이다!<end>"

## 1330179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お主は…運命に…挑み、<line-break>そして…自らの手で…<line-break>未来をつかみ取ろうというのか…。<line-break>わしは…わしは…運命に屈した、<line-break>ただの負け犬だったというのか…？<line-break>バカ…な…ぐふっ！<end>"
EN: "You would... defy fate... and seize the future... with your own hands? Was I... was I nothing but a beaten cur... who surrendered to fate? Impossible... Gah!<end>"
KO: "…너는… 운명에… 도전하고, 그리고… 자신의 손으로… 미래를 붙잡겠다는 것이냐…. 나는… 나는… 운명에 굴복한 그저 패배자였다는 건가…? 말도… 안 돼… 크헉!<end>"

## 1330180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…よかろう、証明してみせよ。<end>"
EN: "Very well... Prove it.<end>"
KO: "…좋다, 증명해 보아라.<end>"

## 1330181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主の体内に棲み着いておる<line-break>円卓騎士マゴスを<line-break>…召喚する。<line-break>きゃつを倒せるか？<line-break>絶望すべきは運命に屈した者だけだと<line-break>証明…できるか！？<end>"
EN: "I shall summon Magos, the Knight of the Round Table dwelling within you. Can you defeat him? Can you prove... that only those who surrender to fate need despair?!<end>"
KO: "그대 몸 안에 깃든 원탁기사 마고스를… 소환하겠다. 녀석을 쓰러뜨릴 수 있겠나? 절망해야 할 자는 운명에 굴복한 자뿐임을 증명할 수 있겠는가!?<end>"

## 1330182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "できるわ！<line-break>私はそのためにここに来た！<end>"
EN: "I can! That's why I came here!<end>"
KO: "할 수 있어! 난 그러려고 여기 왔어!<end>"

## 1330183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかろう！　示してみせよ！<line-break>これこそが…おぬしの絶望…。<line-break>円卓騎士マゴスじゃ！！<end>"
EN: "Very well! Show me! Behold... your despair: Magos, Knight of the Round Table!<end>"
KO: "좋다! 보여 봐라! 이것이야말로… 네 절망…. 원탁기사 마고스다!!<end>"

## 1330184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "終わったわ…。<end>"
EN: "It's over...<end>"
KO: "끝났어….<end>"

## 1330185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいえ…これからが始まりなのね。<line-break>これからの毎日こそ…私が私として<line-break>生きてゆく、本当の未来なのだもの。<end>"
EN: "No... This is where it begins. From this day forward, I'll live as myself. That will be my true future.<end>"
KO: "아니… 이제부터가 시작이구나. 앞으로의 하루하루야말로… 내가 나로서 살아가는 진짜 미래니까.<end>"

## 1330186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やはり逆らえぬ。何人も逆らえぬもの…<line-break>それこそが運命よ。この女の有様が、<line-break>如実にそれを語っておろう。<end>"
EN: "In the end, fate cannot be defied. What no one can defy―that is fate. This woman's state proves it plainly.<end>"
KO: "역시 거스를 수 없다. 누구도 거스를 수 없는 것… 그것이 바로 운명이다. 이 여자의 모습이 그걸 여실히 보여 주지 않느냐.<end>"

## 1330187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ…私、皇帝になったの。<line-break>ザギヴがディンガルを支配する…。<line-break>運命の預言は、現実となったわ…。<line-break>この力！　<line-break>私の心身にみなぎる、この力！<line-break>フフ…フフフフ…！<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... I became emperor. Zagiv rules Dyneskal now... The prophecy of fate has come true... Such power! This power filling my body and mind! Heh... heh heh heh...!<end>"
KO: "후후후… 나 황제가 됐어. 자기브가 딘갈을 지배한다…. 운명의 예언은 현실이 되었어…. 이 힘! 내 몸과 마음에 넘치는 이 힘! 후후… 후후후후…!<end>"

## 1330188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主が留守にしとる間に、<line-break>この女は、堕ちてしもうたのじゃよ。<line-break>底も知れぬ闇にな…。<line-break>運命にはあらがえぬ。<line-break>抗っても、傷つくのみじゃ。<line-break>お主にも、これでわかったろう。<end>"
EN: "While you were away, this woman fell and became a fathomless darkness. Fate cannot be resisted. Struggle against it, and you will only be hurt. Surely you understand that now.<end>"
KO: "네가 자리를 비운 사이 이 여자는 타락해 버렸다. 밑도 끝도 없는 어둠으로…. 운명에는 거스를 수 없어. 맞서 봐야 상처만 입을 뿐이다. 너도 이제 알겠지.<end>"

## 1330189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どう、<value:$28>、<line-break>お気に召したかしら…？<line-break>この静寂の都、エンシャントはいかが？<line-break>私は皇帝。もう、私を否定できる者は、<line-break>誰ひとりとして存在しないわ。<line-break>フフフフ…。<end>"
EN: "Well, <value:$28>? Do you like it...? What do you think of Ancient, the city of silence? I am emperor now. No one remains who can deny me. Heh heh heh...<end>"
KO: "어때, <value:$28>, 마음에 들었어…? 이 고요한 도시 엔샨트는 어때? 나는 황제야. 이제 나를 부정할 수 있는 자는 누구 하나 존재하지 않아. 후후후후….<end>"

## 1330190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしたの？　顔が真っ青よ、<line-break><value:$28>。まるで、<line-break>悪い夢にうなされているみたい…。<line-break>そう…気に入らないの…。<line-break>少し残念だけど…それなら、<line-break>出て行ってもらうしかないわね…。<end>"
EN: "What's wrong? You're deathly pale, <value:$28>. You look as though you're trapped in a nightmare... Oh... you don't like it. That's a little disappointing... but then I have no choice but to make you leave...<end>"
KO: "왜 그래? 얼굴이 새파랗네, <value:$28>. 꼭 악몽에 시달리는 것 같아…. 그래… 마음에 안 드는구나…. 조금 아쉽지만… 그렇다면 나가 달라고 할 수밖에 없겠네….<end>"

## 1330191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の帝都から…<line-break>私の支配する、<line-break>この静かな世界から…。<line-break>さようなら、<value:$28>。<line-break>私はもうこの静寂しか愛せないの…。<end>"
EN: "Leave my imperial capital... this silent world under my rule... Farewell, <value:$28>. Silence is all I can love now...<end>"
KO: "나의 제도에서… 내가 지배하는 이 고요한 세계에서…. 안녕, <value:$28>. 나는 이제 이 정적밖에 사랑할 수 없어….<end>"

## 1330192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うう…<line-break>ウウ…ア…アア…アアグ…<line-break>ガアアアアアッ！<end>"
EN: "Ugh... Uuugh... Ah... Aah... Aaagh... GRAAAAAAH!<end>"
KO: "으으… 우우… 아… 아아… 아아그… 가아아아앗!<end>"

## 1330193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギヒッ…！　<line-break>ギーヒッヒッ！<line-break>やぁーっと、くたばりやがったか！<end>"
EN: "Gih...! Geee-hee-hee! Finally kicked the bucket, did she?!<end>"
KO: "기힛…! 기히히힛! 드디어 뒈졌나!<end>"

## 1330195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギーヒッヒッ！　この女が死んで、<line-break>俺は本格的に活動できる！<line-break>ありがとよ。ギーヒッヒッ！<line-break>まず手始めに、お前を殺す！<line-break>マゴス様の恐怖のフルコース、<line-break>とくと味わえ！　ギーヒッ！<end>"
EN: "Geee-hee-hee! Now that this woman's dead, I can finally cut loose! Thanks for that. Geee-hee-hee! And I'll start by killing you! Prepare to feast on a full course of the great Magos's horrors! Gih-hee!<end>"
KO: "기히히힛! 이 여자가 죽어서 이제 내가 본격적으로 움직일 수 있다! 고맙다. 기히히힛! 우선 너부터 죽이겠다! 마고스 님의 공포 풀코스를 실컷 맛봐라! 기히힛!<end>"

## 1330196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギヒヒ…ご苦労なこったぜ…。<line-break>俺を倒したって、<line-break>何も変わりゃしねえぜ。<end>"
EN: "Gih heh heh... All that work for nothing... Defeating me won't change a thing.<end>"
KO: "기히히… 수고가 많구만…. 날 쓰러뜨린다고 아무것도 달라지진 않아.<end>"

## 1330197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめん…なさい。<line-break>私、あなたを…待てなかった！<line-break>　<line-break>運命に立ち向かえなかった、<line-break>弱い私を…許し…て…。<end>"
EN: "I'm... sorry. I couldn't... wait for you! I was too weak to face fate... Please... forgive me...<end>"
KO: "미안…해. 나, 당신을… 기다리지 못했어! 운명에 맞서지 못한 약한 나를… 용서…해 줘….<end>"

## 1330198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "『僕は人間になれるのかなあ…』<end>"
EN: "“I wonder if I can become human...”<end>"
KO: "『나는 인간이 될 수 있을까…』<end>"

## 1330199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "『…そう、哀れな人形は<line-break>　おじいさんに尋ねました』<line-break> <line-break>ウフフアハハ！<end>"
EN: "“...And so the poor puppet asked the old man.” Hee-hee-ha-ha!<end>"
KO: "『…그래서 가엾은 인형은 할아버지에게 물었습니다』 우후후아하하!<end>"

## 1330200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "空中都市では世話になったね。<line-break>竜王があのとき言った言葉、<line-break>覚えてるかなあ？<end>"
EN: "You helped me out in the Floating City. Do you remember what the Dragon King said back then?<end>"
KO: "공중도시에서는 신세를 졌지. 그때 용왕이 했던 말, 기억하나?<end>"

## 1330201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕はね、闇と虚無の狭間にたまった<line-break>かなえられなかった願いと悲しみ…<line-break>ひしめく苦しみから生まれたんだ。<line-break>この世界を虚無に帰し、<line-break>すべての悲しみを終わらせるためにね。<line-break>僕は人間の形をした何か…人形なのさ。<end>"
EN: "I was born from all the suffering crowded between darkness and the Void―the unfulfilled wishes and sorrows that had gathered there. I exist to return this world to the Void and put an end to all sorrow. I'm something in human form... a puppet.<end>"
KO: "나는 말이지, 어둠과 허무의 틈에 쌓인 이루지 못한 소원과 슬픔… 들끓는 고통에서 태어났어. 이 세계를 허무로 돌려 모든 슬픔을 끝내기 위해서 말이야. 나는 인간 모습을 한 무언가… 인형인 셈이지.<end>"

## 1330202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕が何をたくらんでるか<line-break>わかってくれたかな。<line-break>ねえ。僕のこと殺したいだろう？<end>"
EN: "Do you understand what I'm planning now? You want to kill me, don't you?<end>"
KO: "내가 뭘 꾸미는지 이해했으려나. 있지, 나를 죽이고 싶지?<end>"

## 1330204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフアハハ！<line-break>それは、賢明だ。<line-break>だって、僕を殺してもムダだもん。<line-break>人の悲しみが終わらない限り<line-break>僕はいくらでも現れる。<end>"
EN: "Hee-hee-ha-ha! That's wise. Killing me would be pointless, you see. As long as human sorrow endures, I'll keep appearing again and again.<end>"
KO: "우후후, 아하하! 그건 현명하네. 날 죽여도 소용없거든. 인간의 슬픔이 끝나지 않는 한 나는 얼마든지 나타날 거야.<end>"

## 1330205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、その悲しみまでも利用して<line-break>このバカげた創造という輪を<line-break>至聖神は回し続けているんだよ。<end>"
EN: "And the Most Holy God exploits even that sorrow to keep this absurd wheel called creation turning.<end>"
KO: "그리고 그 슬픔마저 이용해 이 바보 같은 창조라는 수레바퀴를 지성신은 계속 돌리고 있는 거야.<end>"

## 1330206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、いらっしゃい。<line-break>待っていたよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Ah, welcome. I've been waiting for you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "어서 와. 기다리고 있었어, <value:$28>.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

What are you doing here?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>23What are you doing here?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27What are you doing in a place like this?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26What are you doing here?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24What are you doing here?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25What are you doing in a place like this?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192What are you doing here?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193W-what do you want with us?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Shalli! Stop!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Shalli! Scheming again?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Shalli! What are you planning this time?!<end>Shalli! What are you planning to do all the way out here?!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2또 당신이야! 샤리!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3또 네놈이냐! 샤리!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4어머? 이런 데서 뭐 하고 있는 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5네놈, 이런 데서 뭐 하고 있는 거냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6또 너냐, 샤리! 이번엔 뭘 하려는 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>7신기를 찾을 때도 몇 번 봤지…. 네놈이 샤리냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>9왠지 위험해 보이는 꼬마네. 네가 소문난 샤리라는 녀석이지!<end><if><value:$01><equal>12로스톨뿐만 아니라 이런 데까지 나타난 거냐. 대체 뭘 하고 있는 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>11이런 데서 뭐 하고 있는 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13네놈! 여기서 뭐 하고 있는 거냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>10네놈, 뭐 하고 있는 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14네놈, 무슨 용건이냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15샤리! 이런 곳까지 뭐 하러 온 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17잠깐, 너 대체 뭐예요! 이 몸들 방해만 하고! 이 몸의 인내심에도 한계가 있다고요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19샤리이! 이런 데까지 나타나서 뭐 하는 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20네놈… 대체 정체가 뭐냐. 여기서 뭘 하고 있지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>21네놈…. 여기서 뭘 하고 있는 거냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>23여기서 뭘 하고 있는 거냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27이런 데서 뭘 하고 있는 거냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26여기서 뭘 하고 있는 게냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24여기서 뭘 하고 있는 겁니까!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25이런 데서 뭐 하고 있는 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192여기서 뭘 하고 있는 겁니까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193우, 우리에게 무슨 용건이죠!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194샤리! 그만둬!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191샤리! 또 나쁜 짓을 꾸미는 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190샤리! 이번엔 뭘 할 셈이냐!<end>샤리! 이런 곳까지 나타나서 뭘 하려는 거야!<end>"

## 1330208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あはは。そう邪険にしないでよ。<line-break>ネメアを助けに来ただけなんだから。<line-break><value:$28>だって、そうでしょ？<line-break>でも、もういいや。<line-break>ネメア、いらなくなっちゃった。<end>"
EN: "Ahaha. Don't be so cold. I only came to save Nemea. That's why you're here too, isn't it, <value:$28>? But never mind. I don't need Nemea anymore.<end>"
KO: "아하하. 그렇게 매몰차게 굴지 마. 네메아를 구하러 왔을 뿐이니까. <value:$28>도 그렇지? 하지만 이제 됐어. 네메아, 필요 없어졌거든.<end>"

## 1330209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>君がいるからね。<end>"
EN: "Because I have you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 네가 있으니까.<end>"

## 1330211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、儀式を始めようか。<end>"
EN: "Now, shall we begin the ritual?<end>"
KO: "자, 의식을 시작할까.<end>"

## 1330212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウルグはネメアに降臨する。<line-break>僕もそう思っていたよ。<line-break>　<line-break>でもね、ウルグは<line-break>いつだって捜してるんだ。<line-break>もっとも適した入れ物をね。<end>"
EN: "Ulugh would descend into Nemea. That's what I thought too. But you see, Ulugh is always searching for the most suitable vessel.<end>"
KO: "울그는 네메아에게 강림한다. 나도 그렇게 생각했어. 하지만 울그는 언제나 찾고 있어. 가장 알맞은 그릇을.<end>"

## 1330213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>君は強くなりすぎたんだ。<line-break>ウルグはネメアより君がいいってさ。<line-break>君に降臨したほうが<line-break>より強大な力を出せるんだよ。<line-break>破壊神は君の肉体をお望みだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you've grown too strong. Ulugh says you're preferable to Nemea. Descending into you would unleash far greater power. The God of Destruction desires your body.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 너는 너무 강해졌어. 울그는 네메아보다 네가 좋대. 네게 강림하는 편이 더 강대한 힘을 낼 수 있거든. 파괴신은 네 육체를 원한다.<end>"
