KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2뭐라고! 울그가 <value:$28>에게!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>3뭐라고! 울그가 <value:$28>에게!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4왠지 엄청난 일이 되어 가네. …도망치는 게 좋을지도.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5뭐, 뭐라고! 울그가 <value:$28>에게!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6그, 그럴 수가…. 울그가 <value:$28>에게!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>7뭐라고! 울그가 <value:$28>에게!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>9파괴신 눈에 들다니 <value:$28>도 제법이네. …하지만 가만있을 순 없지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12뭐라고! 울그가 <value:$28>에게!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11그, 그럴 수가…. 울그가 <value:$28>에게!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13뭐라고! 울그가 <value:$28>에게!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10울그가 누나에게? 진짜야!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14뭐라고! 울그가 <value:$28>에게!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15뭐, 뭐라고! 울그가 <value:$28>에게!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17뭐, 뭐, 뭐라고오! 울그가 <value:$28>에게!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19에!? 그, 그럴 수가아…. 안 돼, <value:$28>! 파괴신 같은 게 되면 안 돼!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20뭐! 울그가 <value:$28>에게라고…!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>21이럴 수가! <value:$28>!<end><if><value:$01><equal>23울그라고!? 이럴 수가!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27뭐라고! 울그가 <value:$28>에게!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26뭐라고! 울그가 <value:$28>에게!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>24그, 그럴 수가…. 울그가 <value:$28> 님에게!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>25울그가…?! 그럴 수가….<end><if><value:$01><equal>192울그가….<end><if><value:$01><equal>193<value:$28> 씨!? 안 돼요! 파괴신 같은 게 되지 마세요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194울그가 <value:$28>에게!? 말도 안 돼!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191그럴 수가!? 이대로면 <value:$28>가 파괴신이 돼 버려!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190뭐!? <value:$28>에게 울그가!?<end><value:$28>가 파괴신으로!? 굉장한 시가 나올 것 같아…. 아니, 이럴 때가 아니지.<end>"

## 1330215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれ？　なんだ…？<end>"
EN: "Huh? What's happening...?<end>"
KO: "어라? 뭐지…?<end>"

## 1330216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神器に異物が混入…だと？<line-break>意志あるもの…、ソウル…？<end>"
EN: "A foreign presence within the divine artifact...? Something with a will of its own... a Soul...?<end>"
KO: "신기에 이물질이 섞였다고…? 의지를 지닌 것… 소울…?<end>"

## 1330217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はじめまして、シャリ君。<line-break>私はオルファウスといいます。<line-break>よろしくお見知り置きを。<end>"
EN: "A pleasure to meet you, Shalli. My name is Orphaus. Do remember me.<end>"
KO: "처음 뵙겠습니다, 샤리 군. 저는 오르파우스라고 합니다. 잘 부탁합니다.<end>"

## 1330218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クッ、こうなることを見越し、<line-break>魂吸いの指輪にわざとソウルを<line-break>吸わせて潜んでいたのか…。<line-break>破壊神の復活を<line-break>直前でくい止めるために…。<end>"
EN: "Damn... You foresaw this, had the Soul-Draining Ring absorb your Soul, and hid inside it deliberately... all to stop the God of Destruction's resurrection at the final moment...<end>"
KO: "큭, 이렇게 될 걸 내다보고 영혼흡수의 반지에 일부러 소울을 빨아들이게 한 뒤 숨어 있었나…. 파괴신의 부활을 직전에 막기 위해….<end>"

## 1330219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とっさの思いつきだったんですけど、<line-break>ネメアはできのいい子でしてね。<line-break>無言で理解してくれましたよ。<line-break>ウルグの復活は、失敗ですね。<end>"
EN: "It was only a spur-of-the-moment idea, but Nemea is a clever boy. He understood without a word. Ulugh's resurrection has failed.<end>"
KO: "순간 떠오른 생각이었지만 네메아는 영리한 아이여서 말없이 이해해 줬습니다. 울그의 부활은 실패네요.<end>"

## 1330220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうでもないさ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ウルグの力の一部は、<line-break>すでに<value:$28>の精神に<line-break>入り込んでるんだよ？<end>"
EN: "I wouldn't say that. Some of Ulugh's power has already seeped into <value:$28>'s mind, you know.<end>"
KO: "꼭 그렇지도 않아. 울그의 힘 일부는 이미 <value:$28>의 정신 속에 들어가 있거든?<end>"

## 1330221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうするつもり？<end>"
EN: "What are you going to do?<end>"
KO: "어떻게 할 생각이야?<end>"

## 1330222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>を<line-break>甘く見ないことですね。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>闇の神器の力さえ取り除けば、<line-break><value:$28>は<line-break>破壊神にも負けはしないでしょう。<end>"
EN: "You underestimate <value:$28>. Once the power of the Dark Artifacts is removed, even the God of Destruction will not defeat <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>을 얕보지 마세요. 어둠의 신기의 힘만 제거하면 <value:$28>은 파괴신에게도 지지 않을 겁니다.<end>"

## 1330223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あはは、おもしろいね。<line-break>確かにそうかもしれないよ。<line-break>ま、今回は一本とられたってとこかな。<line-break>でもね、これが切り札じゃないんだよ。<line-break>フフフ、ここから戻れたら、<line-break>エンシャントにおいでよ。<end>"
EN: "Ahaha, how interesting. You may be right. I suppose you've bested me this time. But this wasn't my trump card. Heh heh... If you make it back from here, come to Ancient.<end>"
KO: "아하하, 재미있네. 확실히 그럴지도 몰라. 뭐, 이번엔 한 방 먹었다고 해 두지. 하지만 이게 비장의 수는 아니야. 후후후, 여기서 돌아올 수 있으면 엔샨트로 와.<end>"

## 1330224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ってるよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'll be waiting, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "기다리고 있을게, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1330225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　ワレを…うけいれよ…。　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　お前の…ニクタイには…、<line-break>　　　　邪神の魂こそ…、ふさわしい…。<end>"
EN: "Accept me... The soul of an evil god... is the one most fitting for your flesh...<end>"
KO: "나를… 받아들여라…. 네… 육체에는… 사신의 영혼이야말로… 어울린다….<end>"

## 1330226
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　相容れぬの…ならば…、　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　ヨワき存在が…、<line-break>　　　　　　消え去る…、のみ…。<end>"
EN: "If we cannot coexist... then the weak being... will simply vanish...<end>"
KO: "서로 양립할 수… 없다면…. 약한 존재가… 사라질… 뿐….<end>"

## 1330227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　よもや人の子に…、屈するとは…。　　　<line-break>　　　　ワが魂の一部…、ムネンだが、<line-break>　　　　　　汝のモノとなろう…。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　フフ…、これも…よかろう…。<line-break>　　　　ワレのかけらが…残るのだ…。<line-break>　　　　わざわいの種は…、まかれた…。<end>"
EN: "To think I would yield... to a child of man... Though it galls me, a part of my soul... shall become yours... Heh... so be it... A fragment of me... will remain... The seed of calamity... has been sown...<end>"
KO: "설마 인간의 아이에게… 굴복할 줄이야…. 내 혼의 일부… 원통하지만 그대의 것이 되리라…. 후후… 이것도 좋겠지…. 나의 조각이… 남는 것이다…. 재앙의 씨앗은… 뿌려졌다….<end>"

## 1330228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　よもや人の子に…、屈するとは…。　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　だが…、いずれ…、<line-break>　　　　　滅びの時は…訪れよう…。<line-break>　　　ワレが…、テを…下すまでも…なく。<line-break>　　　　だが…、ネガわくば…いつか…、<line-break>　　　　我がテにて…汝らに…、滅びヲ！<end>"
EN: "To think I would yield... to a child of man... But one day... the hour of destruction will come... without my lifting a hand... And yet... I pray that someday... I may bring ruin upon you all by my own hand!<end>"
KO: "설마 인간의 아이에게… 굴복하다니…. 하지만… 언젠가… 멸망의 때는 찾아오리라…. 내가… 손을… 쓰지 않더라도…. 하지만… 바라건대… 언젠가… 내 손으로… 너희에게… 멸망을!<end>"

## 1330229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>…。<line-break>お願い、無事でいて！<end><if><value:$01><equal>3おい、本当に<line-break>大丈夫なんじゃろうな！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>4平気かしら？<line-break>全然動かないですよ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>5<value:$28>、大丈夫なのか？<line-break>このままってわけじゃないよな…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>6<value:$28>…。<line-break>大丈夫だよね。<line-break>絶対、帰ってくるよね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>7<value:$28>…。<line-break>無事なんでしょうね…！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>9帰ってこないわね。<line-break>ホントに大丈夫かな、<line-break><value:$28>…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>12勝てよ、<value:$28>。<end><if><value:$01><equal>11<value:$28>…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13<value:$28>…。<line-break>大丈夫なのか…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>10姉ちゃん、ひょっとして…。<line-break>いや、まさか。いや、でもなぁ。<line-break>ああっ！　早く帰ってこいよ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>14ううむ。戻ってこんな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>15大丈夫だよね、<value:$28>…。<line-break>君なら、きっと…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17いつまで伸びてるつもりかしらね。<line-break>早くこんなところから出たいのに。<line-break>もう！　なんで帰ってこないのよー！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>…。<line-break>お願い、帰ってきてぇ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…勝負が<line-break>ついたようだな…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>21まさか、このまま<line-break>ということは、あるまいな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>23<value:$28>…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>27<value:$28>…<line-break>早く帰ってこい…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>26大丈夫じゃろうか…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>24<value:$28>様…<line-break>どうかご無事で…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>25…お願いよ<line-break>早く帰ってきて…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>192…どうしてでしょう、<line-break>こんなに不安を覚えるのは。<line-break><line-break>…どうか、<line-break>無事で戻ってきて…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193<value:$28>さん…。<line-break>早く帰ってきて…<end><if><value:$01><equal>194お願いだ、<value:$28>…。<line-break>無事でいてくれ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191きっと…きっと大丈夫よね？<line-break><value:$28>…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190無事なのか…<line-break><value:$28>…。<end>帰ってきてよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>そうしてぼくに、ウルグに勝った<line-break><value:$28>の詩を作らせて…。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>... Please be safe!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3You're sure everything's going to be all right?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Is everything all right? There's no movement at all.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5<value:$28>, are you all right? You won't stay like this, will you...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6<value:$28>... You're all right, aren't you? Are you really all right, <value:$28>...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>12Win, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11<value:$28>...<end><if><value:$01><equal>13<value:$28>... Are you all right...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Sis, could you have...? No, impossible. But still... Ah! Hurry up and come back!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Hmm. It will, won't it? <value:$28>...<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Are you safe, <value:$28>...?<end>Come back, <value:$28>. Then let me compose a poem about how <value:$28> defeated Ulugh...<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>…. 부탁이야, 무사해 줘!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3이봐, 정말 괜찮은 거겠지!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4괜찮을까? 전혀 움직이지 않는데.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5<value:$28>, 괜찮은 거야? 이대로 끝인 건 아니지….<end><if><value:$01><equal>6<value:$28>…. 괜찮지? 반드시 돌아올 거지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>7<value:$28>…. 무사한 거겠지…!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>9안 돌아오네. 정말 괜찮을까, <value:$28>….<end><if><value:$01><equal>12이겨라, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11<value:$28>….<end><if><value:$01><equal>13<value:$28>…. 괜찮은 건가….<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 혹시…. 아니, 설마. 그래도…. 아아! 빨리 돌아와!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14으음. 안 돌아오는군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15괜찮지, <value:$28>…. 너라면 분명….<end><if><value:$01><equal>17언제까지 뻗어 있을 생각이야. 빨리 이런 곳에서 나가고 싶은데. 정말! 왜 안 돌아오는 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>…. 부탁이야, 돌아와아!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…승부가 난 것 같군….<end><if><value:$01><equal>21설마 이대로 끝나는 건 아니겠지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23<value:$28>….<end><if><value:$01><equal>27<value:$28>… 빨리 돌아와….<end><if><value:$01><equal>26괜찮은 게냐….<end><if><value:$01><equal>24<value:$28> 님… 부디 무사하시길….<end><if><value:$01><equal>25…부탁이야. 빨리 돌아와….<end><if><value:$01><equal>192…왜 이렇게 불안한 걸까요. …부디 무사히 돌아와 줘….<end><if><value:$01><equal>193<value:$28> 씨…. 빨리 돌아와 주세요….<end><if><value:$01><equal>194부탁이다, <value:$28>…. 무사해 줘.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191분명… 분명 괜찮겠지? <value:$28>….<end><if><value:$01><equal>190무사한 건가… <value:$28>….<end>돌아와 줘, <value:$28>. 그리고 내가 울그에게 이긴 <value:$28>의 시를 만들게 해 줘….<end>"

## 1330231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくがんばりましたね。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ウルグが<value:$28>に<line-break>降臨するとは計算外でしたが、<line-break>復活は阻止されました。<end>"
EN: "You did well. I hadn't anticipated Ulugh descending into <value:$28>, but Ulugh's resurrection has been stopped.<end>"
KO: "정말 수고하셨습니다. 울그가 <value:$28>에게 강림한 것은 계산 밖이었지만 부활은 저지되었습니다.<end>"

## 1330232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウルグさえ退けてしまえば、<line-break>ネメアは自力で<line-break>戻ってこられるはずです。<line-break>我々はいったん戻って、<line-break>備えなければなりません。<line-break>最後の戦いにね。<end>"
EN: "Once Ulugh has been driven out, Nemea should be able to return on his own. For now, we must go back and prepare for the final battle.<end>"
KO: "울그만 물리치면 네메아는 자기 힘으로 돌아올 수 있을 겁니다. 우리는 일단 돌아가 준비해야 합니다. 마지막 싸움을 위해서요.<end>"

## 1330233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、猫屋敷に戻りますよ。<end>"
EN: "Come. We're returning to Cat Manor.<end>"
KO: "자, 고양이 저택으로 돌아가죠.<end>"

## 1330234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、いらっしゃい。<line-break>待っていたよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Ah, welcome. I've been waiting for you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "어서 와. 기다리고 있었어, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1330235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2また、あなたなの！<line-break>シャリ！！<line-break>ネメア様に何を！<end><if><value:$01><equal>3また、お主か！<line-break>シャリ！！<line-break>ネメア様に何をしおった！<end><if><value:$01><equal>4また、シャリが何かしたみたい。<line-break>ネメア様の様子が変！<end><if><value:$01><equal>5てめえ、ネメアに何しやがった！<end><if><value:$01><equal>6ネメアに何をしたの！<end><if><value:$01><equal>7貴様！　ネメア様に何をした！<end><if><value:$01><equal>9ちょっと、あんた。<line-break>ネメア様に何してくれたわけ？<end><if><value:$01><equal>12ロストールだけじゃなく、　<line-break>こんなところでも<line-break>悪さしてやがったか。<line-break>ネメアにいったい何をした！<end><if><value:$01><equal>11こんなところで何をしているの！<line-break>ネメア様に何をしたの！<end><if><value:$01><equal>13貴様！<line-break>ネメアに何をした！<end><if><value:$01><equal>10ネメアの様子がおかしいぞ。<line-break>てめぇ、何しやがった！<end><if><value:$01><equal>14お主！<line-break>ネメアに何をしおった！<end><if><value:$01><equal>15<value:$28>、<line-break>ネメアさんの様子が変だよ！<line-break>シャリが何かやったんだ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>17また、やっかいごとなのぉー！<line-break>ちょっと、シャリ！<line-break>今度はネメアに何したのよー！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19ネメアさんの様子が変だよぉ！<line-break>シャリが何かやったんだ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>20貴様…<line-break>ネメアに何をした？<end><if><value:$01><equal>21貴様…！<line-break>ネメアに何をした！<end><if><value:$01><equal>23貴様…！<line-break>ネメア様に何をした！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>27様子がおかしい…<line-break>シャリ！　何をした！！<end><if><value:$01><equal>26ネメア殿に何をしたんじゃ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>24ネメア様に何をしたのですか！<end><if><value:$01><equal>25様子がおかしい…。<line-break>あなた、何をしたの？！<end><if><value:$01><equal>192闇の気配…。<line-break>限りなく黒く、限りなく深い…。<line-break>これは…？<end><if><value:$01><equal>193ネメアさんに何を！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>194ネメア…！？<line-break>くっ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>191あなた…。<line-break>ネメアに何したの！<end><if><value:$01><equal>190シャリ！<line-break>ネメアに何をした！<end><value:$28>、<line-break>ネメアの様子が変だよ？<line-break>シャリが何かやったんだ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2You again! Shalli! What have you done to Lord Nemea?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3You again, Shalli! What have you done to Lord Nemea?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4It looks like Shalli's done something again. There's something wrong with Lord Nemea!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5What the hell did you do to Nemea?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6What did you do to Nemea?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>7You! What did you do to Lord Nemea?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>9Hey, you. Just what have you done to Lord Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>12So you were making trouble here too, not just in Rostorl. What the hell did you do to Nemea?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>11What are you doing here?! What did you do to Lord Nemea?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13You! What did you do to Nemea?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>10There's something wrong with Nemea. What the hell did you do?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14You! What have you done to Nemea this time?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Something's wrong with Nemea! Shalli did something to him.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20You... What did you do to Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>21You...! What did you do to Nemea?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>23You...! What did you do?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26What did you do to Lord Nemea?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24What did you do to Lord Nemea?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25Something's wrong... What did you do?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192A presence of darkness... infinitely black, infinitely deep... What is this...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193What did you do to Nemea?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Nemea...?! Damn!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191You... What did you do to Nemea?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Shalli! What did you do to Nemea?!<end><value:$28>, something's wrong with Nemea. Shalli did something to him.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2또 당신이야! 샤리!! 네메아 님에게 무슨 짓을!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3또 네놈이냐! 샤리!! 네메아 님에게 무슨 짓을 한 게냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4또 샤리가 뭔가 한 것 같아. 네메아 님 상태가 이상해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5네놈, 네메아에게 무슨 짓을 한 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6네메아에게 무슨 짓을 한 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>7네놈! 네메아 님에게 무슨 짓을 했나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>9잠깐, 너. 네메아 님에게 무슨 짓 한 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>12로스톨뿐 아니라 이런 곳에서도 못된 짓을 했나. 네메아에게 대체 무슨 짓을 했어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>11이런 곳에서 뭘 하고 있는 거야! 네메아 님에게 무슨 짓을 했어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13네놈! 네메아에게 무슨 짓을 했나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>10네메아 상태가 이상해. 네놈, 무슨 짓 한 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14네놈! 네메아에게 무슨 짓을 한 게냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15<value:$28>, 네메아 씨 상태가 이상해! 샤리가 뭔가 한 거야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17또 골칫거리야!? 잠깐 샤리! 이번엔 네메아에게 무슨 짓을 한 거야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19네메아 씨 상태가 이상해애! 샤리가 뭔가 한 거야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20네놈… 네메아에게 무슨 짓을 했지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>21네놈…! 네메아에게 무슨 짓을 했나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>23네놈…! 네메아 님에게 무슨 짓을 했나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27상태가 이상해… 샤리! 무슨 짓을 했나!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26네메아 공에게 무슨 짓을 한 게냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24네메아 님에게 무슨 짓을 한 겁니까!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25상태가 이상해…. 당신, 무슨 짓을 한 거야?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192어둠의 기척…. 한없이 검고, 한없이 깊어…. 이건…?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193네메아 씨에게 무슨 짓을!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>194네메아…!? 큭!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191당신…. 네메아에게 무슨 짓을 했어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190샤리! 네메아에게 무슨 짓을 했나!<end><value:$28>, 네메아 상태가 이상해. 샤리가 뭔가 한 거야.<end>"

## 1330236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誤解しないでほしいな。<line-break>僕は、ネメアを救ってあげたんだ。<line-break>次元のはざまからね。<end>"
EN: "Don't misunderstand. I saved Nemea―from the Interdimensional Rift.<end>"
KO: "오해하지 않았으면 해. 나는 네메아를 구해 준 거야. 차원의 틈새에서 말이지.<end>"

## 1330237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグを呼び出して、<line-break>世界を虚無に帰すんだ。<end>"
EN: "I'm going to summon Ulugh, the God of Destruction, and return the world to the Void.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그를 불러내서 세계를 허무로 되돌리는 거야.<end>"

## 1330238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれ？　なんだ…？<end>"
EN: "Huh? What's happening...?<end>"
KO: "어라? 뭐지…?<end>"

## 1330239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神器に異物が混入…だと？<line-break>意志あるもの…、ソウル…？<end>"
EN: "A foreign presence within the divine artifact...? Something with a will of its own... a Soul...?<end>"
KO: "신기에 이물질이 섞였다고…? 의지를 지닌 것… 소울…?<end>"

## 1330240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はじめまして、シャリ君。<line-break>私はオルファウスといいます。<line-break>よろしくお見知り置きを。<end>"
EN: "A pleasure to meet you, Shalli. My name is Orphaus. Do remember me.<end>"
KO: "처음 뵙겠습니다, 샤리 군. 저는 오르파우스라고 합니다. 잘 부탁합니다.<end>"

## 1330241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クッ、こうなることを見越し、<line-break>魂吸いの指輪にわざとソウルを<line-break>吸わせて潜んでいたのか…。<line-break>破壊神の復活を<line-break>直前でくい止めるために…。<end>"
EN: "Damn... You foresaw this, had the Soul-Draining Ring absorb your Soul, and hid inside it deliberately... all to stop the God of Destruction's resurrection at the final moment...<end>"
KO: "큭, 이렇게 될 걸 내다보고 영혼흡수의 반지에 일부러 소울을 빨아들이게 한 뒤 숨어 있었나…. 파괴신의 부활을 직전에 막기 위해….<end>"

## 1330242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とっさの思いつきだったんですけど、<line-break>ネメアはできのいい子でしてね。<line-break>無言で理解してくれましたよ。<line-break>ウルグの復活は、失敗ですね。<end>"
EN: "It was only a spur-of-the-moment idea, but Nemea is a clever boy. He understood without a word. Ulugh's resurrection has failed.<end>"
KO: "순간 떠오른 생각이었지만 네메아는 영리한 아이여서 말없이 이해해 줬습니다. 울그의 부활은 실패네요.<end>"

## 1330243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうでもないさ。<line-break>ウルグの力の一部は、<line-break>すでにネメアの精神に入り込んでる。<line-break>どうするつもり？<end>"
EN: "I wouldn't say that. Some of Ulugh's power has already seeped into Nemea's mind. What are you going to do?<end>"
KO: "그렇지만도 않아. 울그의 힘 일부는 이미 네메아의 정신에 들어가 있어. 어떻게 할 생각이지?<end>"

## 1330244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "決まっています。<line-break>ネメアの精神世界に入り込んで<line-break>追い払います。<end>"
EN: "The answer is obvious. The plan is to enter Nemea's mind and drive Ulugh out.<end>"
KO: "정해져 있습니다. 네메아의 정신세계에 들어가 쫓아내겠습니다.<end>"

## 1330245
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あはは、おもしろいね。<line-break>さすがに賢者様はよく考えてるや。<line-break>ま、今回は一本とられたってとこかな。<line-break>でもね、これが切り札じゃないんだよ。<line-break>フフフ、ここから戻れたら、<line-break>エンシャントにおいでよ。<end>"
EN: "Ahaha, how interesting. The great sage has certainly thought this through. I suppose you've bested me this time. But this wasn't my trump card. Heh heh... If you make it back from here, come to Ancient.<end>"
KO: "아하하, 재미있네. 역시 현자님은 잘 생각해. 뭐, 이번엔 한 방 먹었다고 해 두지. 하지만 이게 비장의 수는 아니야. 후후, 여기서 돌아갈 수 있으면 엔샨트로 와.<end>"

## 1330246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ってるよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'll be waiting, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "기다리고 있을게, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1330247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、ぐずぐずしていられません。<line-break>今から、ネメアの精神世界に行きます。<line-break>　<line-break>おそらく、破壊神に半ば乗っ取られた<line-break>ネメアと戦うことになるでしょう。<line-break>心してください。<end>"
EN: "There is no time to waste. It is time to enter Nemea's mind. Nemea will most likely be half possessed by the God of Destruction and will have to be fought. Be prepared.<end>"
KO: "자, 꾸물거릴 수 없습니다. 지금부터 네메아의 정신세계로 갑니다. 아마 파괴신에게 반쯤 잠식된 네메아와 싸우게 될 겁니다. 각오하세요.<end>"

## 1330248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、行きますよ。<end>"
EN: "Then let us go.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 갑니다.<end>"

## 1330249
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　ダレ…だ…。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　ワレの…復活を…、<line-break>　　　　　　邪魔する…モノは…。<end>"
EN: "Who... is it... obstructing my resurrection...?<end>"
KO: "누구…냐…. 나의… 부활을… 방해하는… 자는….<end>"

## 1330250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やはり、かなり不安定な状態ですね。<line-break>これならば、ネメア本来の力も<line-break>ウルグとしての力も出せないはずです。<line-break>勝てるはずです。<line-break>今ならまだ間に合います。<end>"
EN: "As I suspected, his condition is highly unstable. He should be unable to wield either Nemea's own power or Ulugh's. We can defeat him. There is still time.<end>"
KO: "역시 상당히 불안정한 상태군요. 이렇다면 네메아 본래의 힘도 울그의 힘도 낼 수 없을 겁니다. 이길 수 있어요. 지금이라면 아직 늦지 않았습니다.<end>"

## 1330251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私がネメアの精神を守っておきます。<line-break>本気で戦って打ち負かしてください。<line-break>でないと、みんな死にますよ！<end>"
EN: "I will protect Nemea's mind. Fight him with all your strength and defeat him. Otherwise, everyone will die!<end>"
KO: "제가 네메아의 정신을 지키겠습니다. 전력을 다해 싸워 쓰러뜨려 주세요. 그러지 않으면 모두 죽습니다!<end>"

## 1330252
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　シすべき…モノたち…か…。　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　キエうせるが…いい…。<line-break>　　　　オロかなる存在に…、滅びを！<end>"
EN: "So... you are the ones who must die... Begone... Doom upon you, foolish creatures!<end>"
KO: "죽어야 할… 자들인가…. 사라져라…. 어리석은 존재들에게… 멸망을!<end>"

## 1330253
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　いずれ…滅びの時は…訪れよう…。　　　<line-break>　　　ワレが…、テを…下すまでも…なく。<line-break>　　　　だが…、ネガわくば…いつか…、<line-break>　　　　我がテにて…汝らに…、滅びヲ！<end>"
EN: "One day... the hour of destruction will come... without my lifting a hand... And yet... I pray that someday... I may bring ruin upon you all by my own hand!<end>"
KO: "언젠가… 멸망의 때는… 찾아올 것이다…. 내가… 손을… 쓰지 않아도…. 하지만… 바라건대… 언젠가… 내 손으로… 너희에게… 멸망을!<end>"

## 1330254
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "せっかく<value:$28>と僕で<line-break>闇の神器を集めたのに、<line-break>あんなことで死なれちゃ困るからね。<line-break>フフフ。さあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>残りの神器を僕にくれないかな。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "After all the trouble <value:$28> and I took to gather the Dark Artifacts, I can't have you dying over something like that. Heh heh. Now then, <value:$28>. Would you give me the remaining Dark Artifacts?<end>"
KO: "모처럼 <value:$28>와 내가 어둠의 신기를 모았는데 그런 일로 죽으면 곤란하잖아. 후후후. 자, <value:$28>. 남은 신기를 내게 주지 않을래?<end>"

## 1330255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2ネメア様は<line-break>本当に大丈夫なの！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>3ネメア様は<line-break>本当に大丈夫なんじゃろうな！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>4あら、平気かしら？<line-break>殺しちゃった？<end><if><value:$01><equal>5おお、すげえぜ！　ネメアに勝った！<line-break>って、そういう問題じゃねぇか。<line-break>ネメア、大丈夫かな？<end><if><value:$01><equal>6これで本当に<line-break>ウルグを追い払えたの？<end><if><value:$01><equal>7ネメア様は<line-break>本当に無事なのだろうな！<end><if><value:$01><equal>9あら、平気かしら？<line-break>ひょっとして、やりすぎちゃった？<end><if><value:$01><equal>12本来のネメアだったら<line-break>やばかっただろうな…。<line-break>これでウルグは追い払えたのかい？<end><if><value:$01><equal>11ネメア様は<line-break>本当に無事なの？<end><if><value:$01><equal>13ネメアはどうなった？<end><if><value:$01><equal>10これでウルグは<line-break>いなくなったのかな？<end><if><value:$01><equal>14これでウルグは<line-break>追い払えたのかの？<end><if><value:$01><equal>15本当の力じゃなかったとはいえ、<line-break>…あのネメアさんに勝っちゃった。<line-break>でも、ネメアさんは大丈夫なの？<end><if><value:$01><equal>17ま、アタクシたちにかかれば、<line-break>こんなものね。<line-break>で、ウルグはいなくなったのかしら？<end><if><value:$01><equal>19わわわ、ネメアさん<line-break>倒れちゃったよぉ。<line-break>大丈夫なの？<end><if><value:$01><equal>20弱いな…。<line-break>ネメアとは思えん。<end><if><value:$01><equal>21これで、ウルグは、<line-break>去ったのだろうな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>23ネメア様は<line-break>本当に無事なのか…？！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>27本当にウルグを<line-break>追い払うことができたのか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>26本当に邪神ウルグを<line-break>追い払うことができたのじゃろうか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>24ネメア様…<line-break>ご無事なのでしょうか…<end><if><value:$01><equal>25これでウルグを…<line-break>追い払うことができたの…？<end><if><value:$01><equal>192危機は…<line-break>去ったのでしょうか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>193勝っちゃいました…。<line-break>あ、ネメアさんは平気でしょうか！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>194ウルグは…<line-break>奴から去ったのか？<end><if><value:$01><equal>191本来の力を出せてなかったようね。<line-break>ネメアは無事かしら？<end><if><value:$01><equal>190ふぅ…。<line-break>これでウルグを追い払えたのか？<end>邪神ウルグから英雄ネメアを救う…。<line-break>なんてすごい冒険なんだろう！<line-break>って、ホントにウルグに勝てたの？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Is Lord Nemea really all right?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3Lord Nemea truly is all right, isn't he?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Oh, is he all right? Did we kill him?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Whoa, incredible! We beat Nemea! Wait, that's not the point. Is Nemea all right?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6Did we really drive Ulugh out?<end><if><value:$01><equal>7Lord Nemea truly is unharmed, isn't he?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>9Oh, is he all right? Did we perhaps overdo it?<end><if><value:$01><equal>12If Nemea had been at full strength, we'd have been in trouble... Did that drive Ulugh out?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11Is Lord Nemea really safe?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13What happened to Nemea?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Do you think Ulugh is gone now?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Have we driven Ulugh out?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15He wasn't at full strength, but still... We actually beat Nemea. Is he all right, though?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Well, naturally we prevailed. So, is Ulugh gone?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Oh no, Nemea fell down! Is he all right?<end><if><value:$01><equal>20Weak... That could hardly have been Nemea.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21Urg has departed now, I trust.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23Is Lord Nemea truly unharmed...?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Did we truly manage to drive Ulugh out?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Did we truly manage to drive out Ulugh, the evil god?<end><if><value:$01><equal>24Lord Nemea... Is he safe...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>25Did we... manage to drive Ulugh out?<end><if><value:$01><equal>192Has the danger... passed?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193We won... Oh! Is Nemea all right?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Has Ulugh... left him?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191It seems he couldn't use his full power. Is Nemea safe?<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Whew... Did that drive Ulugh out?<end>Saving the hero Nemea from Ulugh, the evil god... What an incredible adventure! Wait, did we really defeat Ulugh?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2네메아 님은 정말 괜찮은 거야!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>3네메아 님은 정말 괜찮은 게냐!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4어머, 괜찮을까? 죽인 거 아니야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5오, 대단해! 네메아를 이겼어! …아니, 그게 문제가 아니지. 네메아 괜찮나?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6이걸로 정말 울그를 쫓아낸 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>7네메아 님은 정말 무사한 것이겠지!<end><if><value:$01><equal>9어머, 괜찮을까? 혹시 너무 심하게 한 거 아냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>12원래 네메아였다면 위험했겠지…. 이걸로 울그를 쫓아낸 건가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11네메아 님은 정말 무사한 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13네메아는 어떻게 됐지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10이걸로 울그는 사라진 걸까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14이걸로 울그를 쫓아낸 건가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15본래 힘은 아니었다지만… 그 네메아 씨를 이겨 버렸어. 그런데 네메아 씨는 괜찮은 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17뭐, 아타쿠시들이 나서면 이 정도지. 그런데 울그는 사라진 걸까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>19와와, 네메아 씨 쓰러졌어어. 괜찮은 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>20약하군…. 네메아라고는 생각되지 않아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21이걸로 울그는 떠난 것이겠지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23네메아 님은 정말 무사한 건가…?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27정말 울그를 쫓아낸 건가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26정말 사신 울그를 쫓아낸 것이냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>24네메아 님… 무사하신 걸까요…<end><if><value:$01><equal>25이걸로 울그를… 쫓아낸 거야…?<end><if><value:$01><equal>192위기는… 지나간 걸까요?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193이겨 버렸어요…. 아, 네메아 씨는 괜찮을까요!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>194울그는… 그에게서 떠난 건가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191본래 힘을 못 냈던 것 같네. 네메아는 무사할까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>190후우…. 이걸로 울그를 쫓아낸 건가?<end>사신 울그에게서 영웅 네메아를 구하다니…. 굉장한 모험이야! 그런데 정말 울그를 이긴 거야?<end>"

## 1330256
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうでしょうね？<line-break>あとはネメア次第です。<line-break>　<line-break>この子の精神が<line-break>ウルグに破壊されていなければ、<line-break>戻ってくるでしょう。<end>"
EN: "Who can say? The rest is up to Nemea. If Ulugh has not destroyed his mind, he will return.<end>"
KO: "글쎄요? 이제 네메아에게 달렸습니다. 이 아이의 정신이 울그에게 파괴되지 않았다면 돌아오겠지요.<end>"

## 1330257
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ともかく、<line-break>今は猫屋敷に戻りましょう。<end>"
EN: "In any case, let us return to Cat Manor for now.<end>"
KO: "아무튼 지금은 고양이 저택으로 돌아가죠.<end>"

## 1330258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーあ、らしくないよねー。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>このカルラさんが、ここまで<line-break>コテンパンにやられちゃう<line-break>なんてさー。<end>"
EN: "Oh, this is so unlike me. Who'd have thought the great Karula could get beaten this badly?<end>"
KO: "아아, 나답지 않네. 이 칼라 님이 이렇게 철저하게 당해 버리다니.<end>"

## 1330259
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "全軍、ハンカチ噛みつつ、退却ー！<end>"
EN: "All forces, bite your handkerchiefs and retreat!<end>"
KO: "전군, 손수건을 깨물며 후퇴~!<end>"

## 1330260
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ！<end>"
EN: "Yes!<end>"
KO: "핫!<end>"

## 1330261
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょい待ち。<line-break>あんた、撤退に不服そうな顔ね。<end>"
EN: "Hold it. You don't look too happy about retreating.<end>"
KO: "잠깐. 너, 후퇴하는 게 불만인 얼굴이네.<end>"

## 1330262
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ、その…。<end>"
EN: "No, it's just...<end>"
KO: "아니, 그게….<end>"

## 1330263
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いい？　人には生きどころと<line-break>死にどころがあんのよ。<line-break> <line-break>死にどころでちゃんと死ぬためには<line-break>生きどころで逃げる勇気も必要なわけ。<end>"
EN: "Listen. There's a time to live and a time to die. If you want to die properly when your time comes, you need the courage to run when it's time to live.<end>"
KO: "잘 들어. 사람에겐 살아야 할 때와 죽어야 할 때가 있어. 죽어야 할 때 제대로 죽으려면 살아야 할 때 도망칠 용기도 필요한 거야.<end>"

## 1330264
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたの死にどころ、<line-break>ここじゃないっしょ？<end>"
EN: "And this isn't your time to die, is it?<end>"
KO: "네가 죽을 곳은 여기가 아니잖아?<end>"

## 1330265
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ぎょ、御意っ！<end>"
EN: "Y-yes, my lady!<end>"
KO: "…어, 어명 받들겠습니다!<end>"

## 1330266
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ様！<end>"
EN: "Lady Karula!<end>"
KO: "카를라 님!<end>"

## 1330267
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "状況は？<end>"
EN: "What's the situation?<end>"
KO: "상황은?<end>"

## 1330268
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔物が至る所を徘徊し、<line-break>城へは、たどり着くことすら<line-break>できません。<end>"
EN: "Monsters are roaming everywhere. We cannot even reach the castle.<end>"
KO: "마물이 곳곳을 배회해 성까지 도달하는 것조차 불가능합니다.<end>"

## 1330269
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのシャリって坊や、ズルいよねー。<line-break>そりゃ、あんだけの数の魔物を<line-break>一度に召喚すりゃ、負けないって。<line-break>しかも邪竜まで呼ぶ、フツウ？<line-break>なんとも大人げないっつーか、<line-break>完全にガキっつーか。<end>"
EN: "That Shalli boy doesn't play fair, does he? Summon that many monsters at once and of course you won't lose. And who calls up an Evil Dragon on top of that? Honestly, I can't decide whether he's a bad sport or just a complete brat.<end>"
KO: "그 샤리라는 꼬맹이, 정말 치사하지. 그 많은 마물을 한꺼번에 소환하면 지기 어렵지. 게다가 사룡까지 부르는 게 보통이야? 어른답지 못하다기보다 완전히 애라니까.<end>"

## 1330270
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "認めたくありませんが、<line-break>我々では…<line-break>まったく歯が立ちません…！<end>"
EN: "I hate to admit it, but we are completely outmatched!<end>"
KO: "인정하고 싶지 않지만 저희로는… 전혀 상대가 되지 않습니다…!<end>"

## 1330271
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔王バロルの時と同じなのね。<line-break>必要なのは、軍隊なんかじゃなく、<line-break>勇者ってこと。<line-break>魔王バロルを倒せたのは、<line-break>ネメア様とレーグ、オルファウス、<line-break>ケリュネイアという勇者たちだった。<end>"
EN: "It's just like the time of Balor, the Demon King. What we need isn't an army, but heroes. It was the heroes Lord Nemea, Reig, Orphaus, and Ceryneia who defeated him.<end>"
KO: "마왕 발로르 때와 똑같네. 필요한 건 군대가 아니라 용사라는 거야. 마왕 발로르를 쓰러뜨린 것도 네메아 님과 레그, 오르파우스, 케류네이아라는 용사들이었지.<end>"

## 1330272
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、ネメア様のいない今、<line-break>いったい誰が、その役割を<line-break>成しえるのだろうか？<line-break>…なーんて言ってる場合じゃ<line-break>ないんだけどねえ。<end>"
EN: "But with Lord Nemea not here, who could possibly take on that role? Not that this is the time to stand around wondering.<end>"
KO: "하지만 네메아 님이 없는 지금, 대체 누가 그 역할을 해낼 수 있을까? …이런 말이나 하고 있을 때는 아니지만.<end>"

## 1330273
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "発見！　その手があったね！<end>"
EN: "Found one! That's our answer!<end>"
KO: "찾았다! 그 수가 있었네!<end>"

## 1330274
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャント獲りに<line-break>軍をここまで進めてきたんだけど、<line-break>これが、ぜんぜんダメダメ。<end>"
EN: "I marched this army all the way here to take Ancient, but it's been a total disaster.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트를 차지하려고 군을 여기까지 진군시켰는데 이게 완전히 엉망이야.<end>"

## 1330275
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリって魔道士が<line-break>ガンガンに魔物呼び出すんだもん、<line-break>尻尾まいて逃げようってとこよ。<end>"
EN: "That mage Shalli keeps summoning monsters left and right, so we're about to tuck tail and run.<end>"
KO: "샤리라는 마도사가 몬스터를 마구 불러내잖아. 꼬리 말고 도망치려는 참이야.<end>"

## 1330276
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この中に突入する気なんだ。<line-break>奴らと戦うために？<line-break> <line-break>たぶん、犬死ぬよ？<line-break>で、なおかつ、あんたにとって<line-break>たいした見返りもないと思うよ？<end>"
EN: "You're planning to go in there and fight them? You'll probably die for nothing, and I doubt there's much in it for you, either.<end>"
KO: "이 안으로 뛰어들 생각이야? 녀석들과 싸우려고? 아마 개죽음일걸? 게다가 당신한테 큰 보상도 없을 것 같은데?<end>"

## 1330277
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぅーん。<end>"
EN: "Hmm.<end>"
KO: "흐~응.<end>"

## 1330278
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、どのみち軍隊じゃ、<line-break>かないっこないし。<line-break>ほっとくとヤバそうな雰囲気だし。<line-break>未来…よろしくね。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Well, an army doesn't stand a chance anyway, and leaving this alone feels like a very bad idea. So... the future's in your hands, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 어차피 군대로는 상대도 안 되고. 내버려 두면 위험할 것 같고. 미래를 부탁해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1330279
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "遺憾ながら、<line-break>エンシャントを放棄する。<line-break>退却の準備に入れ。<end>"
EN: "Regrettably, we must abandon Ancient. Prepare to withdraw.<end>"
KO: "유감이지만 엔샨트를 포기한다. 퇴각 준비에 들어가라.<end>"

## 1330280
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ！<end>"
EN: "Yes!<end>"
KO: "핫!<end>"

## 1330281
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセンの青竜軍へも<line-break>伝令を走らせろ。<end>"
EN: "Send a messenger to Rocen's Azure Dragon Army as well.<end>"
KO: "로센의 청룡군에도 전령을 보내라.<end>"

## 1330282
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ！<end>"
EN: "Yes!<end>"
KO: "핫!<end>"

## 1330283
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ！　退けい、退くのだ！<end>"
EN: "Damn it! Fall back! Retreat!<end>"
KO: "큭! 물러나라, 물러나!<end>"

## 1330284
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "市内の状況はどうなっている。<end>"
EN: "What is the situation in the city?<end>"
KO: "시내 상황은 어떻게 되어 있지.<end>"

## 1330285
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "完全に制圧されました！<line-break>魔物が至る所を徘徊し、<line-break>城へは、たどり着くことすらできません。<end>"
EN: "It has been completely overrun! Monsters are roaming everywhere, and we cannot even reach the castle.<end>"
KO: "완전히 제압당했습니다! 마물이 곳곳을 돌아다녀 성에는 도달조차 할 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 1330286
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ！　あのシャリという奴、<line-break>いったい何者なのだ！<line-break> <line-break>あれだけの数の魔物を…<line-break>魔物どころか邪竜までもを<line-break>召喚してしまうとは！<end>"
EN: "Damn it! Who in the world is this Shalli? To think he could summon that many monsters... even an Evil Dragon!<end>"
KO: "큭! 그 샤리라는 놈, 대체 정체가 뭐지! 저 많은 마물을… 마물도 모자라 사룡까지 소환하다니!<end>"

## 1330287
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "認めたくありませんが、<line-break>我々では…<line-break>歯が立ちません…！<end>"
EN: "I hate to admit it, but we are outmatched!<end>"
KO: "인정하고 싶진 않지만 저희로서는… 상대가 안 됩니다…!<end>"

## 1330288
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの時と同じだ…。<line-break>必要なのは、軍隊ではなく、<line-break>勇者だということなのか…。<line-break>魔王バロルを倒せたのは、<line-break>ネメア様とレーグ、オルファウス、<line-break>ケリュネイアという勇者たちだった。<end>"
EN: "It is just like before. Does this mean what we need is not an army, but heroes? It was the heroes Lord Nemea, Reig, Orphaus, and Ceryneia who defeated Balor, the Demon King.<end>"
KO: "그때와 똑같아…. 필요한 건 군대가 아니라 용사라는 건가…. 마왕 발로르를 쓰러뜨린 건 네메아 님과 레그, 오르파우스, 케류네이아라는 용사들이었지.<end>"

## 1330289
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、ネメア様ご不在の今、<line-break>いったい誰が、その役割を<line-break>成しえるというんだ…？<end>"
EN: "But with Lord Nemea absent, who could possibly fulfill that role?<end>"
KO: "하지만 네메아 님이 안 계신 지금, 대체 누가 그 역할을 해낼 수 있단 말인가…?<end>"

## 1330290
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><greater-equal>50あ、あなたは…<line-break><value:$28>殿！<end>き、貴公は…<line-break><value:$2B>の<line-break><value:$28>殿！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><greater-equal>50Y-you are... Lord <value:$28>!<end>Y-you are... Lord <value:$28> of <value:$2B>!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><greater-equal>50아, 당신은…<value:$28> 님!<end>귀, 귀공은…<value:$2B>의<value:$28> 님!<end>"

## 1330291
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お初にお目にかかります！<line-break>本職は玄武軍第１１守備軍副官、<line-break>ガラーナ・イガヴィル！　<line-break>お聞きください、<line-break><value:$28>殿！<end>"
EN: "It is an honor to meet you! I am Garana Igavir, adjutant of the Black Tortoise Army's Eleventh Garrison! Please hear me out, Lord <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "처음 뵙겠습니다! 본직은 현무군 제11수비군 부관, 가라나 이가빌! 들어 주십시오, <value:$28>님!<end>"

## 1330292
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "現在、玄武、白虎、朱雀の３軍は、<line-break>本職の指揮のもとでエンシャント<line-break>奪還作戦を展開中なのですが…<line-break>あのシャリと名乗る魔道士に阻まれ、<line-break>断腸の思いで転進作戦に<line-break>移るところなのです。<end>"
EN: "The Black Tortoise, White Tiger, and Vermilion Bird Armies are currently conducting an operation under my command to retake Ancient. But the mage calling himself Shalli has stopped us, and we are about to commence a tactical withdrawal―with the deepest reluctance.<end>"
KO: "현재 현무·백호·주작 세 군은 각 지휘관의 지휘 아래 엔샨트 탈환 작전을 전개 중입니다만… 샤리라는 마도사에게 막혀 피눈물을 머금고 전진 방향을 바꾸려는 참입니다.<end>"

## 1330293
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "や、奴らと戦うつもりなのですか！？<line-break>危険です！<line-break>城内は魔物の巣窟となっており…<end>"
EN: "Y-you intend to fight them? It is too dangerous! The castle is a den of monsters...<end>"
KO: "그, 그들과 싸울 생각입니까!? 위험합니다! 성 안은 마물의 소굴이 되어 있고…<end>"

## 1330294
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "た、確かに我々では…<line-break>しかし…<end>"
EN: "I-it is true that we cannot prevail, but...<end>"
KO: "화, 확실히 우리로서는… 하지만…<end>"

## 1330295
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…了解しました！<line-break>では我々は魔物をエンシャントから<line-break>出さないようにこの地を死守します。<line-break>未来は、あなたに託します。<line-break>御武運を、<value:$28>殿！<end>"
EN: "Understood! Then we will hold this ground to keep the monsters from leaving Ancient. We entrust the future to you. May fortune favor you, Lord <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "…알겠습니다! 그럼 우리는 마물이 엔샨트 밖으로 나가지 못하게 이곳을 사수하겠습니다. 미래는 당신께 맡깁니다. 무운을, <value:$28> 님!<end>"

## 1330296
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントから撤退する。<line-break>すぐに兵を集結させ、<line-break>退却の準備に入れ。<end>"
EN: "We are withdrawing from Ancient. Rally the troops at once and prepare to retreat.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트에서 철수한다. 즉시 병력을 집결시키고 퇴각 준비에 들어가라.<end>"

## 1330297
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ！<end>"
EN: "Yes!<end>"
KO: "핫!<end>"

## 1330298
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセンの青竜軍へ<line-break>伝令を走らせろ。<line-break> <line-break>エンシャントは陥落、<line-break>撤退を開始するとな。<end>"
EN: "Send a messenger to Rocen's Azure Dragon Army. Tell them Ancient has fallen and that we are beginning our withdrawal.<end>"
KO: "로센의 청룡군에 전령을 보내라. 엔샨트는 함락됐다. 철수를 시작한다고 전해.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml

EN: "Yes!<end>"

## 1330301
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "市内の状況はどうなっている。<end>"
EN: "What is the situation in the city?<end>"
KO: "시내 상황은 어떻게 되어 있지.<end>"

## 1330302
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "完全に制圧されました！<line-break>中心地から西側にかけては、<line-break>壊滅状態です。<line-break>魔物が至る所を徘徊し、<line-break>城へは、たどり着くことすら<line-break>できません。<end>"
EN: "It has been completely overrun! Everything from the city center westward has been devastated. Monsters are roaming everywhere, and we cannot even reach the castle.<end>"
KO: "완전히 제압당했습니다! 중심지에서 서쪽까지는 괴멸 상태입니다. 마물이 곳곳을 배회해 성에는 도달조차 할 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 1330303
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリめ…いったい何者だ！<end>"
EN: "Curse Shalli... What in the world is he?<end>"
KO: "샤리 녀석… 대체 누구지!<end>"

## 1330304
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔物の兵団を一瞬にして誕生させ、<line-break>あまつさえ、召喚に莫大な魔力を<line-break>要する邪竜をも襲来させるとは…<line-break>魔人級、いや、<line-break>魔人をもはるかに超えている！<end>"
EN: "He created an army of monsters in an instant, then even brought forth an Evil Dragon, whose summoning demands an immense amount of magical power. He is on the level of a demon―no, far beyond one!<end>"
KO: "마물 군단을 순식간에 만들어 내고, 엄청난 마력이 필요한 사룡까지 소환하다니… 마인급, 아니 마인조차 훨씬 뛰어넘는다!<end>"

## 1330305
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "認めたくありませんが、<line-break>我々では…<line-break>全く歯が立ちません…！<end>"

KO: "인정하고 싶지 않지만, 우리로서는… 전혀 상대가 되지 않습니다…!<end>"

## 1330307

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1330309
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "驚いただろう？<line-break>栄華を誇った帝都エンシャントが、<line-break>一瞬にして失陥し、このザマだ。<line-break>城内の状況はほとんど直視に耐えん。<line-break>すでに死の都だ。<end>"
EN: "A shocking sight, isn't it? Ancient, the Imperial Capital, fell in an instant, and this is what remains. What has happened inside the castle is almost too horrible to behold. It is already a city of the dead.<end>"
KO: "놀랐겠지? 영화를 누리던 제도 엔샨트가 한순간에 함락돼 이 꼴이 됐다. 성 안 상황은 차마 눈 뜨고 보기 힘들어. 이미 죽음의 도시다.<end>"

## 1330310
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだと…？<line-break>まさか、乗り込むつもりか？<line-break>奴らと戦うというのか！？<end>"
EN: "What? You cannot mean to go in there and fight them!<end>"
KO: "뭐라고…? 설마, 쳐들어갈 생각이냐? 놈들과 싸우겠다는 거냐!?<end>"

## 1330311
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、私たちとて全力を<line-break>尽くしてあらがったのだぞ？<line-break> <line-break>それすらシャリにはまったく<line-break>通用しなかったというのに、<line-break>そこへ、君たち数人が乗り込んで…<end>"
EN: "But we resisted with everything we had. Nothing we did had the slightest effect on Shalli, and now just a handful of you mean to go in there...<end>"
KO: "하지만 우리도 온 힘을 다해 저항했어. 그런데도 샤리에게는 전혀 통하지 않았는데, 거기에 너희 몇 명이 쳐들어가서…<end>"

## 1330312
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>そうか…！<line-break>なるほど、<value:$28>、<line-break>君が…！<end>"
EN: "...Of course. Now I understand, <value:$28>. It is you...!<end>"
KO: "………………. 그런가…! 그렇군, <value:$28>, 네가…!<end>"

## 1330313
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どのみち、軍隊では勝ち目はない。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>我々は魔物をエンシャントから<line-break>出さないように防衛に徹する。<end>"
EN: "An army has no hope of victory in any case. We will devote ourselves to containing the monsters within Ancient.<end>"
KO: "어차피 군대로는 승산이 없어. 우리는 마물이 엔샨트 밖으로 나오지 못하도록 방어에 전념한다.<end>"

## 1330314
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未来は、君に託そう。<end>"
EN: "I entrust the future to you.<end>"
KO: "미래는 네게 맡기겠다.<end>"

## 1330315
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "汝ら人類の革新には遠いクズの群も<line-break>ずいぶん魔物に数を減らされたな。<line-break>そろそろエンシャントから撤退しよう。<end>"
EN: "The monsters have considerably thinned the ranks of that human refuse still far from Humanity's Evolution. It is time we withdrew from Ancient.<end>"
KO: "너희 인류 혁신과는 거리가 먼 쓰레기 무리도 마물에게 많이 줄었군. 슬슬 엔샨트에서 철수하자.<end>"

## 1330321
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "完全に制圧されました！<line-break>魔物が至る所を徘徊し、<line-break>城へは、たどり着くことすらできません。<end>"
EN: "It has been completely overrun! Monsters are roaming everywhere, and we cannot even reach the castle.<end>"
KO: "완전히 제압당했습니다! 마물이 곳곳을 돌아다녀 성에는 도달조차 할 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 1330322
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリとやら、ずいぶんガラクタを<line-break>ひっくり返してきたではないか。<line-break>邪竜、魔人…そして闇の巨人も使う気か。<line-break>人の魂を集め、新たなる神を作るか？<line-break>しかし、それは人類の革新ではない。<line-break>残念だが共闘はできぬな…<end>"
EN: "That Shalli has dredged up quite an assortment of rubbish, hasn't he? An Evil Dragon, a demon... and does he mean to use the Dark Giant as well? Is he gathering human souls to create a new god? But that is not Humanity's Evolution. Regrettably, I cannot join forces with him...<end>"
KO: "샤리라는 자, 꽤나 고물을 뒤집어 꺼내 왔군. 사룡, 마인… 그리고 어둠의 거인까지 쓸 셈인가. 사람의 혼을 모아 새로운 신을 만들겠다고? 하지만 그건 인류의 혁신이 아니다. 유감이지만 함께 싸울 수는 없겠군….<end>"

## 1330323
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は？　宰相、何を…？<end>"
EN: "What? Chancellor, what are you...?<end>"
KO: "예? 재상, 무슨…?<end>"

## 1330324
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "必要なのは<line-break>軍隊という凡愚の群れではない。<line-break>英雄だということだよ。<line-break>魔王バロルを倒せたのは、<line-break>ネメアとレーグ、オルファウス、<line-break>ケリュネイアという勇者たちだった。<end>"
EN: "What is needed is not the witless rabble called an army, but a hero. It was the heroes Nemea, Reig, Orphaus, and Ceryneia who defeated Balor, the Demon King.<end>"
KO: "필요한 건 군대라는 평범한 무리가 아니야. 영웅이지. 마왕 발로르를 쓰러뜨린 건 네메아와 레그, 오르파우스, 케류네이아라는 용사들이었다.<end>"

## 1330325
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかしネメアもしょせんは魔人よ。<line-break>ネメアを超える、人としての存在、<line-break>現れぬものか…？<end>"
EN: "Yet Nemea is merely a demon in the end. Will no one arise who surpasses Nemea while remaining human?<end>"
KO: "하지만 네메아도 결국 마인이야. 네메아를 넘어설 인간으로서의 존재는 나타나지 않는 건가…?<end>"

## 1330328
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガラクタと言えど、神代の存在。<line-break>凡庸な愚か者どもでは、<line-break>エンシャントを守りきれなかったのだ。<line-break>ま、革新ができぬ輩が減ってよかった<end>"
EN: "Rubbish they may be, but they are beings from the Age of the Gods. Those commonplace fools could not defend Ancient against them. Still, it is good that there are fewer people incapable of contributing to Humanity's Evolution.<end>"
KO: "잡동사니라 해도 신대의 존재다. 평범한 어리석은 자들로는 엔샨트를 지키지 못했다. 뭐, 혁신하지 못할 자들이 줄어 다행이군.<end>"

## 1330329
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この地獄と化した城内に乗り込むか？<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>よかろう。<line-break>行け、人類の革新のために。<end>"
EN: "You would enter that castle, now transformed into hell? Very well. Go, for Humanity's Evolution.<end>"
KO: "지옥으로 변한 성 안으로 들어가겠다고? 좋다. 가라, 인류의 혁신을 위해.<end>"

## 1330330
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よい返事だ。<line-break>人類の革新は近いぞ。<line-break>神代の遺物どもを滅ぼすのだ。<end>"
EN: "A fine answer. Humanity's Evolution draws near. Destroy those relics of the Age of the Gods.<end>"
KO: "좋은 대답이다. 인류의 혁신은 가깝다. 신대의 유물들을 멸하는 거다.<end>"

## 1330331
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか！　なんという好機だ！<line-break>この機に人類の革新を妨げる<line-break>神も魔人も滅ぼし尽くせばよいのだ！<end>"
EN: "I see! What an extraordinary opportunity! Now we can annihilate every god and demon that stands in the way of Humanity's Evolution!<end>"
KO: "그렇군! 이 얼마나 좋은 기회인가! 이 기회에 인류의 혁신을 방해하는 신도 마인도 모조리 없애 버리면 된다!<end>"

## 1330332
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行け、<value:$28><line-break>神々の黄昏の果てに。<line-break> <line-break>人類の革新の夜明けを開くために！<end>"
EN: "Go, <value:$28>, beyond the twilight of the gods. Bring forth the dawn of Humanity's Evolution!<end>"
KO: "가라, <value:$28>. 신들의 황혼 끝으로. 인류 혁신의 새벽을 열기 위해!<end>"

## 1330333
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "頼むぞ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "부탁한다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1330334
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "傷ついたら戻るがいい。<end>"
EN: "If you are wounded, come back.<end>"
KO: "다치면 돌아오거라.<end>"

## 1330335
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>よ、<line-break>余のかわいい人類の革新の鉄槌よ。<line-break>回復してやろう。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, my beloved hammer of Humanity's Evolution. I shall heal you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>여, 짐의 귀여운 인류 혁신의 철퇴여. 회복시켜 주마.<end>"

## 1330336
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "天空神に属するものも、<line-break>破壊神に属するものも、<line-break>ひとしく滅ぼし尽くせ。<line-break>神話の時代はもはや終わったことを、<line-break>連中に教えてやるのだ。<end>"
EN: "Destroy all who serve the Sky God and all who serve the God of Destruction alike. Teach them that the age of myth is over.<end>"
KO: "천공신에 속한 자도 파괴신에 속한 자도 모두 똑같이 멸하라. 신화의 시대는 이미 끝났다는 걸 놈들에게 알려 줘라.<end>"

## 1330337
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んじゃ、あとは任せたかんね。<line-break>ほい、回復。<end>"
EN: "All right, I'll leave the rest to you. There, all healed up.<end>"
KO: "그럼 나머지는 맡길게. 자, 회복.<end>"

## 1330338
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんくらい、お安い御用ってねー。<line-break>次帰ってくるまで、<line-break>気ぃ抜かないよーに！<end>"
EN: "That was nothing. Don't let your guard down before you make it back!<end>"
KO: "이 정도야 식은 죽 먹기지. 다음에 돌아올 때까지 방심하지 말라고!<end>"

## 1330339
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悔しいことですが、我々では、<line-break>奇跡の名将ゼネテスと同じようには<line-break>いかないようです。<end>"
EN: "Regrettably, it seems we cannot hope to equal Xenetes, that miracle of a commander.<end>"
KO: "분하지만 우리로서는 기적의 명장 제네테스처럼 해낼 수 없는 모양입니다.<end>"

## 1330340
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、微々たることでもいい、<line-break>何とか勇者殿のお役には立ちたい。<line-break>…回復して差し上げましょう。<end>"
EN: "Still, however little it may be, I want to be of some use to you, Hero. Allow me to heal you.<end>"
KO: "하지만 아주 조금이라도 좋으니 어떻게든 용사님께 도움이 되고 싶습니다. …회복시켜 드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 1330341
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これでも本職は魔道アカデミーの<line-break>卒業者です。傷ついたら、<line-break>いつでも戻ってきてください。<line-break>御武運を！<end>"
EN: "Believe it or not, I am a graduate of the Magic Academy. If you are wounded, come back at any time. May fortune favor you in battle!<end>"
KO: "이래 봬도 본업은 마도 아카데미 졸업자입니다. 다치면 언제든 돌아오세요. 무운을 빕니다!<end>"

## 1330342
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　愚かなり、ナーシェス…　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　この竜王が与えし、<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者を<line-break>　　　　　　使いこなせぬとは…<end>"
EN: "How foolish, Narscess... To think you could not make use of the Bearer of the Infinite Soul granted by this Dragon King...<end>"
KO: "어리석구나, 나셰스…. 이 용왕이 내려 준 무한의 소울을 가진 자를 제대로 다루지 못하다니….<end>"

## 1330343
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>殿、<line-break>ここはお任せください！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, please leave this to me!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 여기는 맡겨 주십시오!<end>"

## 1330344
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よろしくお願いします。<line-break><value:$28>殿！<line-break>ここはお任せください。<end>"
EN: "I am at your service, <value:$28>! Leave this position to me.<end>"
KO: "잘 부탁드립니다, <value:$28>님! 여기는 맡겨 주십시오.<end>"

## 1330345
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（もう後戻りするわけにはいかない…。）<end>"
EN: "(There's no turning back now...)<end>"
KO: "(이제는 되돌아갈 수 없어….)<end>"

## 1330346
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>の目…。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>ロセン軍に攻め込まれたあたしの村を、<line-break>救いに現れた、あの時の<line-break>ネメア様の目にそっくりだった…。<end>"
EN: "The look in <value:$28>'s eyes... It was just like Lord Nemea's that day, when he came to save my village from the invading Rocen army...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>의 눈…. 로센군이 쳐들어온 내 마을을 구하러 나타났던 그때의 네메아 님 눈과 똑같았어….<end>"

## 1330347
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何千というロセン兵を、<line-break>たった２８騎で迎え討とうっていう、<line-break> <line-break>何のてらいも、曇りもない、<line-break>あの時のネメア様の目に…。<end>"
EN: "Just like Lord Nemea's eyes that day, utterly free of pretense or doubt, as he prepared to face thousands of Rocen soldiers with only twenty-eight riders...<end>"
KO: "수천의 로센 병사를 단 28기로 맞아 싸우려 했던, 아무 꾸밈도 흐림도 없던 그때의 네메아 님의 눈에….<end>"

## 1330348
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "新たなる英雄伝説誕生の瞬間か！<line-break><value:$28>殿！<line-break>及ばずながら、本職も手助けいたします！<end>"
EN: "Are we witnessing the birth of a new heroic legend? <value:$28>! My aid may be modest, but you shall have it!<end>"
KO: "새로운 영웅전설이 탄생하는 순간인가! <value:$28>님! 미력하나마 저도 돕겠습니다!<end>"

## 1330349
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "全軍、<value:$28>殿のために<line-break>ここを死守するのだ！<line-break> <line-break>魔物一匹たりとて門外に出すなよ！<end>"
EN: "All forces, hold this ground for <value:$28> at any cost! Do not let a single monster beyond the gate!<end>"
KO: "전군, <value:$28>님을 위해 이곳을 사수하라! 마물 한 마리도 문밖으로 내보내지 마라!<end>"

## 1330350
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア様に代わる勇者か…。<line-break>人類革新の鍵も、引き継がれていく<line-break>ものなのかも知れないな…。<end>"
EN: "A Hero to take Lord Nemea's place... Perhaps the key to Humanity's Evolution can be passed on as well...<end>"
KO: "네메아 님을 대신할 용사인가…. 인류 혁신의 열쇠도 그렇게 이어져 가는 걸지도 모르겠군….<end>"

## 1330351
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…余は確信したぞ。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>人類の革新は、<line-break>今日、この時より始まる。<end>"
EN: "I am certain now. Humanity's Evolution begins today, at this very moment.<end>"
KO: "…짐은 확신했다. 인류의 혁신은 오늘, 지금 이 순간부터 시작된다.<end>"

## 1330352
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フォフォフォ…。<end>"
EN: "Hoh hoh hoh...<end>"
KO: "호호호….<end>"

## 1330353
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この死都に戻ってくるとはの…。<line-break>やはり、無限のソウルを持つとも、<line-break>運命の波には逆らえぬようじゃの。<end>"
EN: "So you have returned to this city of the dead... It seems even the bearer of the Infinite Soul cannot resist the tides of fate.<end>"
KO: "이 죽은 도시에 다시 돌아오다니…. 역시 무한의 소울을 지녔어도 운명의 파도에는 거스를 수 없는 모양이구나.<end>"

## 1330354
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここを通りたい？<line-break>冗談じゃ…<line-break>………………。 <line-break>本気なのですね。<line-break>わかりました。こちらの抜け道から坑道に<line-break>入れます。ついてきてください。<end>"
EN: "You want to get through here? Don't be ridiculous... You're serious, aren't you? All right. This hidden passage leads into the mine tunnels. Follow me.<end>"
KO: "여길 지나가고 싶다고? 농담도…………………. 진심이군요. 알겠습니다. 이 샛길로 갱도에들어갈 수 있습니다. 따라오세요.<end>"

## 1330355
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな醜い世界…<line-break>見なければ良かった…。<line-break>知らなければ良かった…。<line-break>全てを無に還すの。<end>"
EN: "This hideous world... I wish I'd never seen it. I wish I'd never known. I'll return it all to nothingness.<end>"
KO: "이런 추악한 세상… 보지 말았어야 했어…. 몰랐어야 했어…. 모든 것을 무로 돌릴 거야.<end>"

## 1330356
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…愚かな。<line-break>己の可能性を信じず、<line-break>闇にその身をゆだねたか…。<end>"
EN: "Foolish... You had no faith in your own potential, so you surrendered yourself to darkness...<end>"
KO: "…어리석군. 자신의 가능성을 믿지 않고 어둠에 몸을 맡겼나….<end>"

## 1330357
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人は運命にあらがえないのよ。<line-break>ベルゼーヴァ…。<end>"
EN: "No one can resist fate, Beelzeva...<end>"
KO: "사람은 운명에 거스를 수 없어. 벨제바….<end>"

## 1330358
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうじゃ。<line-break>お前たちがここに戻ってきた<line-break>ことすら運命なのじゃよ。<end>"
EN: "Precisely. Even your return here was ordained by fate.<end>"
KO: "그렇다. 너희가 이곳으로 돌아온 것조차 운명이란다.<end>"

## 1330359
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはどうかな？<line-break>私は運命を切り開くために<line-break>戻ってきた。<line-break>いくぞ！　<value:$28>！<line-break>闇を払い、<line-break>人類に革新の光を示すのだ！<end>"
EN: "Is that so? I returned to forge a path beyond fate. Come, <value:$28>! We shall drive back the darkness and show humanity the light of Humanity's Evolution!<end>"
KO: "그건 어떨까? 나는 운명을 개척하기 위해 돌아왔다. 가자, <value:$28>! 어둠을 걷어 내고 인류에게 혁신의 빛을 보여 주자!<end>"

## 1330360
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…見えなければ、よかった。<end>"
EN: "I wish I'd never been able to see.<end>"
KO: "…보이지 않았다면 좋았을 텐데.<end>"

## 1330361
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇にいるままでよかった。<line-break>闇にいた方がよかった。<line-break>何も見えないままならよかった。<line-break>なぜ、このつらい現実に光を当てた。<line-break>ずっとこんなもの見たくなかった。<line-break>苦しまずにすんだのに…。<end>"
EN: "I should have stayed in the darkness. I was better off there. I should never have seen anything. Why did you cast light on this painful reality? I never wanted to see any of it. Then I wouldn't have had to suffer...<end>"
KO: "어둠 속에 그대로 있었으면 좋았어. 어둠 속에 있는 편이 나았어. 아무것도 보이지 않았으면 좋았어. 왜 이 괴로운 현실에 빛을 비춘 거야. 계속 이런 건 보고 싶지 않았어. 괴롭지 않아도 됐는데….<end>"

## 1330362
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生まれなければよかった。<line-break>苦しまずにすんだのに！<end>"
EN: "I wish I'd never been born. Then I wouldn't have had to suffer!<end>"
KO: "태어나지 않았으면 좋았을 텐데. 그랬으면 괴롭지도 않았을 텐데!<end>"

## 1330363
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この世界の悲しみが<line-break>かなえられない願いが<line-break>どうしようもない思いが<line-break>闇の王女の暗き心に共鳴して<line-break>闇の巨人として<line-break>世界中で立ち上がる…。<end>"
EN: "The sorrow of this world, its unfulfilled wishes, its hopeless yearnings―they resonate with the dark heart of the Princess of Darkness and rise throughout the world as Giants of Darkness...<end>"
KO: "이 세계의 슬픔이, 이루어지지 않는 소원이, 어찌할 수 없는 마음이 어둠의 왕녀의 어두운 마음에 공명해 어둠의 거인으로서 세계 곳곳에 일어선다…<end>"

## 1330364
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーあ、かわいそうに。<line-break>生まれたてだったのに。<line-break>ひどいことするなぁ。<end>"
EN: "Aw, poor thing. It had only just been born. That was cruel.<end>"
KO: "아아, 불쌍해라. 태어난 지 얼마 되지도 않았는데. 너무하잖아.<end>"

## 1330365
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、いっか。<line-break>まだまだたくさん<line-break>産まれたからね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, well. Plenty more have been born.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 됐나. 아직 많이 태어났으니까.<end>"

## 1330366
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あらがっても無駄。<line-break>私の可愛い子たちが…<line-break>必ず、あなたたちに絶望を…<end>"
EN: "Struggling is useless. My precious children will be sure to plunge you into despair...<end>"
KO: "저항해도 소용없어. 내 귀여운 아이들이… 반드시 너희에게 절망을…<end>"

## 1330367
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…シャリ、やめろ。<line-break>やめてくれ…<line-break>これでは世界が…滅ぶ…。<end>"
EN: "Shalli, stop. Please... At this rate, the world will be destroyed...<end>"
KO: "…샤리, 그만해. 그만둬 줘… 이러다 세계가… 멸망해….<end>"

## 1330368
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ、何言ってんの？<line-break>自分で守れないものを<line-break>壊すのをとめてくれなんて。<line-break>君にはなんの手もないんだ。<line-break>世界も、<value:$28>も守れない。<line-break>だったら、黙って見ててよ。アハハ！<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, what are you talking about? You're asking me not to destroy something you can't protect yourself. There's nothing you can do. You can't protect the world or <value:$28>. So just shut up and watch. Ahaha!<end>"
KO: "후후, 무슨 소리야? 스스로 지키지도 못하는 걸 부수지 말아 달라니. 너에겐 아무 수단도 없어. 세계도, <value:$28>도 지키지 못해. 그러니 입 다물고 보고 있어. 아하하!<end>"

## 1330369
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめるんだ、シャリ。<line-break>できれば、お前を討ちたくはない…。<end>"
EN: "Stop this, Shalli. I'd rather not have to strike you down...<end>"
KO: "그만둬, 샤리. 가능하면 너를 쓰러뜨리고 싶지는 않아….<end>"

## 1330370
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界の人たちよ。<line-break>聞こえると思う。<line-break>我が言葉を聞くのだ…。<line-break>滅びの時が来た…。<line-break>この恐怖をぬぐうのは<line-break>救世主たる我より他にない…<end>"
EN: "People of the world, I know you can hear me. Hear my words... The hour of destruction has come. None but I, your Savior, can free you from this terror...<end>"
KO: "세계의 사람들이여. 들릴 것이다. 내 말을 들어라…. 멸망의 때가 왔다…. 이 공포를 씻어낼 자는 구세주인 나밖에 없다….<end>"

## 1330371
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くだらない。<line-break>アトレイア、闇の巨人で<line-break>やっちゃってよ。<end>"
EN: "What nonsense. Atleia, have the Giants of Darkness take care of it.<end>"
KO: "시시하네. 아트레이아, 어둠의 거인으로 해치워 버려.<end>"

## 1330372
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我を信じよ…<line-break>すべてを我になげうて…<line-break>すべてをゆだねよ…<line-break>祈るがいい…<line-break>そして心を開くのだ…<line-break>すべてをゆだねよ…<end>"
EN: "Believe in me... Sacrifice everything to me... Entrust everything to me... Pray... Open your hearts... Entrust everything to me...<end>"
KO: "나를 믿어라… 모든 것을 내게 바쳐라… 모두 맡겨라… 기도하라… 그리고 마음을 열어라… 모두 맡겨라….<end>"

## 1330373
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフアハハ…！<line-break>かわいいこと言うね、エルファス。<line-break>そういう甘ちゃんなところは大好きさ。<line-break>でも、君はもう舞台を降りちゃったし、<line-break>ちょっと惜しいけど、<line-break>代役を立てさせてもらうよ。<line-break>破壊の化身、魔王バロルをね。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, ahaha! How sweet of you, Elfas. I do love that softhearted side of you. But you've already left the stage, so regrettable though it is, I'll bring in your understudy: Balor, Demon King, the Embodiment of Destruction.<end>"
KO: "우후후 아하하…! 귀여운 말을 하는구나, 엘파스. 그런 순진한 점은 정말 좋아해. 하지만 넌 이미 무대에서 내려갔으니 조금 아쉽지만 대역을 세워야겠어. 파괴의 화신, 마왕 발로르를 말이야.<end>"

## 1330374
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフアハハ…！<line-break>君が僕を裏切ってもね<line-break>僕は信じていたよ、エルファス。<line-break>君が愛を信じる負け犬になれば<line-break>必ず、<value:$28>を守るため<line-break>神になると…ね。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, ahaha! Even after you betrayed me, I still believed in you, Elfas. I knew that once you became a pathetic loser who believed in love, you'd become a god to protect <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "우후후아하하…! 네가 날 배신해도 난 믿고 있었어, 엘파스. 네가 사랑을 믿는 패배자가 되면 반드시 <value:$28>를 지키기 위해 신이 될 거라고… 말이야.<end>"

## 1330375
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうだい。<line-break>これが僕の新しいオモチャ。<line-break>新たなる神だよ。<end>"
EN: "What do you think? This is my new toy―a new god.<end>"
KO: "어때? 이게 내 새 장난감. 새로운 신이야.<end>"

## 1330376
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "驚いたかい？<line-break>強大な闇の巨人が世界中を襲ってるのさ。<line-break>でも、これはほんの序の口<line-break>ここからが本番だ。<end>"
EN: "Surprised? Mighty Giants of Darkness are attacking all over the world. But that's only the beginning. Now the real show starts.<end>"
KO: "놀랐어? 강대한 어둠의 거인들이 온 세상을 습격하고 있지. 하지만 이건 시작일 뿐이야. 이제부터가 진짜다.<end>"

## 1330377
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見るがいい…<line-break>世界中の人のソウルを吸収し…、<line-break>僕は…、神になる…<end>"
EN: "Behold... I shall absorb the Souls of people throughout the world... and become... a god...<end>"
KO: "보아라… 세상 사람들의 소울을 흡수해… 나는… 신이 된다…<end>"

## 1330378
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界の人たちよ。<line-break>聞こえると思う。<line-break>我が言葉を聞くのだ…<line-break>滅びの時が来た…。<line-break>この恐怖をぬぐうのは<line-break>救世主たる我より他にない…<end>"
EN: "People of the world, I know you can hear me. Hear my words... The hour of destruction has come. None but I, your Savior, can free you from this terror...<end>"
KO: "세계의 사람들이여. 들릴 것이다. 내 말을 들어라…. 멸망의 때가 왔다…. 이 공포를 씻어낼 자는 구세주인 나밖에 없다….<end>"

## 1330379
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
