## 1330380
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ…。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee...<end>"
KO: "후후후….<end>"

## 1330381
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうだい。<line-break>これが僕の新しいオモチャ。<line-break>新たなる神だよ。<line-break>ねえ、エルファス、<line-break>神になった気分はどう？<end>"
EN: "What do you think? This is my new toy―a new god. So, Elfas, how does it feel to be a god?<end>"
KO: "어때. 이게 내 새로운 장난감이야. 새로운 신이지. 있잖아, 엘파스. 신이 된 기분은 어때?<end>"

## 1330382
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやおや。いろんな人の精神が<line-break>入り込んできて<line-break>自分を失ってしまったみたいだね。<line-break>ま、君は自分の痛みばっかりに夢中で、<line-break>他人にも痛みが存在するなんて<line-break>気づきもしなかったからねえ…。<end>"
EN: "Well, well. It seems all those other minds flooded in and made you lose yourself. You were always so consumed by your own pain that you never even noticed other people had pain of their own...<end>"
KO: "이런 이런. 여러 사람의 정신이 들어와서 자신을 잃어버린 모양이구나. 뭐, 넌 자기 아픔에만 빠져서 다른 사람에게도 아픔이 있다는 걸 깨닫지도 못했으니까….<end>"

## 1330383
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君みたいな痛みなら強弱の差はあれ、<line-break>誰でも持ってるものなんだよ。<line-break>いい勉強になった？　アハハ…！<line-break>でも困ったな。精神が無くて<line-break>判断ができないんじゃ粗悪品だよ。<line-break>ちゃんと動くのかな…。<end>"
EN: "Everyone carries pain like yours, even if some feel it more keenly than others. Did you learn something? Ahaha! But this is a problem. A product with no mind to make decisions is defective. I wonder if it will even work...<end>"
KO: "너 같은 아픔이라면 정도의 차이는 있어도 누구나 가지고 있어. 좋은 공부가 됐어? 아하하…! 하지만 곤란하네. 정신이 없어서 판단도 못 하면 불량품이잖아. 제대로 움직이려나….<end>"

## 1330384
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁて、邪眼帝と呼ばれたバロルの<line-break>お手並み拝見といこうかな。<end>"
EN: "Now then, let's see what Balor, the Evil-Eyed Emperor, can do.<end>"
KO: "자, 사안제라 불리던 발로르의 솜씨를 구경해 볼까.<end>"

## 1330385
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ…アハハ！　すごい！<line-break>予想以上だね、バロルの力は！<end>"
EN: "Hee hee... Ahaha! Amazing! Balor's power exceeds even my expectations!<end>"
KO: "우후후… 아하하! 대단해! 예상 이상이야, 발로르의 힘은!<end>"

## 1330386
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私に与えられた以上の闇と絶望を。<line-break>世界を虚無に落としこみ、<line-break>永遠の静寂を。<end>"
EN: "Darkness and despair greater than what was given to me. Cast the world into the Void, and bring eternal silence.<end>"
KO: "…내게 주어진 것 이상의 어둠과 절망을. 세계를 허무로 떨어뜨리고 영원한 정적을.<end>"

## 1330387
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だってさ。困っちゃうよねぇ。<line-break>勇者様、この世界を救って～！<line-break>…って感じかな。アハハ…<line-break>あとは頼んだよ、<line-break>勇者<value:$28>様。<line-break>アハハハ…！<end>"
EN: "You heard her. What a predicament. “Oh, great Hero, save the world!” Something like that, I suppose. Ahaha... I'll leave the rest to you, mighty Hero <value:$28>. Ahahaha!<end>"
KO: "그렇다네. 곤란하지? 용사님, 이 세계를 구해 줘~! …이런 느낌인가. 하하…. 나머지는 부탁할게, 용사 <value:$28>님. 하하하…!<end>"

## 1330388
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは私が光を求めたときにすら、<line-break>冷たくあしらった…。<line-break>世界を滅ぼす前にあなたを殺すわ。<line-break>復讐するの。<end>"
EN: "Even when I sought the light, you turned me away coldly... Before I destroy the world, I'll kill you. I'll have my revenge.<end>"
KO: "당신은 내가 빛을 원했을 때조차 차갑게 대했어…. 세계를 멸망시키기 전에 당신부터 죽일 거야. 복수하겠어.<end>"

## 1330389
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたさえ、私に光をくれなければ、<line-break>こんな事にはならなかった…。<line-break>世界を滅ぼす前にあなたを殺すわ。<line-break>復讐するの。<end>"
EN: "If only you had never given me light, none of this would have happened... Before I destroy the world, I'll kill you. I'll have my revenge.<end>"
KO: "당신이 내게 빛을 주지만 않았어도 이런 일은 없었어…. 세계를 멸망시키기 전에 당신을 죽일 거야. 복수할 거야.<end>"

## 1330390
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な…、何…！？<end>"
EN: "W-What?!<end>"
KO: "뭐…, 뭐야…!?<end>"

## 1330391
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無事か、<line-break><value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "Are you all right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "무사하냐, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1330392
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネ、ネメア…。<line-break>生きていたの…。<line-break> <line-break>ふふふ…でも、同じ事。<line-break>新しき神、エルファスよ、<line-break>闇の者、ネメアを消し去りたまえ！<end>"
EN: "N-Nemea... You were alive... Hee hee... But it makes no difference. Elfas, the new god, erase Nemea, being of darkness, from existence!<end>"
KO: "네, 네메아…. 살아 있었어…. 후후후… 하지만 똑같아. 새로운 신 엘파스여, 어둠의 자 네메아를 없애 주소서!<end>"

## 1330393
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…たとえ闇に落ちようと、<line-break>私は私だ！<end>"
EN: "Even if I fall into darkness, I am still myself!<end>"
KO: "…설령 어둠에 떨어져도 나는 나다!<end>"

## 1330394
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ！<line-break>破滅の槍の力を解放する気？<end>"
EN: "Damn! Are you going to unleash the power of the Spear of Ruin?<end>"
KO: "큭! 파멸의 창의 힘을 해방할 셈이야?<end>"

## 1330395
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…必ず、あなたを殺す。<line-break>それまで、この静かな世界を<line-break>虚無に帰すのは待ってあげるわ。<line-break>あなたは、私と戦わなければならない。<line-break>この静寂から世界を救うためにね。<line-break>お城の謁見の間でお待ちします。ふふ…<end>"
EN: "I will kill you. Until then, I'll refrain from returning this silent world to the Void. You must fight me if you wish to save the world from this silence. I shall await you in the castle's audience chamber. Hee hee...<end>"
KO: "…반드시 당신을 죽이겠어. 그때까지 이 고요한 세계를 허무로 돌리는 건 기다려 줄게. 당신은 나와 싸워야 해. 이 정적에서 세계를 구하기 위해서 말이야. 성의 알현실에서 기다릴게. 후후….<end>"

## 1330396
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "必ず、いらしてください。<line-break>その死に様を見届けてさしあげます。<line-break>フフ…フフフ…アハハ…！<end>"
EN: "Do be sure to come. I shall watch you die. Hee hee... Hee hee hee... Ahaha!<end>"
KO: "꼭 와 주세요. 그 최후를 지켜봐 드리겠습니다. 후후… 후후후… 아하하…!<end>"

## 1330397
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまぬ。<line-break>手間取った。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me. I was delayed.<end>"
KO: "미안하다. 시간이 좀 걸렸다.<end>"

## 1330398
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのおびただしいソウルの流れ<line-break>おそらく、世界中の人間が<line-break>バロルにソウルを奪われた…。<end>"
EN: "That enormous torrent of Souls... Balor must have stolen the Souls of people throughout the world.<end>"
KO: "저 엄청난 소울의 흐름…. 아마 전 세계의 인간들이 발로르에게 소울을 빼앗긴 거야….<end>"

## 1330399
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界のすべてが<line-break>静寂に<line-break>支配されているだろう。<end>"
EN: "Silence must now reign over the entire world.<end>"
KO: "세계의 모든 것이 정적에 지배되어 있겠지.<end>"

## 1330400
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2人間って、弱いものね…。<line-break>いざとなったら、<line-break>すべてを投げ出してしまうなんて…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>3人間とは、弱いものじゃな。<line-break>いざとなったら、誰かにすがって<line-break>すべてを投げ出してしまうんじゃから。<end><if><value:$01><equal>5へっ、人間なんて弱いもんだよな。<line-break>いざとなったら、何もかも投げ出して<line-break>助けて、神様、救世主様～、かよ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>9人間なんて、こんなもんよね。<line-break>いざとなったら、何もかも投げ出して<line-break>助けて、神様、救世主様～、だもんね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>11人間、やはり、弱いものよ<line-break>いざとなったら、自分の力を投げ出して<line-break>神にすがってしまう…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13フッ、人間などボンクラの集まりだな。<line-break>いざとなったら他人にすがって、<line-break>自分はすべてを放棄してしまう。<end>姉ちゃん、やっぱ、人間て弱いよな。<line-break>いざとなったら、神にすがって、<line-break>なにもかも、投げ出しちまうなんてさ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Humans are so weak... When the moment comes, they simply cast everything aside...<end><if><value:$01><equal>3Humans are frail creatures. When the hour of need comes, they cling to someone else and cast everything aside.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Hah! Humans sure are weak. The moment things get bad, they throw everything away and cry, “Save us, God! Save us, Savior!”<end><if><value:$01><equal>9That's humans for you. The moment things get bad, they toss everything aside and start crying, “Save us, God! Save us, Savior!”<end><if><value:$01><equal>11Humans truly are weak. When the moment comes, they surrender their own strength and cling to a god...<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Hmph. Humans are nothing but a pack of fools. When the moment comes, they cling to someone else and abandon everything themselves.<end>Sis, humans really are weak, aren't they? The moment things get bad, they cling to a god and throw everything away.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2인간은 약한 존재구나… 막상 닥치면 모든 걸 내던져 버리다니…<end><if><value:$01><equal>3인간이란 약한 존재로군. 막상 닥치면 누군가에게 매달려 모든 걸 내던져 버리니 말이야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5흥, 인간은 약하네. 막상 닥치면 전부 내던지고 살려 주세요, 신님, 구세주님~ 이거냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>9인간은 결국 이런 거네. 막상 닥치면 전부 내던지고 살려 주세요, 신님, 구세주님~ 이러잖아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11인간은 역시 약하군. 막상 닥치면 자기 힘을 포기하고 신에게 매달려 버려…<end><if><value:$01><equal>13후, 인간 따윈 머저리들의 집합이군. 막상 닥치면 남에게 매달리고 자신은 전부 포기해 버리지.<end>누나, 역시 인간은 약하네. 막상 닥치면 신에게 매달리고 전부 내던져 버리다니.<end>"

## 1330401
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、そんなに悲観したもんじゃないと<line-break>思いましたよ、私は。<end>"
EN: "Well, I didn't think there was any reason to be so pessimistic.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그렇게까지 비관할 일은 아니라고 생각했습니다, 저는.<end>"

## 1330402
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人間は、善きにつけ悪しきにつけ、<line-break>変わってゆくものです。<line-break>　<line-break>人の思いは、やがて世界を<line-break>新たな未来に導いていくでしょう。<line-break>これからは人間の時代が来るんですね。<end>"
EN: "For better or worse, humans change. In time, people's thoughts and feelings will lead the world toward a new future. The age of humanity is coming.<end>"
KO: "인간은 좋든 나쁘든 변해 가는 존재입니다. 사람의 마음은 언젠가 세계를 새로운 미래로 이끌겠지요. 이제부터는 인간의 시대가 오는 거군요.<end>"

## 1330403
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…強くなったな、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>私も力を貸そう。<end>"
EN: "You've grown strong, <value:$28>. Let me lend you my strength as well.<end>"
KO: "…강해졌군, <value:$28>. 나도 힘을 보태지.<end>"

## 1330404
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何が見えるのかしら？<end>"
EN: "What do you see?<end>"
KO: "뭐가 보이는 걸까?<end>"

## 1330405
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人の心の中…？<end>"
EN: "Inside people's hearts...?<end>"
KO: "사람의 마음속…?<end>"

## 1330406
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "痛みが痛みを生み、<line-break>悲しみが悲しみを生む。<line-break>人の思いは人を縛ってゆく。<line-break>人は自分が逃れられない<line-break>悲しみと痛みの檻の中にいることに<line-break>いつか気づく…。そして叫ぶの…。<end>"
EN: "Pain gives birth to pain, and sorrow to sorrow. People's feelings bind one another. One day, they realize they are trapped in a prison of sorrow and pain from which they can never escape... and then they cry out.<end>"
KO: "고통이 고통을 낳고 슬픔이 슬픔을 낳는다. 사람의 마음은 사람을 얽매어 가지. 인간은 자신이 벗어날 수 없는 슬픔과 고통의 우리 안에 있다는 걸 언젠가 깨닫게 돼…. 그리고 외치는 거야….<end>"

## 1330407
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生まれなければよかった。<line-break>苦しまずにすんだのに…とね。<end>"
EN: "“I wish I'd never been born. Then I wouldn't have had to suffer...”<end>"
KO: "태어나지 않았으면 좋았을 텐데. 그러면 괴롭지 않았을 거라고….<end>"

## 1330408
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この世界の悲しみが<line-break>かなえられない願いが<line-break>どうしようもない思いが<line-break>闇の王女の暗き心に共鳴して<line-break>闇の巨人として<line-break>世界中で立ち上がる…。<end>"
EN: "The sorrow of this world, its unfulfilled wishes, its helpless yearnings―all resonate with the darkness in the Princess of Darkness's heart, rising throughout the world as Giants of Darkness...<end>"
KO: "이 세계의 슬픔이, 이루어지지 않는 소원이, 어찌할 수 없는 마음이 어둠의 왕녀의 어두운 마음에 공명해 어둠의 거인으로서 세계 곳곳에 일어선다…<end>"

## 1330409
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーあ、かわいそうに。<line-break>生まれたてだったのに。<line-break>ひどいことするなぁ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, poor thing. It had only just been born. That was cruel.<end>"
KO: "아아, 불쌍해라. 태어난 지 얼마 되지도 않았는데. 너무하잖아.<end>"

## 1330410
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、いっか。<line-break>まだまだたくさん<line-break>産まれたからね。<end>"
EN: "Oh well. Plenty more have been born.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 됐나. 아직 많이 태어났으니까.<end>"

## 1330411
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ…あらがわないほうが<line-break>幸せなのに。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee... You'd be happier if you stopped resisting.<end>"
KO: "후후… 거스르지 않는 편이 행복할 텐데.<end>"

## 1330412
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…シャリ、やめろ。<line-break>やめてくれ…<line-break>これでは世界が…滅ぶ…。<end>"
EN: "Shalli, stop. Please... If this continues, the world will be destroyed...<end>"
KO: "…샤리, 그만해. 그만둬 줘… 이러다 세계가… 멸망해….<end>"

## 1330413
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ、何言ってんの？<line-break>自分で守れないものを<line-break>壊すのをとめてくれなんて。<line-break>君にはなんの手もないんだ。<line-break>世界も、<value:$28>も守れない。<line-break>だったら、黙って見ててよ。アハハ！<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, what are you talking about? You want me to stop destroying what you can't even protect yourself? There's nothing you can do. You can't protect the world or <value:$28>. So shut up and watch. Ahaha!<end>"
KO: "후후, 무슨 소리야? 스스로 지키지도 못하는 걸 부수지 말아 달라니. 너에겐 아무 수단도 없어. 세계도, <value:$28>도 지키지 못해. 그러니 입 다물고 보고 있어. 아하하!<end>"

## 1330414
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…手だては…ある。<line-break>お前が教えてくれた手だてが…。<end>"
EN: "There is... a way. The way you taught me...<end>"
KO: "…방법은… 있어. 네가 알려 준 방법이….<end>"

## 1330415
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界の人たちよ。<line-break>聞こえると思う。<line-break>我が言葉を聞くのだ…。<line-break>滅びの時が来た…。<line-break>この恐怖をぬぐうのは<line-break>救世主たる我より他にない…<end>"
EN: "People of the world, I believe you can hear me. Hear my words... The hour of destruction is upon us... None but I, your Savior, can deliver you from this terror...<end>"
KO: "세계의 사람들이여. 들릴 것이다. 내 말을 들어라…. 멸망의 때가 왔다…. 이 공포를 씻어낼 자는 구세주인 나밖에 없다….<end>"

## 1330416
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くだらない。<line-break>ティアナ、闇の巨人で<line-break>やっちゃってよ。<end>"
EN: "What nonsense. Tiana, take care of it with the Giants of Darkness.<end>"
KO: "시시해. 티아나, 어둠의 거인으로 해치워 버려.<end>"

## 1330417
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我を信じよ…<line-break>すべてを我になげうて…<line-break>すべてをゆだねよ…<line-break>祈るがいい…<line-break>そして心を開くのだ…<line-break>すべてをゆだねよ…<end>"
EN: "Believe in me... Cast aside everything for me... Surrender everything to me... Pray... Open your hearts... Surrender everything to me...<end>"
KO: "나를 믿어라… 모든 것을 내게 바쳐라… 모두 맡겨라… 기도하라… 그리고 마음을 열어라… 모두 맡겨라….<end>"

## 1330418
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ…。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee...<end>"
KO: "후후후….<end>"

## 1330419
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフアハハ…！<line-break>君が僕を裏切ってもね<line-break>僕は信じていたよ、エルファス。<line-break>君が愛を信じる負け犬になれば<line-break>必ず、<value:$28>を守るため<line-break>神になると…ね。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, ahaha...! Even after you betrayed me, I still believed in you, Elfas. I knew that once you became a loser who believed in love, you would become a god to protect <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "우후후아하하…! 네가 날 배신해도 난 믿고 있었어, 엘파스. 네가 사랑을 믿는 패배자가 되면 반드시 <value:$28>를 지키기 위해 신이 될 거라고… 말이야.<end>"

## 1330420
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうだい。<line-break>これが僕の新しいオモチャ。<line-break>新たなる神だよ。<end>"
EN: "What do you think? This is my new toy―a new god.<end>"
KO: "어때? 이게 내 새 장난감. 새로운 신이야.<end>"

## 1330421
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "驚いたかい？<line-break>強大な闇の巨人が世界中を襲ってるのさ。<line-break>でも、これはほんの序の口<line-break>ここからが本番だ。<end>"
EN: "Surprised? Mighty Giants of Darkness are attacking all over the world. But that's only the beginning. Now the real show starts.<end>"
KO: "놀랐어? 강대한 어둠의 거인들이 온 세상을 습격하고 있지. 하지만 이건 시작일 뿐이야. 이제부터가 진짜다.<end>"

## 1330422
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見るがいい…<line-break>世界中の人のソウルを吸収し…、<line-break>僕は…、神になる…<end>"
EN: "Behold... I shall absorb the Souls of people throughout the world... and I... shall become a god...<end>"
KO: "보아라… 세상 사람들의 소울을 흡수해… 나는… 신이 된다…<end>"

## 1330423
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界の人たちよ。<line-break>聞こえると思う。<line-break>我が言葉を聞くのだ…<line-break>滅びの時が来た…<line-break>この恐怖をぬぐうのは<line-break>救世主たる我より他にない…<end>"
EN: "People of the world, I believe you can hear me. Hear my words... The hour of destruction is upon us... None but I, your Savior, can deliver you from this terror...<end>"
KO: "세계의 사람들이여. 들릴 것이다. 내 말을 들어라…. 멸망의 때가 왔다…. 이 공포를 씻어낼 자는 구세주인 나밖에 없다….<end>"

## 1330424
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我を信じよ…<line-break>すべてを我になげうて…<line-break>すべてをゆだねよ…<line-break>祈るがいい…<line-break>そして心を開くのだ…<line-break>すべてをゆだねよ…<end>"
EN: "Believe in me... Cast aside everything for me... Surrender everything to me... Pray... Open your hearts... Surrender everything to me...<end>"
KO: "나를 믿어라… 모든 것을 내게 바쳐라… 모두 맡겨라… 기도하라… 그리고 마음을 열어라… 모두 맡겨라….<end>"

## 1330425
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ…。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee...<end>"
KO: "후후후….<end>"

## 1330426
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうだい。<line-break>これが僕の新しいオモチャ。<line-break>新たなる神だよ。<line-break>ねえ、エルファス、<line-break>神になった気分はどう？<end>"
EN: "What do you think? This is my new toy―a new god. Tell me, Elfas, how does it feel to be a god?<end>"
KO: "어때. 이게 내 새로운 장난감이야. 새로운 신이지. 있잖아, 엘파스. 신이 된 기분은 어때?<end>"

## 1330427
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやおや。いろんな人の精神が<line-break>入り込んできて<line-break>自分を失ってしまったみたいだね。<line-break>ま、君は自分の痛みばっかりに夢中で、<line-break>他人にも痛みが存在するなんて<line-break>気づきもしなかったからねえ…。<end>"
EN: "Oh dear. It seems all those other minds flooding in have made you lose yourself. You were so consumed by your own pain that you never even noticed other people suffered too...<end>"
KO: "이런 이런. 여러 사람의 정신이 들어와서 자신을 잃어버린 모양이구나. 뭐, 넌 자기 아픔에만 빠져서 다른 사람에게도 아픔이 있다는 걸 깨닫지도 못했으니까….<end>"

## 1330428
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君みたいな痛みなら強弱の差はあれ、<line-break>誰でも持ってるものなんだよ。<line-break>いい勉強になった？　アハハ…！<line-break>でも困ったな。精神が無くて<line-break>判断ができないんじゃ粗悪品だよ。<line-break>ちゃんと動くのかな…。<end>"
EN: "Everyone has pain like yours, even if some feel it more keenly than others. Did you learn something? Ahaha...! Still, this is a problem. A product with no mind to make decisions is defective. I wonder if it'll even work...<end>"
KO: "너 같은 아픔이라면 정도의 차이는 있어도 누구나 가지고 있어. 좋은 공부가 됐어? 아하하…! 하지만 곤란하네. 정신이 없어서 판단도 못 하면 불량품이잖아. 제대로 움직이려나….<end>"

## 1330429
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁて、邪眼帝と呼ばれたバロルの<line-break>お手並み拝見といこうかな。<end>"
EN: "Now then, let's see what Balor, the Evil-Eyed Emperor, can do.<end>"
KO: "자, 사안제라 불리던 발로르의 솜씨를 구경해 볼까.<end>"

## 1330430
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ…アハハ！　すごい！<line-break>予想以上だね、バロルの力は！<end>"
EN: "Hee hee... Ahaha! Amazing! Balor's power is greater than I expected!<end>"
KO: "우후후… 아하하! 대단해! 예상 이상이야, 발로르의 힘은!<end>"

## 1330431
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この世界を無に帰すの…。<line-break>人の想いの網から解放してあげるの…。<line-break>すべてを…。<end>"
EN: "I will return this world to nothingness... I will free everything from the web of human feeling... Everything...<end>"
KO: "이 세계를 무로 돌리는 거야…. 사람들의 생각이라는 그물에서 해방해 주는 거야…. 모든 것을….<end>"

## 1330432
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だってさ。困っちゃうよねぇ。<line-break>勇者様、この世界を救って～！<line-break>…って感じかな。アハハ…<line-break>あとは頼んだよ、<line-break>勇者<value:$28>様。<line-break>アハハハ…！<end>"
EN: "So there you have it. Quite the predicament, isn't it? “Oh, great Hero, save the world!” Something like that. Ahaha... The rest is up to you, Hero <value:$28>. Ahahaha...!<end>"
KO: "그렇다네. 곤란하지? 용사님, 이 세계를 구해 줘~! …이런 느낌인가. 하하…. 나머지는 부탁할게, 용사 <value:$28>님. 하하하…!<end>"

## 1330433
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたがアトレイアを<line-break>助けたせいで…こうなったわ…。<line-break>すべて、あなたのせい。<line-break>いかが？<line-break>私は、あなたが作り上げた化け物よ。<line-break>美しいかしら？<end>"
EN: "This happened because you saved Atleia... It's all your fault. What do you think? I am the monster you created. Am I beautiful?<end>"
KO: "네가 아트레이아를 도운 탓에… 이렇게 됐어…. 전부 네 탓이야. 어때? 나는 네가 만들어 낸 괴물이야. 아름다워?<end>"

## 1330434
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は、こうなったことを<line-break>喜んでいるのよ…。<line-break>だって…。<line-break>あなたを死の静寂で包んで<line-break>私を永遠に拒絶できないように<line-break>できるんですもの…。<end>"
EN: "I'm glad this happened... Because now I can wrap you in the silence of death, so that you can never reject me again...<end>"
KO: "나는 이렇게 된 걸 기뻐하고 있어… 왜냐하면… 당신을 죽음의 정적에 감싸 영원히 나를 거절할 수 없게 만들 수 있으니까…<end>"

## 1330435
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な…、何…！？<end>"
EN: "Wh-what...!?<end>"
KO: "뭐…, 뭐야…!?<end>"

## 1330436
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無事か、<line-break><value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "Are you all right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "무사하냐, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1330437
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、ネメア…。<line-break>破壊神の門たる<line-break>偽りの勇者様。<line-break>あなたに彼らと一緒にいる<line-break>資格はないわ。<line-break>神よ、汚らわしきネメアを消せ！<end>"
EN: "Why, Nemea... False Hero, gateway of the God of Destruction. You have no right to stand with them. God, erase that filthy Nemea!<end>"
KO: "어머, 네메아…. 파괴신의 문인 거짓 용사님. 당신에겐 그들과 함께 있을 자격이 없어. 신이여, 더러운 네메아를 없애 주소서!<end>"

## 1330438
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…たとえ闇に落ちようと、<line-break>私は私だ！<end>"
EN: "Even if I fall into darkness, I am still myself!<end>"
KO: "…설령 어둠에 떨어져도 나는 나다!<end>"

## 1330439
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ！<line-break>破滅の槍の力を解放する気なの？<end>"
EN: "Damn! Are you going to unleash the Spear of Ruin's power?<end>"
KO: "큭! 파멸의 창의 힘을 해방하려는 거야?<end>"

## 1330440
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…必ず、あなたを殺す。<line-break>それまで、この静かな世界を<line-break>虚無に帰すのは待ってあげるわ。<line-break>あなたは私と戦わなければならない<line-break>この静寂から世界を救うためにはね<line-break>お城の謁見の間で待ってるわ。<end>"
EN: "I will kill you. Until then, I'll wait before returning this silent world to the Void. You must fight me if you want to save the world from this silence. I'll be waiting in the castle's audience chamber.<end>"
KO: "…반드시 널 죽이겠어. 그때까지 이 고요한 세계를 허무로 돌려보내는 건 기다려 줄게. 이 정적에서 세계를 구하려면 넌 나와 싸워야 하니까. 성의 알현실에서 기다릴게.<end>"

## 1330441
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…強くなったな、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "You've grown strong, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…강해졌구나, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1330442
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>…私も力を貸そう。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Let me lend you my strength as well.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, …나도 힘을 보태지.<end>"

## 1330443
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、待ってたよ。<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Hey. I've been waiting for you, bearer of the Infinite Soul. <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "여어, 기다리고 있었어. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1330444
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はシスティーナの伝道師の１人、<line-break>黒の祈りのジュサプブロス…。<line-break>ま、早い話がシャリのダチだ。<line-break>君に興味があってね。<line-break>世界がなくなる前に<line-break>君を殺したくなってここに来た。<end>"
EN: "I'm one of Sistina's missionaries: Jusabros of the Black Prayer. Or, to put it simply, I'm a friend of Shalli's. You intrigued me, so I came here to kill you before the world disappears.<end>"
KO: "나는 시스티나 전도사 중 하나, 검은 기도의 주사프브로스…. 쉽게 말하면 샤리 친구야. 네게 흥미가 있어서 말이지. 세상이 없어지기 전에 널 죽이고 싶어져서 왔어.<end>"

## 1330445
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ははあ、これは予想外な<line-break>展開になったな。<line-break> <line-break>誰かと徒党を組むのは、<line-break>俺が弱いと思われるから、<line-break>本当は嫌なんだけどなあ。<end>"
EN: "Well now, I didn't expect this. I don't really like joining forces with anyone. It makes people think I'm weak.<end>"
KO: "하하, 예상 밖의 전개가 됐군. 누군가와 패거리를 이루면 내가 약해 보일 것 같아서 사실 싫은데.<end>"

## 1330446
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何も、頼んでるわけじゃねえ！<line-break>俺が出てきたとこにお前が<line-break>いたんじゃねえか！<end>"
EN: "It's not like I asked you for anything! You just happened to be here when I showed up!<end>"
KO: "아무것도 부탁한 거 아니야! 내가 나온 곳에 네가 있었던 거잖아!<end>"

## 1330447
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、どうでもいいか。<line-break>勝った奴のみが生き、負けた奴は…<end>"
EN: "Well, whatever. The winner lives, and the loser...<end>"
KO: "뭐, 아무래도 상관없나. 이긴 자만 살아남고 진 자는….<end>"

## 1330448
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行くぞ、<value:$28>！<line-break>今こそ、ナッジの恨みを晴らす！<end>"
EN: "Let's go, <value:$28>! It's time to avenge Nadge!<end>"
KO: "가자, <value:$28>! 지금이야말로 나지의 원한을 풀 때다!<end>"

## 1330449
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その前に聞いておく。<line-break>５歳以下の子供は殺さない主義でね。<line-break>５歳以下の人いる？<end>"
EN: "Before we begin, I have to ask. I make it a rule not to kill children five or younger. Is anyone here five or younger?<end>"
KO: "그전에 물어 두지. 다섯 살 이하의 아이는 죽이지 않는 주의라서. 다섯 살 이하인 사람 있어?<end>"

## 1330450
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いないようだね、よかった。<line-break>それじゃあ、<line-break>お手合わせ願おうかな？<end>"
EN: "It doesn't look like it. Good. Then shall we see what you've got?<end>"
KO: "…없는 것 같네, 다행이야. 그럼, 한번 겨뤄 볼까?<end>"

## 1330451
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待てよ、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Wait, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "기다려, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1330452
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前の敵が世界を滅ぼそうっていう<line-break>悪者だけだと<line-break>思っちゃいないだろうな…？<line-break>世の中のテメエ勝手な正義で<line-break>友を失った男の存在を、<line-break>忘れるんじゃねえぜ。<end>"
EN: "You don't think your only enemies are villains out to destroy the world, do you? Don't forget the man who lost a friend to this world's self-serving justice.<end>"
KO: "네 적이 세상을 멸망시키려는 악당뿐이라고 생각하진 않겠지…? 세상의 제멋대로인 정의 때문에 친구를 잃은 남자가 있다는 걸 잊지 마라.<end>"

## 1330453
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナッジは…悪人なんかじゃなかった。<line-break>いい奴だった…<line-break>あいつは死ななくてよかった！<end>"
EN: "Nadge wasn't a bad guy. He was a good guy... He didn't have to die!<end>"
KO: "나지는… 악인이 아니었어. 좋은 녀석이었어…. 그 녀석은 죽지 않아도 됐어!<end>"

## 1330454
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナッジが死ななきゃならなかった<line-break>ケツの穴の小さい世界なんて、<line-break>ブッ壊れちまえばいいんだ。<line-break>この世界の片棒かついだ<line-break><value:$28>、お前もだ！<line-break>死んでもらうぜ！<end>"
EN: "If the world is so tight-assed that Nadge had to die, then let the whole damn thing be destroyed. You helped prop it up, <value:$28>, so that goes for you too! I'm going to kill you!<end>"
KO: "나지가 죽어야 했던 속 좁은 세계 따위 부서져 버리면 돼. 이 세계를 떠받든 <value:$28>, 너도 마찬가지야! 죽어 줘야겠어!<end>"

## 1330455
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うう…。なぜだ…。<line-break>どうしてなんだよ…。<line-break> <line-break>悪いのは…俺たちなのか…？<line-break>う、薄汚れてんのは、<line-break>お前たちのほうじゃねえのか？<end>"
EN: "Ngh... Why...? Why...? Are we the ones in the wrong...? A-aren't you the filthy ones?<end>"
KO: "으으… 왜지… 어째서야… 나쁜 건… 우리인 건가…? 더, 더러운 건 너희 쪽 아니냐고?<end>"

## 1330456
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺やナッジみたいなやつが…<line-break>笑いながら暮らせない世界なんて…<line-break>こっちから、出てってやるぜ…。<line-break>ナッジ…俺たちゃ、<line-break>ダチ…だかんな…。<line-break>いつでも一緒だ…ぜ…。<end>"
EN: "A world where guys like Nadge and me can't live with a smile... I'll be the one to leave it... Nadge... we're pals, you know... Always together...<end>"
KO: "나나 나지 같은 녀석이… 웃으며 살 수 없는 세상이라면… 내가 먼저 나가 주겠어…. 나지… 우린 친구…잖아…. 언제까지나 함께다….<end>"

## 1330457
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "照りつける太陽の下、俺は死ぬ…、<line-break>ふふ…、それでいい…。<end>"
EN: "So I die beneath the blazing sun... Heh. That's all right.<end>"
KO: "내리쬐는 태양 아래에서 난 죽는다… 후후… 그걸로 됐어….<end>"

## 1330458
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…、<value:$28>様…。<line-break>いらしてくださったのですね…。<end>"
EN: "Ah... <value:$28>. You came...<end>"
KO: "아… <value:$28>님…. 와 주셨군요….<end>"

## 1330459
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナのことを嫌いになられて、<line-break>もういらしてくださらないのではと<line-break>胸を痛めていたのです…。<end>"
EN: "I was afraid you had come to hate Tiana and would never visit again. The thought broke my heart...<end>"
KO: "티아나를 싫어하게 되어 이제 와 주지 않으실까 봐 마음 아파하고 있었어요….<end>"

## 1330460
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…魔王バロルはどこだ？<end>"
EN: "Where is Balor, Demon King?<end>"
KO: "…마왕 발로르는 어디 있지?<end>"

## 1330461
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ。<line-break>魔王バロルをネメアが倒すという予言…<line-break>その運命にあくまで操られるおつもり？<end>"
EN: "Heh. The prophecy that Nemea will defeat Balor, Demon King... Do you really intend to remain a pawn of that fate?<end>"
KO: "후후. 마왕 발로르를 네메아가 쓰러뜨린다는 예언… 그 운명에 끝까지 휘둘릴 생각이야?<end>"

## 1330462
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "運命など関係ない。<line-break>バロルが世を<line-break>虚無に帰すがゆえに討つのだ。<end>"
EN: "Fate has nothing to do with it. I will strike Balor down because he would return the world to the Void.<end>"
KO: "운명 따윈 상관없다. 발로르가 세상을 허무로 돌리려 하기에 쓰러뜨리는 것이다.<end>"

## 1330463
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ…。<line-break>果たしてあなたに<line-break>今のバロルが倒せるかしらね。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee... I wonder whether you can defeat Balor as he is now.<end>"
KO: "우후후…. 과연 당신이 지금의 발로르를 쓰러뜨릴 수 있을까.<end>"

## 1330464
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…フフ、ウフフ、アハハ！<line-break>傲慢なる獅子に死を！<line-break>傷ついた神に無限のソウルを！<line-break>神はいよいよ完璧になり、<line-break>そして、世界を完全な沈黙で<line-break>覆うことができる…！<end>"
EN: "Heh... hee hee... ah ha ha! Death to the arrogant lion! The Infinite Soul to the wounded god! At last, the god will be perfect―and able to cover the world in absolute Silence!<end>"
KO: "…후후, 우후후, 아하하! 오만한 사자에게 죽음을! 상처 입은 신에게 무한의 소울을! 신은 마침내 완벽해지고, 세계를 완전한 침묵으로 뒤덮을 수 있다…!<end>"

## 1330465
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、あなたは永遠に<line-break>私を拒絶できなくなる…！<end>"
EN: "And then you will never be able to reject me again!<end>"
KO: "그리고 당신은 영원히 나를 거부할 수 없게 돼…!<end>"

## 1330466
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いかがですか？<line-break>すべての人が消えた<line-break>静かな世界は…？<end>"
EN: "How do you like this silent world, with everyone gone?<end>"
KO: "어떻습니까? 모든 사람이 사라진 조용한 세계는…?<end>"

## 1330467
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この、どうしようもない静寂…。<line-break>ふふふ…。<line-break>これは、私の人生そのものです。<end>"
EN: "This inescapable silence... Heh heh heh... This is my life itself.<end>"
KO: "이 구제불능의 정적…. 후후후…. 이건 제 인생 그 자체입니다.<end>"

## 1330468
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたも、この永遠の寂寞の中で<line-break>死んでいくがいいわ。<line-break> <line-break>息吹も鼓動も止めて、<line-break>静寂の一部として溶け込むのよ。<line-break>フフフ…それが運命…<end>"
EN: "You, too, shall die amid this eternal desolation. Let your breath and heartbeat cease, and dissolve into the silence. Heh heh heh... Such is fate...<end>"
KO: "당신도 이 영원한 적막 속에서 죽어 가렴. 숨결도 심장 박동도 멈추고 정적의 일부로 녹아드는 거야. 후후후… 그게 운명….<end>"

## 1330469
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…それが運命だと言うのであれば、<line-break>あらがうまで！<end>"
EN: "If that is what fate decrees, then I shall defy it!<end>"
KO: "…그게 운명이라면 끝까지 거슬러 주겠어!<end>"

## 1330470
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ…。<line-break>あらがっても苦しむだけなのに…<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... Defiance will only bring you pain...<end>"
KO: "후후…. 저항해 봐야 괴로울 뿐인데….<end>"

## 1330471
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃい…いとしい殿方たち。<end>"
EN: "Welcome... my beloved gentlemen.<end>"
KO: "어서 와… 사랑스러운 남자들이여.<end>"

## 1330472
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼らは、私を絶対に否定しませんわ。<line-break>フフ、ウフフ…すてきでしょう？<end>"
EN: "They will never reject me. Heh, hee hee... Isn't it wonderful?<end>"
KO: "저들은 절대로 나를 부정하지 않아. 후후, 우후후… 멋지지?<end>"

## 1330473
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたにとっては、さしずめ、<line-break>感動の再会というところかしら？<end>"
EN: "For you, I suppose this would be a touching reunion.<end>"
KO: "너에게는 말하자면 감동의 재회 정도려나?<end>"

## 1330474
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何を言ってもムダよ。<line-break>彼らには意志がない…。<line-break>彼らは完全に私のものなの。<line-break>彼らは、私を絶対に否定しない。<line-break>フフ、ウフフ…すてきでしょう？<end>"
EN: "Whatever you say, it's useless. They have no will of their own... They belong entirely to me. They will never reject me. Heh, hee hee... Isn't it wonderful?<end>"
KO: "무슨 말을 해도 소용없어. 저들에게는 의지가 없어… 저들은 완전히 내 것이야. 절대로 나를 부정하지 않아. 후후, 우후후… 멋지지?<end>"

## 1330475
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたにとっては、さしずめ、<line-break>感動の再会というところかしら？<end>"
EN: "For you, I suppose this would be a touching reunion.<end>"
KO: "너에게는 말하자면 감동의 재회 정도려나?<end>"

## 1330476
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何を言ってもムダよ。<line-break>彼らには意志がない…。<line-break>彼らは完全に私のものなの。<line-break>彼らは、私を絶対に否定しない。<line-break>フフ、ウフフ…すてきでしょう？<end>"
EN: "Whatever you say, it's useless. They have no will of their own... They belong entirely to me. They will never reject me. Heh, hee hee... Isn't it wonderful?<end>"
KO: "무슨 말을 해도 소용없어. 저들에게는 의지가 없어… 저들은 완전히 내 것이야. 절대로 나를 부정하지 않아. 후후, 우후후… 멋지지?<end>"

## 1330477
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界が虚無に帰るまでの短い間だけど、<line-break>あなたにも、こうなってもらうわ。<end>"
EN: "It will only last a little while, until the world returns to the Void, but I'll make you just like them too.<end>"
KO: "세계가 허무로 돌아가기 전의 짧은 시간이지만, 당신도 이렇게 되어줘야겠어.<end>"

## 1330478

## 1330479
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クーデターで死んだゼネテスを、<line-break>私の操り人形としてよみがえらせたの。<line-break>フフ、ウフフ…すてきでしょう？<end>"

## 1330481
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クーデターで死んだゼネテスを<line-break>私の操り人形としてよみがえらせたの。<line-break>何を言っても無駄よ。<line-break>彼は完全に私のもの。<line-break>彼はもう私を否定できない。<line-break>フフ、ウフフ…すてきでしょう？<end>"
EN: "I resurrected Xenetes, who died in the coup, as my puppet. Whatever you say, it's useless. He belongs entirely to me. He can no longer reject me. Heh, hee hee... Isn't it wonderful?<end>"
KO: "쿠데타로 죽은 제네테스를 내 꼭두각시로 되살렸어. 무슨 말을 해도 소용없어. 그는 완전히 내 거야. 이제 나를 부정할 수 없어. 후후, 우후후… 멋지지?<end>"

## 1330482
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界が虚無に帰るまでの短い間だけど、<line-break>あなたにも、こうなってもらうわ。<end>"
EN: "It will only last a little while, until the world returns to the Void, but I'll make you just like him too.<end>"
KO: "세계가 허무로 돌아가기 전의 짧은 시간이지만, 당신도 이렇게 되어줘야겠어.<end>"

EN: "Welcome, Lemghon...<end>"
KO: "어서 와, 레무온….<end>"

## 1330484

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1330486
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何を言ってもムダよ。<line-break>レムオンには意志がない…。<line-break>レムオンは完全に私のものなの。<line-break>レムオンは私のいいなりの人形。<line-break>私を絶対に否定しませんわ。<line-break>フフ、ウフフ…すてきでしょう？<end>"
EN: "Whatever you say, it is useless. Lemghon has no will of his own... He belongs entirely to me. He is a puppet who obeys my every command. He will never reject me. Heh, hee hee... Isn't it wonderful?<end>"
KO: "무슨 말을 해도 소용없어. 레무온에게는 의지가 없어…. 레무온은 완전히 내 거야. 내 말대로 움직이는 인형. 절대로 나를 부정하지 않아. 후후, 우후후… 멋지지?<end>"

## 1330487
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "It will only last a little while, until the world returns to the Void, but I'll make you just like him too.<end>"
KO: "세계가 허무로 돌아가기 전의 짧은 시간이지만, 당신도 이렇게 되어줘야겠어.<end>"

## 1330488
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがね、<value:$28>。<line-break>やはり抜け殻では力不足、<line-break>といったところかしら。<end>"
EN: "Impressive, <value:$28>. It seems an empty shell was no match for you after all.<end>"
KO: "역시네, <value:$28>. 역시 껍데기만으로는 힘이 부족한 모양이군.<end>"

## 1330489
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お遊びはこれまで。<line-break>このティアナじきじきに<line-break>お相手をいたしましょう…！<end>"
EN: "Playtime is over. Tiana herself will be your opponent now!<end>"
KO: "장난은 여기까지. 이 티아나가 직접 상대해 드리죠…!<end>"

## 1330491
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お前…<value:$28>？<line-break><value:$28>…なの…か…？<end>"
EN: "You... <value:$28>? Is it... really <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "…너…<value:$28>? <value:$28>…인…거야…?<end>"

## 1330492
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオン…まさか…？<line-break>ありえない…！<line-break>ゼネテス、レムオンを処分なさい！<end>"
EN: "Lemghon... No... That is impossible! Xenetes, dispose of Lemghon!<end>"
KO: "레무온… 설마…? 있을 수 없어…! 제네테스, 레무온을 처리하세요!<end>"

## 1330493
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>…か。<line-break>屋敷に…戻ってきたのなら…<line-break>どうして…俺に会いに来ない…？<line-break>とにかく…お前が…無事で…よかった。<line-break>エストは心配していた…ぞ？<line-break>俺は…、俺は…どうして…こんな…？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... So it is you. If you came back to the manor... why didn't you come to see me? Anyway... I'm glad you're safe. Est was worried... you know? Why... Why am I like this...?<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>…인가. 저택에… 돌아왔다면… 왜… 나를 만나러 오지 않았지…? 아무튼… 네가… 무사해서… 다행이다. 에스트가 걱정하고… 있었어. 나는… 나는… 왜… 이런…?<end>"

## 1330494
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…それに…お前はどうして、<line-break>そんなに傷ついて…いる…のだ？<line-break>誰が…俺の大事な…<value:$28>を<end>"
EN: "And why... are you so badly hurt? Who did this... to my precious <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "…그런데… 넌 왜 그렇게 다쳐… 있는 거지? 누가… 내 소중한… <value:$28>을….<end>"

## 1330495
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺…か…？<line-break>ずっと、俺がお前を…傷つけて、<line-break>今も…<value:$28>を…？<end>"
EN: "Was it... me? Have I been hurting you... all this time? Am I still... hurting <value:$28>...?<end>"
KO: "나…인가…? 계속 내가 널… 상처 입히고, 지금도… <value:$28>을…?<end>"

## 1330496
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…すま…ん。<line-break>俺の…大事な<value:$28>…。<line-break>俺は、お前を守りたか…ったのに…。<line-break>すま…ん…。<end>"
EN: "I'm... sorry. My precious <value:$28>... I wanted... to protect you. I'm... sorry...<end>"
KO: "…미안…하다. 내… 소중한 <value:$28>…. 나는 너를 지키고 싶었…는데…. 미안…하다….<end>"

## 1330497
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…使えない奴め。<line-break>クーデターの前から、<line-break>口ほどにもないと思ってたわ。<line-break>お遊びはこれまで。<line-break>このティアナじきじきに<line-break>お相手をいたしましょう…！<end>"
EN: "Useless fool. Even before the coup, I knew you were all talk. Playtime is over. Tiana herself will be your opponent now!<end>"
KO: "…쓸모없는 녀석. 쿠데타 전부터 말뿐이라고 생각했어. 장난은 끝이야. 이 티아나가 직접 상대해 주지…!<end>"

## 1330499
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これで<value:$28>も、<line-break>もう、私を否定できなくなる…。<line-break>フフ…ウフフ…。<end>"
EN: "Now <value:$28> will never be able to reject me again... Heh... hee hee...<end>"
KO: "이제 <value:$28>도 더는 나를 부정할 수 없게 돼…. 후후… 우후후….<end>"

## 1330500
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ティア…ナ、<line-break>それ…お前…不幸じゃねえ…か？<end>"
EN: "Tia... na... Isn't that... making you miserable...?<end>"
KO: "…티아…나, 그거… 너… 불행한 거… 아니냐?<end>"

## 1330501
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス？<line-break>そんな、ありえない…！<line-break>死んでまで、私を否定する気なの？<line-break>レムオン！<line-break>ゼネテスを片づけて！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes? No, that is impossible! You mean to reject me even in death? Lemghon! Get rid of Xenetes!<end>"
KO: "제네테스? 그럴 리 없어…! 죽어서까지 나를 부정할 셈이야? 레무온! 제네테스를 처리해!<end>"

## 1330502
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺も…ついて…るな。<line-break>んな…最期の最期…で…<line-break>お前を…助けられる…なんて…よ。<end>"
EN: "Guess I'm... lucky. At the very... very end... I get to save you...<end>"
KO: "나도… 운이… 좋네. 이런… 마지막 순간에… 널… 구할 수 있다니… 말이야.<end>"

## 1330503
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>。<line-break>俺…の最高…の女に…<line-break>見送って…もらえるのか…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... My best girl... gets to see me off...<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>. 내… 최고의 여자에게… 배웅받을 수 있는 건가….<end>"

## 1330504
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私が不幸ですって？<line-break>では、あなたはどれだけ幸せなの、<line-break>ゼネテス？<line-break>死んでなお前向きなのはわかったわ。<line-break>でも、それは負け惜しみに過ぎない。<line-break>ブザマよ！<end>"
EN: "You call me miserable? Then how happy are you, Xenetes? I see you're still looking on the bright side, even in death. But that's nothing more than a loser's bravado. How pathetic!<end>"
KO: "내가 불행하다고? 그럼 넌 얼마나 행복한데, 제네테스? 죽어서도 긍정적인 건 알겠어. 하지만 그건 패자의 허세일 뿐이야. 꼴사나워!<end>"

## 1330505
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…もうたくさんよ、ゼネテス！<line-break>あなたは最低の男よ。<line-break>あなたのせいで世界が滅ぶのよ？<line-break>あなたが私を否定し<line-break><value:$28>のほうだけを<line-break>見ていたから、こうなったのよ！？<end>"
EN: "Enough, Xenetes! You are the worst kind of man. The world is going to end because of you! This happened because you rejected me and had eyes only for <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "…이제 지긋지긋해, 제네테스! 넌 최악의 남자야. 네 탓에 세계가 멸망하는 거라고? 네가 나를 부정하고 <value:$28>만 바라봤으니까 이렇게 된 거야!?<end>"

## 1330507

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1330525
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺…か…？<line-break>ずっと、俺がお前を…傷つけて、<line-break>今も…<value:$28>を…？<end>"

## 1330526
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…すま…ん。<line-break>俺の…大事な<value:$28>…。<line-break>俺は、お前を守りたか…ったのに…。<line-break>すま…ん…。<end>"

## 1330527
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…使えない奴め。<line-break>クーデターの前から、<line-break>口ほどにもないと思ってたわ。<line-break>お遊びはこれまで。<line-break>このティアナじきじきに<line-break>お相手をいたしましょう…！<end>"
EN: "Useless fool. Even before the coup, I knew you were all talk. Playtime is over. Tiana herself will be your opponent now!<end>"
KO: "…쓸모없는 녀석. 쿠데타 전부터 말뿐이라고 생각했어. 장난은 끝이야. 이 티아나가 직접 상대해 주지…!<end>"

## 1330528
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、このままでは終わらない…<line-break>ま…魔王バロルよ。<line-break>わ、我が血肉を糧に…<end>"

KO: "이, 이대로 끝나진 않는다… 마… 마왕 발로르여. 내… 내 피와 살을 양식으로…<end>"

## 1330529
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ア…新たナるチカらヲ…。<line-break>コ、コノヨニ滅びヲもたらしタマエ！<line-break>アアアア…！！<end>"

KO: "아… 새로운 힘을…. 이, 이 세상에 멸망을 가져다주소서! 아아아아…!!<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "어서 와… 귀여운 인형들이여.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1330533

## 1330534
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1330550
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "후후… 후후후후… 죽어서도 긍정적이라니… 소름 끼쳐.<end>"

## 1330569
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お遊びはこれまで。<line-break>このアトレイアじきじきに<line-break>お相手をいたしましょう…！<end>"

## 1330570
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ…よかった。<line-break>人形にあなたがやられてしまったら<line-break>どうしようかと思っていました。<line-break>さすがは<value:$28>。<line-break>私が直接手を下す必要が<line-break>あるようですわね…。<end>"

KO: "후후… 다행이에요. 인형에게 당신이 당해 버리면 어쩌나 했답니다. 역시 <value:$28>. 제가 직접 손을 써야 할 모양이군요….<end>"

## 1330571
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ…、それでは私がお相手します。<line-break>あんまりあっけなくやられて<line-break>私をがっかりさせないでくださいね。<end>"

KO: "후후… 그럼 제가 상대해 드리죠. 너무 싱겁게 당해서 절 실망시키진 말아 주세요.<end>"

## 1330572

KO: "………………? …나는… 죽었을… 텐데….<end>"

## 1330576
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1330585
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>…か。<line-break>屋敷に…戻ってきたのなら…<line-break>どうして…俺に会いに来ない…？<line-break>とにかく…お前が…無事で…よかった。<line-break>エストは心配していた…ぞ？<line-break>俺は…、俺は…どうして…こんな…？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... So it is you. If you came back to the manor... why didn't you come to see me? In any case... I'm glad you're safe. Est was worried... you know? I... Why am I... like this...?<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>…인가. 저택에… 돌아왔다면… 왜… 나를 만나러 오지 않았지…? 아무튼… 네가… 무사해서… 다행이다. 에스트가 걱정하고… 있었어. 나는… 나는… 왜… 이런…?<end>"

## 1330586
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…그런데… 넌 왜 그렇게 다쳐… 있는 거지? 누가… 내 소중한… <value:$28>을….<end>"

## 1330587
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "나…인가…? 계속 내가 널… 상처 입히고, 지금도… <value:$28>을…?<end>"

## 1330588
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…すま…ん。<line-break>俺の…大事な<value:$28>…。<line-break>俺は、お前を守りたか…ったのに…。<line-break>すま…ん…。<end>"

## 1330589
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心温まる展開というやつなのかしら？<line-break>そんなもの…<line-break>ただ、むしずが走るだけよ！<line-break>お遊びはこれまで！<line-break>このアトレイアじきじきに<line-break>お相手いたしましょう…！<end>"
EN: "Is this what they call a heartwarming turn of events? It makes my skin crawl! Playtime is over! Atleia herself shall deal with you now...!<end>"
KO: "마음 따뜻해지는 전개라는 건가? 그런 건… 그저 소름 끼칠 뿐이야! 장난은 여기까지! 이 아트레이아가 직접 상대해 드리지…!<end>"

## 1330590

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1330591
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔王バロルよ、どうか…<line-break>こ、この世に…あ、明けない闇を…！<end>"
EN: "Balor, Demon King, I beseech you... B-bring upon this world... d-darkness without dawn...!<end>"
KO: "마왕 발로르여, 부디… 이, 이 세상에… 끝나지 않는 어둠을…!<end>"

## 1330592
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　封印の巨人の…呪縛から…　　　　<line-break>　　　　　解き放たれ…、　　　　　　<line-break>　　　復讐の時を…得たり…。<end>"
EN: "Freed... from the Sealed Giant's curse... At last... the hour of vengeance is mine...<end>"
KO: "봉인의 거인의… 속박에서… 풀려나… 복수의 때를… 얻었도다….<end>"

## 1330593
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　強い…ソウル…。　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　我が身に取り込み…、　　　　　<line-break> 　　　　　　我が…糧とせん…。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　許さぬぞ…ノトゥーンの子らよ！　　<line-break>　　　　我が大地を…荒らす者たちよ！<line-break>　　　　この大地は、我が子らのもの！<end>"
EN: "A powerful... Soul... I shall take it into myself... and make it... my sustenance... I will not forgive you, children of Notun! You who ravage my land! This land belongs to my children!<end>"
KO: "강한… 소울…. 내 몸에 받아들여… 나의… 양식으로 삼으리라…. 용서하지 않겠다… 노툰의 자식들이여! 내 대지를… 어지럽히는 자들이여! 이 대지는 나의 자식들의 것이다!<end>"

## 1330594
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイアの死によってできた<line-break>強力な力場を通って現れたか。<line-break>ゆくぞ、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "So it came through the powerful force field created by Atleia's death. Let's go, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "아트레이아의 죽음으로 생긴 강력한 역장을 통해 나타났나. 간다, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1330595
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　 おのれ…。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　 だが、これでは…終わらぬ…。　　<line-break>　 必ず…、この大地を…取り戻す…。<line-break>　　　 いつの日か…、必ず…！<end>"
EN: "Curse you... But this... is not the end... I will reclaim this land... without fail... Someday... I swear it!<end>"
KO: "이놈…. 하지만 이걸로… 끝나지 않는다…. 반드시… 이 대지를… 되찾겠다…. 언젠가… 반드시…!<end>"

## 1330596
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…さすがです、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>やっぱり、勝ち残って<line-break>しまったんですね。<end>"
EN: "I should have known, <value:$28>. You really did survive to the end.<end>"
KO: "…역시 대단하세요, <value:$28>님. 결국 끝까지 이기셨군요.<end>"

## 1330597
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたの活躍で、<line-break>世界は救われました。<line-break>　<line-break>ですが、そのかわりに、<line-break>世界は新たなる脅威に<line-break>さらされています。<end>"
EN: "Your deeds saved the world. But in return, the world now faces a new threat.<end>"
KO: "당신의 활약으로 세계는 구원받았습니다. 하지만 그 대신 세계는 새로운 위협에 노출되었습니다.<end>"

## 1330598
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはあなたです。<line-break>闇をすべて消し去ってしまうほどの<line-break>強烈な光の存在。<line-break>世界の均衡を保つため、<line-break>わたしは、あなたを<line-break>…とめなければなりません。<end>"
EN: "That threat is you―a light so powerful that it could banish all darkness. To preserve the Balance of the World, I must... stop you.<end>"
KO: "그건 당신입니다. 어둠을 모두 지워 버릴 만큼 강렬한 빛의 존재. 세계의 균형을 지키기 위해 저는 당신을… 막아야 합니다.<end>"

## 1330599
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが、竜王様の意志…。<end>"
EN: "Such is the Dragon King's will...<end>"
KO: "그것이 용왕님의 뜻….<end>"

JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2ちょっと待ってよ！<line-break>今度は<value:$28>が<line-break>世界の脅威になるとでも言うの？　<line-break>冗談じゃない。<line-break>私の知ってる<value:$28>は、<line-break>そんな人じゃないわ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>3ちょっと、待ってくれ。<line-break>今度は<value:$28>が<line-break>世界の脅威になるとでも言うのか？　　<line-break>そんなことはありえん！<line-break>わしの知っている<value:$28>は、<line-break>そのような人間ではない！<end><if><value:$01><equal>4ちょっと、待って。<line-break>今度は<value:$28>が<line-break>世界を滅ぼしちゃうと思ってるの？<line-break>そんなことないもん！<line-break>私の知ってる<value:$28>は<line-break>そこまで物騒じゃないんだから！<end><if><value:$01><equal>5ちょっと、待ってくれよ！<line-break>今度は<value:$28>が<line-break>世界を滅ぼすとか思ってるのか！？<line-break>冗談じゃないぜ！<line-break>俺の知ってる<value:$28>は<line-break>そんなことをする奴じゃない！<end><if><value:$01><equal>6闇を払拭した<value:$28>が<line-break>今度は世界の均衡を崩す存在になる<line-break>…竜王はそう思っているの！？<line-break>それはありえないよ。<line-break><value:$28>は、<line-break>そんなことをする人じゃないから！<end><if><value:$01><equal>7ちょっと、待って！<line-break>今度は<value:$28>が<line-break>世界を滅ぼすとでも言うの！？<line-break>冗談じゃないわ。<line-break>そんなことを考える人と<line-break>私は戦ってきたんじゃない！<end><if><value:$01><equal>8闇を払拭した<value:$28>が<line-break>今度は世界の均衡を崩す存在になる<line-break>…竜王はそう言うのですね？　<line-break>でも、それはありえません。<line-break>私の知っている<value:$28>は<line-break>そんなことをする人ではない。<end><if><value:$01><equal>9今度は<value:$28>が<line-break>世界を滅ぼすんじゃないか　　　　　　<line-break>ってわけ？<line-break>ちょっと、お門違いなんじゃない？<line-break>あたしの知ってる<value:$28>は<line-break>そんな大それたことする人じゃないよ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>11闇を払拭した<value:$28>が<line-break>今度は世界の均衡を崩す存在になる<line-break>…竜王はそう言うのね？<line-break>でも、それはありえないわ。<line-break>私を救ってくれた<value:$28>は<line-break>そんなことをする人ではないのだから！<end><if><value:$01><equal>12闇を払拭した<value:$28>が<line-break>今度は世界の均衡を崩す存在になる<line-break>…竜王はそう思ってるのか？　<line-break>でも、それはありえないぜ。<line-break>俺の知っている<value:$28>は<line-break>そんなバカじゃないからな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>13闇を払拭した<value:$28>が<line-break>今度は世界の均衡を崩す存在になる<line-break>…竜王はそう言うのか？<line-break>それはありえん。<line-break>俺の知っている<value:$28>は<line-break>そこまでバカではない。<end><if><value:$01><equal>10ちょっと、待ってくれよ！<line-break>今度は姉ちゃんが、世界を<line-break>滅ぼすとか思ってるのか！？　<line-break>冗談じゃないぜ！<line-break>俺の姉ちゃんは、<line-break>そんなことをする人じゃない！<end><if><value:$01><equal>14ちょっと、待ってくれ！<line-break>今度は<value:$28>が<line-break>世界を滅ぼすとでも言うのか！？　　<line-break>それはありえん！<line-break>わしは、そんなことをするような人間と<line-break>一緒に旅をするほどバカではない！<end><if><value:$01><equal>15ちょっと、待ってよ！<line-break>今度は<value:$28>が<line-break>世界を滅ぼすとでも言うの！？　<line-break>冗談じゃないよ！<line-break>僕の知ってる<value:$28>は<line-break>そんなことをする人じゃない！<end><if><value:$01><equal>16闇を払拭した<value:$28>が<line-break>今度は世界の均衡を崩す存在になる<line-break>…竜王はそう言うのか？<line-break>それはありえん。<line-break>私がすべてを託した人間は<line-break>そこまで愚かな存在ではない！<end><if><value:$01><equal>17ちょっと、あなた、待ちなさいよ！<line-break>今度は<value:$28>が<line-break>世界を滅ぼすとでも言うの？<line-break>冗談ではなくてよ！　アタクシの<line-break>知っている<value:$28>は<line-break>そこまでアホウではないわ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19ちょっと、待ってよ！<line-break>今度は<value:$28>が<line-break>世界を滅ぼすって言うのぉ？　　<line-break>ルルアンタの知ってる<line-break><value:$28>は、<line-break>そんなこと絶対しないんだから！<end><if><value:$01><equal>20闇を払拭した<value:$28>が<line-break>今度は世界の均衡を崩す存在に<line-break>なる…竜王はそう言うのか？<line-break>バカな。<line-break>我の知る<value:$28>は<line-break>そこまで愚かではない。<end><if><value:$01><equal>21力の均衡がそれほど大事か竜王！<line-break>世界の危機を救ったのは<line-break><value:$28>なのだぞ！<line-break>それを、強くなりすぎたために<line-break>次なる脅威になる前に<line-break>殺そうというのか！<end><if><value:$01><equal>26世界を危機にさらした者は死し<line-break><value:$28>に<line-break>かなう者はのうなった。<line-break>このままでは力の均衡を崩し<line-break>世界に災いをもたらしかねん、か<line-break>竜教徒らしい考えじゃの。<end><if><value:$01><equal>24大神官様がお亡くなりになった今、<line-break><value:$28>様の余りある力は危険<line-break>ゆえに排除せよ…<line-break>それが、竜王様のお考えなのですね。<line-break>残念ですが、うけたまわりかねます、<line-break>ノエル・シルヴァ様。<end><if><value:$01><equal>25世界の脅威が滅んだら<line-break>もう用なしってわけ？<line-break>むしろ、強すぎるから危険ってわけ？<line-break>そんなの納得できない！<end><if><value:$01><equal>23竜王は人間が力を持つことを<line-break>不遜だと考えている。<line-break> <line-break>たとえ、世界に危機を及ぼす存在が<line-break>現れたときはその力を利用しても、だ。<line-break>それは、いささか勝手というものだな。<end><if><value:$01><equal>27強大すぎる力は世界にとって危険、<line-break>ゆえに排除すべきである…<line-break>確かに普通の者ならばそうでしょう<line-break>ですが竜王様、お考えください<line-break><value:$28>はその力に匹敵する<line-break>大きなソウルを持っているのです！<end><if><value:$01><equal>192小さき者よ、下がりなさい。<line-break>その必要はありません。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193そ、そんな…！<line-break><value:$28>さんが<line-break>世界を脅かす存在になる！？<line-break>私、そんなの認めません！<line-break><value:$28>さんは<line-break>そんなことする人じゃありません！<end><if><value:$01><equal>194竜王よ、血迷ったのか！<line-break>そんな理由で争うほうがどうかしてる！<end><if><value:$01><equal>191ちょっと待って！<line-break><value:$28>が<line-break>世界を滅ぼす存在になる！？<line-break>ありえないわ！<line-break><value:$28>は<line-break>そんな人じゃない！<end><if><value:$01><equal>190<value:$28>が<line-break>世界の均衡を壊す存在になる？<line-break>そんなバカなことがあるか！<line-break>俺を救った<value:$28>が<line-break>そんな人間であるわけがない！<end>闇を払拭した<value:$28>が<line-break>今度は世界の均衡を崩す存在になる<line-break>…竜王はそう言うの？<line-break>そんなことは絶対にないよ！<line-break>ぼくの知っている<value:$28>は<line-break>そんなに愚かな人間じゃない！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Wait! Now you're saying <value:$28> will become a threat to the world? Don't be ridiculous. The <value:$28> I know would never do such a thing!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3Wait a moment. Are you saying <value:$28> will now become a threat to the world? Impossible! The <value:$28> I know is no such person!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Wait. You think <value:$28> is going to destroy the world now? No way! The <value:$28> I know isn't that dangerous!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Hold on! You think <value:$28> is going to destroy the world now? Don't be ridiculous! The <value:$28> I know would never do that!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6<value:$28> banished the darkness, and now the Dragon King thinks that same person will upset the Balance of the World? That's impossible. <value:$28> would never do such a thing!<end><if><value:$01><equal>7Wait! Now you're saying <value:$28> will destroy the world? Don't be ridiculous. I didn't fight beside someone who would even contemplate such a thing!<end><if><value:$01><equal>8<value:$28> banished the darkness, and now the Dragon King says that same person will upset the Balance of the World? That cannot be. The <value:$28> I know would never do such a thing.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9So now you think <value:$28> might destroy the world? Aren't you blaming the wrong person? The <value:$28> I know would never do anything so outrageous.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11<value:$28> banished the darkness, and now the Dragon King says that same person will upset the Balance of the World? That cannot be. <value:$28> saved me and would never do such a thing!<end><if><value:$01><equal>12<value:$28> banished the darkness, and now the Dragon King thinks that same person will upset the Balance of the World? No chance. The <value:$28> I know isn't that stupid.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13<value:$28> banished the darkness, and now the Dragon King says that same person will upset the Balance of the World? Impossible. The <value:$28> I know is no such fool.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Hold on! You think my sister's going to destroy the world now? Don't be ridiculous! My sister would never do that!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Wait a moment! Are you saying <value:$28> will now destroy the world? Impossible! I'm not fool enough to travel beside someone capable of such a thing!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Wait! Now you're saying <value:$28> will destroy the world? Don't be ridiculous! The <value:$28> I know would never do that!<end><if><value:$01><equal>16<value:$28> banished the darkness, and now the Dragon King says that same person will upset the Balance of the World? Impossible. I entrusted everything to someone far wiser than that!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Now just wait one moment! Are you suggesting that <value:$28> will destroy the world? Don't be absurd! The <value:$28> I know is not such an imbecile!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Wait! You're saying <value:$28> is going to destroy the world now? The <value:$28> Lulluantha knows would never, ever do that!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20<value:$28> banished the darkness, and now the Dragon King says that same person will upset the Balance of the World? Absurd. The <value:$28> I know is no such fool.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21Does the balance of power matter so much to you, Dragon King? <value:$28> saved the world from disaster! And now you would kill the world's savior merely for becoming too powerful, lest that power become the next threat?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Those who imperiled the world are dead, and none remain who can rival <value:$28>. So you fear that power may upset the balance and bring calamity upon the world? Just what I would expect from a dragon worshipper.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24Now that the High Priest is dead, you deem <value:$28> too powerful and dangerous to be left alive... So that is the Dragon King's judgment. Regrettably, I cannot comply, Lady Noel Silva.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25Once the threat to the world is gone, no longer needed―is that it? Or rather, too powerful, and therefore dangerous? I can't accept that!<end><if><value:$01><equal>23The Dragon King considers it insolent for humans to wield power―even while exploiting that power whenever the world is threatened. That strikes me as rather self-serving.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Power of such magnitude poses a danger to the world and must therefore be eliminated... That might be true of an ordinary person. But consider this, Dragon King: <value:$28> possesses a Soul every bit as great as that power!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192Stand back, little one. There is no need.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193N-no...! <value:$28> will become a threat to the world? I refuse to accept that! <value:$28> would never do such a thing!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Dragon King, have you lost your senses? Fighting for a reason like that is what's insane!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Wait! <value:$28> will become a threat capable of destroying the world? Impossible! <value:$28> isn't like that!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190<value:$28> will upset the Balance of the World? What nonsense! The <value:$28> who saved me could never be that kind of person!<end><value:$28> banished the darkness, and now the Dragon King says that same person will upset the Balance of the World? Never! The <value:$28> I know isn't that foolish!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2잠깐 기다려! 이번엔 <value:$28>가 세계의 위협이 된다는 거야? 말도 안 돼. 내가 아는 <value:$28>는 그런 사람이 아니야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3잠깐 기다려. 이번엔 <value:$28>가 세계의 위협이 된다는 게냐? 그럴 리 없다! 내가 아는 <value:$28>는 그런 인간이 아니다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4잠깐 기다려. 이번엔 <value:$28>가 세계를 멸망시킬 거라고 생각해? 그럴 리 없어! 내가 아는 <value:$28>는 그렇게 위험한 사람이 아니야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5잠깐 기다려! 이번엔 <value:$28>가 세계를 멸망시킬 거라고 생각하는 거야!? 말도 안 돼! 내가 아는 <value:$28>는 그런 짓을 할 녀석이 아니야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6어둠을 몰아낸 <value:$28>가 이번에는 세계의 균형을 무너뜨리는 존재가 된다… 용왕은 그렇게 생각하는 거야!? 그럴 리 없어. <value:$28>는 그런 짓을 할 사람이 아니니까!<end><if><value:$01><equal>7잠깐 기다려! 이번엔 <value:$28>가 세계를 멸망시킨다는 거야!? 말도 안 돼. 그런 생각을 하는 사람과 난 싸워 온 게 아니야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>8어둠을 몰아낸 <value:$28>가 이번에는 세계의 균형을 무너뜨리는 존재가 된다… 용왕은 그렇게 말하는 겁니까? 하지만 그럴 리 없습니다. 제가 아는 <value:$28>는 그런 짓을 할 사람이 아닙니다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9이번엔 <value:$28>가 세계를 멸망시킬 거라는 거야? 좀 엉뚱한 얘기 아냐? 내가 아는 <value:$28>는 그런 엄청난 짓을 할 사람이 아니야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11어둠을 몰아낸 <value:$28>가 이번에는 세계의 균형을 무너뜨리는 존재가 된다… 용왕은 그렇게 말하는 거지? 하지만 그럴 리 없어. 나를 구해 준 <value:$28>는 그런 짓을 할 사람이 아니니까!<end><if><value:$01><equal>12어둠을 몰아낸 <value:$28>가 이번에는 세계의 균형을 무너뜨리는 존재가 된다… 용왕은 그렇게 생각하는 건가? 하지만 그럴 리 없어. 내가 아는 <value:$28>는 그런 바보가 아니니까.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13어둠을 몰아낸 <value:$28>가 이번에는 세계의 균형을 무너뜨리는 존재가 된다… 용왕은 그렇게 말하는 건가? 그럴 리 없다. 내가 아는 <value:$28>는 그렇게 바보가 아니야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10잠깐 기다려! 이번엔 누나가 세계를 멸망시킬 거라고 생각하는 거야!? 말도 안 돼! 우리 누나는 그런 짓을 할 사람이 아니야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14잠깐 기다려! 이번엔 <value:$28>가 세계를 멸망시킨다는 거냐!? 그럴 리 없다! 난 그런 짓을 할 인간과 함께 여행할 만큼 바보가 아니다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15잠깐 기다려! 이번엔 <value:$28>가 세계를 멸망시킨다는 거야!? 말도 안 돼! 내가 아는 <value:$28>는 그런 짓을 할 사람이 아니야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>16어둠을 몰아낸 <value:$28>가 이번에는 세계의 균형을 무너뜨리는 존재가 된다… 용왕은 그렇게 말하는 건가? 그럴 리 없다. 내가 모든 것을 맡긴 인간은 그렇게 어리석지 않아!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17잠깐, 당신 기다려! 이번엔 <value:$28>가 세계를 멸망시킨다는 거야? 말도 안 돼! 아타쿠시가 아는 <value:$28>는 그렇게 멍청하지 않아!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19잠깐 기다려! 이번엔 <value:$28>가 세계를 멸망시킨다는 거야아? 루루안타가 아는 <value:$28>는 절대 그런 짓 안 해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20어둠을 몰아낸 <value:$28>가 이번에는 세계의 균형을 무너뜨리는 존재가 된다… 용왕은 그렇게 말하는 건가? 바보 같은 소리. 내가 아는 <value:$28>는 그렇게 어리석지 않다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21힘의 균형이 그렇게 중요하냐, 용왕! 세계의 위기를 구한 건 <value:$28>다! 그런데 너무 강해졌다는 이유로 다음 위협이 되기 전에 죽이겠다는 거냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26세계를 위기에 빠뜨린 자는 죽었고 <value:$28>에 맞설 자도 없어졌다. 이대로면 힘의 균형을 무너뜨려 세계에 재앙을 가져올 수 있다, 이건가. 용교도다운 생각이군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24대신관님이 돌아가신 지금, <value:$28> 님의 넘치는 힘은 위험하니 제거하라… 그것이 용왕님의 생각이군요. 유감이지만 따를 수 없습니다, 노엘 실바 님.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25세계의 위협이 사라지면 이제 필요 없다는 거야? 오히려 너무 강해서 위험하다는 거야? 그런 건 납득 못 해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>23용왕은 인간이 힘을 갖는 것을 불손하게 여긴다. 세계를 위협하는 존재가 나타났을 때 그 힘을 이용하면서도 말이지. 그건 좀 제멋대로 아닌가.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27너무 강대한 힘은 세계에 위험하니 제거해야 한다… 평범한 자라면 그렇겠지요. 하지만 용왕님, 생각해 주십시오. <value:$28>는 그 힘에 걸맞은 큰 소울을 지니고 있습니다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>192작은 자여, 물러나라. 그럴 필요는 없다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193그, 그럴 수가…! <value:$28> 씨가 세계를 위협하는 존재가 된다니!? 전 인정 못 해요! <value:$28> 씨는 그런 사람이 아니에요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194용왕이여, 정신이 나간 건가! 그런 이유로 싸우는 게 더 이상해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191잠깐 기다려! <value:$28>가 세계를 멸망시키는 존재가 된다니!? 그럴 리 없어! <value:$28>는 그런 사람이 아니야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190<value:$28>가 세계의 균형을 깨는 존재가 된다고? 그런 말도 안 되는 일이 있나! 나를 구한 <value:$28>가 그런 인간일 리 없다!<end>어둠을 몰아낸 <value:$28>가 이번에는 세계의 균형을 무너뜨리는 존재가 된다… 용왕은 그렇게 말하는 거야? 그럴 리 없어! 내가 아는 <value:$28>는 그렇게 어리석지 않아!<end>"

## 1330601
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は今や<line-break>地上最強の存在となりつつある。<line-break> <line-break>もし、その<value:$28>が<line-break>心変わりをしたら<line-break>誰がとめられるというのだ？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> is now on the verge of becoming the most powerful being on earth. If <value:$28> should ever change course, who could possibly stand in the way?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>은 이제 지상 최강의 존재가 되어 가고 있다. 만약 그 <value:$28>이 마음을 바꾼다면 누가 막을 수 있겠는가?<end>"

## 1330602
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうなる前に排除せよとの<line-break>竜王様の賢明なるご判断だ。<line-break>　<line-break>さぁ、ノエル、カフィン、レイヴン、<line-break>竜王様の加護を受けし者たちよ。<line-break>今こそ、その恩に報いるときだ。<end>"
EN: "The Dragon King has wisely decreed that the threat be eliminated before that can happen. Come, Noel, Coffin, Raven―you who have received the Dragon King's blessing. The time has come to repay that grace.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 되기 전에 제거하라는 용왕님의 현명한 판단이다. 자, 노엘, 카핀, 레이븐. 용왕님의 가호를 받은 자들이여. 지금이야말로 그 은혜에 보답할 때다.<end>"

## 1330603
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺はノエルに従うと決めている。<line-break>ノエルの前に立つ敵は<line-break>排除する。…それだけだ。<end>"
EN: "I have chosen to follow Noel. Anyone who stands against her will be eliminated. That is all.<end>"
KO: "…나는 노엘을 따르기로 정했다. 노엘 앞을 막는 적은 제거한다. …그뿐이다.<end>"

## 1330604
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなことになってしまって<line-break>残念だけど、<line-break>全力でいかせてもらうわ！<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry it's come to this, but I won't hold back!<end>"
KO: "이렇게 돼서 유감이지만, 전력으로 상대해 주겠어!<end>"

## 1330605
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カフィンは下がってて。<end>"
EN: "Coffin, stay back.<end>"
KO: "카핀은 물러나 있어.<end>"

## 1330606
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待って！<line-break>あたしもノエルの仲間よ！<line-break>一緒に戦うわ！<end>"
EN: "Wait! I'm Noel's companion too! I'll fight beside her!<end>"
KO: "기다려! 나도 노엘의 동료야! 함께 싸울 거야!<end>"

## 1330607
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…レムオンさんは<line-break>あなたの娘、カミラの子…。<line-break>あなたのお孫さんなんでしょう？<line-break>…忍びないの、お願い。<line-break>これくらいのことなら、<line-break>竜王様も許してくださるわ。<end>"
EN: "Lemghon is Camilla's son―your daughter's child. That makes him your grandson, doesn't it? I can't bear this. Please. Surely the Dragon King will forgive this much.<end>"
KO: "…레무온 씨는 당신의 딸 카밀라의 아이…. 당신 손자잖아요? …차마 못 보겠어요, 부탁이에요. 이 정도라면 용왕님도 용서해 주실 거예요.<end>"

## 1330608
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ノエル。<end>"
EN: "Noel...<end>"
KO: "…노엘.<end>"

## 1330609
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の実の母の…母親…が…<line-break>カフィンだっただと…？<end>"
EN: "Coffin... was my birth mother's mother?<end>"
KO: "내 친어머니의… 어머니가… 카핀이었다고…?<end>"

## 1330610
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル…ありがとう。<line-break>でも、気遣いだけで十分。<line-break><line-break>ソウルイーターと戦ったあの時から、<line-break>あたしもあなたと運命を<line-break>ともにすることになったのだもの！<end>"
EN: "Noel... thank you. But knowing you care is enough. Ever since we fought the Soul Eater, my fate has been bound to yours!<end>"
KO: "노엘… 고마워. 하지만 마음만으로 충분해. 소울 이터와 싸웠던 그때부터 나도 너와 운명을 함께하게 됐으니까!<end>"

## 1330611
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あえて祖母とは呼ばぬ。<end>"
EN: "I will not call you Grandmother.<end>"
KO: "굳이 할머니라고 부르진 않겠다.<end>"

## 1330612
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、ひとりの人として、<line-break>同じダルケニスとして、<line-break><line-break>その堂々とした態度には、<line-break>こちらも全力をもって応えよう！<end>"
EN: "But as one person to another, and as a fellow Darkenith, I will honor that proud resolve by giving you everything I have!<end>"
KO: "하지만 한 사람으로서, 같은 다르케니스로서 그 당당한 태도에는 나도 전력을 다해 응하겠다!<end>"

## 1330613
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ…ありがたいね。<line-break><line-break><line-break>ノエル！<end>"
EN: "Heh... I'm grateful. Noel!<end>"
KO: "후후… 고맙군. 노엘!<end>"

## 1330614
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい！<end>"
EN: "Yes!<end>"
KO: "네!<end>"

## 1330615
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん！<line-break>ノエルの最後の剣、<line-break>受けとめてください！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! Take Noel's final strike!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨! 노엘의 마지막 검을 받아 주세요!<end>"

## 1330616
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うぅ…、やっぱり<line-break>つよい…。<end>"
EN: "Ugh... You really are strong...<end>"
KO: "으으…, 역시 강해….<end>"

## 1330617
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル、わかっているだろうな。<line-break>お前に敗北は許されてはいない。<line-break>お前が退いたそのときは…。<end>"
EN: "Noel, you understand, don't you? You are not permitted to lose. The moment you retreat...<end>"
KO: "노엘, 알고 있겠지. 네게 패배는 허용되지 않는다. 네가 물러나는 순간에는….<end>"

## 1330618
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめて、ナーシェス。<line-break><value:$28>さんに<line-break>そのことは言わないで！<end>"
EN: "Stop, Narscess! Don't tell <value:$28> about that!<end>"
KO: "그만해, 나셰스. <value:$28>에게 그 얘기는 하지 마!<end>"

## 1330619
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、よいではないか。<line-break><value:$28>にも<line-break>聞かせてやるとしよう。　　　　　<line-break>そうすれば、人間の持つ<line-break>哀れみの感情とやらで、<line-break>竜王様に従う気になるかもしれん。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Why not? Let <value:$28> hear it. Perhaps that vaunted human capacity for pity will inspire obedience to the Dragon King.<end>"
KO: "후후, 좋지 않은가. <value:$28>에게도 들려주지. 그러면 인간이 가진 연민이라는 감정으로 용왕님을 따를 마음이 생길지도 모르니.<end>"

## 1330620
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界の均衡を崩すほどに<line-break>成長してしまった<line-break>自らの命を絶つ気にな。フフ…。<end>"
EN: "It may even move you to take your own life―the life that has grown powerful enough to upset the Balance of the World. Heh...<end>"
KO: "세계의 균형을 무너뜨릴 만큼 성장해 버린 자신의 목숨을 끊을 생각이었지. 후후….<end>"

## 1330621
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>お前を慕っていたノエルが<line-break>ここに来た理由がわかるか？<line-break>お前が死なない限り、<line-break>カフィンとレイヴンが死人に<line-break>戻ってしまうからだ！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, do you know why Noel, who cared so deeply for you, came here? Because unless you die, Coffin and Raven will be dead once more!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 너를 따르던 노엘이 왜 여기 왔는지 아나? 네가 죽지 않는 한 카핀과 레이븐이 다시 죽은 자로 돌아가 버리기 때문이다!<end>"

## 1330622
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カフィンも、レイヴンも、<line-break>一度死んでいる。竜王様のお力で<line-break>ソウルを体に留めているにすぎぬ。<line-break>竜王様のご意志に沿えないとき、<line-break>それがどうなるか…。<line-break>フフフ…。<end>"
EN: "Coffin and Raven have both died once already. The Dragon King's power is all that binds their Souls to their bodies. And if they should defy the Dragon King's will... Heh heh heh...<end>"
KO: "카핀도 레이븐도 한 번 죽었다. 용왕님의 힘으로 소울을 육체에 붙들어 두고 있을 뿐이지. 용왕님의 뜻에 따르지 않을 때 그것이 어떻게 될지는…. 후후후….<end>"

## 1330623
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、どうする？<line-break>お前が死ななければ、<line-break>このふたりは死ぬぞ！<end>"
EN: "Well? What will you do? Unless you die, these two will!<end>"
KO: "자, 어떻게 할래? 네가 죽지 않으면 이 둘이 죽는다!<end>"

## 1330624
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ば、バカな！<line-break>貴様、正気か…！？　　<line-break>　<line-break>私に刃向かうのは<line-break>竜王様に剣を向けるのと<line-break>同じことだぞ！<end>"
EN: "I-impossible! Have you lost your mind...?! To defy me is no different from raising your sword against the Dragon King!<end>"
KO: "마, 말도 안 돼! 네놈, 제정신이냐…!? 내게 대드는 것은 용왕님께 칼을 겨누는 것과 같은 일이다!<end>"

## 1330625
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あんたの言いぐさは<line-break>聞くに耐えないんでな。<line-break>　<line-break>殺人機械だった俺を、<line-break>助け出してくれたことには感謝してる。<line-break>だが、ノエルを悲しませるのは許さん。<end>"
EN: "I've heard enough of your vile talk. I'm grateful that you rescued me when I was nothing but a killing machine. But I will not allow you to make Noel suffer.<end>"
KO: "…네 말투는 도저히 듣고 있을 수가 없군. 살인 기계였던 나를 구해 준 건 고맙게 생각한다. 하지만 노엘을 슬프게 하는 건 용서 못 해.<end>"

## 1330626
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レイヴン！<end>"
EN: "Raven!<end>"
KO: "레이븐!<end>"

## 1330627
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル…。<line-break>今の俺はお前の重荷にすぎない。<line-break>それは…俺には許されないことなのだ。<line-break>お前のそばにいるようになって、<line-break>俺は人間に戻ることができた。<line-break>感謝している。…さらばだ。<end>"
EN: "Noel... As I am now, I am nothing but a burden to you. I cannot allow that. At your side, I became human again. Thank you... Farewell.<end>"
KO: "노엘…. 지금의 나는 네게 짐일 뿐이다. 그건… 내겐 허락할 수 없는 일이야. 네 곁에 있게 된 뒤 나는 인간으로 돌아올 수 있었다. 감사한다. …잘 있어라.<end>"

## 1330628
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル…。<line-break>あたしたち…もう…いくわ…<end>"
EN: "Noel... It's time for us... to go...<end>"
KO: "노엘…. 우리… 이제… 갈게….<end>"

## 1330629
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カフィン！<end>"
EN: "Coffin!<end>"
KO: "카핀!<end>"
