## 1330632
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、戻ろう。<line-break>戦いが終わっても、<line-break>やらねばならぬことは残っている。<end>"
EN: "Come. Let us return. The battle may be over, but there is still work to be done.<end>"
KO: "자, 돌아가자. 싸움이 끝나도 해야 할 일은 남아 있다.<end>"

## 1330633
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とりあえず、闇の侵攻は、<line-break>くい止められましたね。<line-break>よくやってくれました。<line-break>でも、これであなたの旅が、<line-break>終わったわけではありません。<end>"
EN: "For now, the advance of darkness has been halted. You have done well. But your journey is not over yet.<end>"
KO: "일단 어둠의 침공은 막았습니다. 잘해 주셨어요. 하지만 이걸로 당신의 여행이 끝난 건 아닙니다.<end>"

## 1330634
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。これからあなたは、<line-break>どんな道を進むのでしょうね？<line-break>楽しみです。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. I wonder what path you will follow from here. I look forward to finding out.<end>"
KO: "후후후. 앞으로 당신은 어떤 길을 걷게 될까요? 기대되네요.<end>"

## 1330635
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、戻りましょうか。<line-break>無事な姿をみんなに見せないと。<line-break>さあ、行きましょう。<end>"
EN: "Shall we head back, then? Everyone will want to see that you're safe. Come, let's go.<end>"
KO: "그럼 돌아갈까요. 무사한 모습을 모두에게 보여 줘야죠. 자, 갑시다.<end>"

## 1330636
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　神聖暦<value:$24>年<value:$25>月　　　　<line-break>後に多くの吟遊詩人に歌われるこの騒乱の後<line-break>　　ディンガル帝国とロストール王国は　　<line-break>　　　　　和平条約を締結した。　　　　　<end>"
EN: "In Year <value:$24>, Month <value:$25> of the Sacred Era, after this upheaval―later celebrated in the songs of many bards―the Dyneskal Empire and the Kingdom of Rostorl concluded a peace treaty.<end>"
KO: "신성력 <value:$24>년 <value:$25>월 후에 수많은 음유시인이 노래하게 될 이 소란 뒤 딩갈 제국과 로스톨 왕국은 평화조약을 체결했다.<end>"

## 1330637
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしか…？<line-break>わしはイオンズ。<line-break>ここの神官じゃ。<end>"
EN: "Me? I'm Ions. I'm the priest here.<end>"
KO: "나 말인가…? 나는 이온즈. 이곳의 신관이다.<end>"

## 1330638
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "天空神の祝福が<line-break>汝にあらんことを…<end>"
EN: "May the Sky God's blessing be upon you...<end>"
KO: "천공신의 축복이 그대에게 있기를….<end>"

## 1330639
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、こういうのが、ここで<line-break>一番似合わぬ神官が<line-break>わしじゃわな。ハハハ<end>"
EN: "Well, I'm the priest here least suited to this sort of thing. Ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 이런 자리가 여기서 가장 어울리지 않는 신관이 바로 나겠지. 하하하.<end>"

## 1330640
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イオンズ様、大変でございます。<line-break>お力を！<end>"
EN: "Lord Ions, there's an emergency. Please help!<end>"
KO: "이온즈 님, 큰일입니다. 힘을 빌려주십시오!<end>"

## 1330641
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、またかいな。<line-break>今行く。<end>"
EN: "Oh, not again. I'm coming.<end>"
KO: "아, 또야? 지금 가.<end>"

## 1330642
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃぁな。<end>"
EN: "Well, see you.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 잘 있어.<end>"

## 1330643
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こう見えてな、<line-break>わしは若いころ、傭兵だったんじゃ。<end>"
EN: "I may not look it, but I was a mercenary in my younger days.<end>"
KO: "이래 보여도 말이지, 난 젊었을 때 용병이었어.<end>"

## 1330644
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "荒くれの無頼でな。<line-break>ずいぶん、ひどいこともしとった。<line-break>自分の腕以外は、何も信じとらなんだ。<line-break>流れ流れて、<line-break>この街の傭兵をやっとった時に、<line-break>アンギルダンというオヤジに会うてな。<end>"
EN: "A lawless brute, at that. I did some terrible things. Trusted nothing but my own skill. I drifted from place to place until I came here and took work as a mercenary. That was when I met an old fellow named Angeerdan.<end>"
KO: "거친 무뢰한이었지. 꽤 심한 짓도 많이 했어. 자기 실력 외에는 아무것도 믿지 않았지. 떠돌다 이 도시에서 용병을 하던 때 앙길단이라는 남자를 만났어.<end>"

## 1330645
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なにか、すごい台詞を聞かされてな。<line-break>瞬間、今までのつまらん人生すべてが<line-break>はがれ落ちる気がして、神官になった。<end>"
EN: "He said something profound to me. In that instant, I felt my whole worthless life falling away, and I became a priest.<end>"
KO: "굉장한 말을 한마디 들었거든. 순간 지금까지의 시시한 인생이 전부 벗겨져 나가는 느낌이 들어서 신관이 됐어.<end>"

## 1330646
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どんなすごいことを言われたか、<line-break>もう覚えとらんのだがな。<end>"
EN: "Mind you, I can't remember what those profound words were anymore.<end>"
KO: "무슨 대단한 말을 들었는지는 이제 기억도 안 나지만 말이야.<end>"

## 1330647
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、そんなことはどうでもええ。<line-break>わしは、こうしてここにおる。<line-break>それで十分なのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Well, never mind all that. Here I am, and that is enough.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그런 건 아무래도 좋다. 나는 이렇게 여기에 있다. 그것으로 충분하다.<end>"

## 1330648
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神官と傭兵だらけ…<line-break>いやはや、殺風景な町じゃ。<end>"
EN: "Nothing but priests and mercenaries... Good grief, what a dreary town.<end>"
KO: "신관과 용병뿐이군…. 참 삭막한 마을이야.<end>"

## 1330649
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "若いころ、傭兵としてここに来たとき<line-break>こんな所に攻めてくる国があるか、<line-break>無駄なことじゃい、と思うたがな。<line-break>実際、攻め入ろうとする国は存在する。<line-break>その理由もわかっとる。いやはや、<line-break>つまらん力は持つもんではないのう。<end>"
EN: "When I first came here as a young mercenary, I wondered what country would ever attack a place like this. It all seemed rather pointless. Yet there are countries that truly would invade, and I know why. Good grief. Some powers are more trouble than they are worth.<end>"
KO: "젊었을 때 용병으로 여기 왔을 땐 이런 곳을 공격할 나라가 있겠냐, 쓸데없는 짓이라 생각했지. 하지만 실제로 공격하려는 나라는 존재한다. 이유도 알고 있어. 이런 힘은 괜히 가진 게 아니구나.<end>"

## 1330650
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしは、若いころは無頼の傭兵でな。<line-break>無論、神官の勉強なんぞは<line-break>これっぽちもしとらんかった。<end>"
EN: "I was a lawless mercenary in my youth. Naturally, I hadn't studied for the priesthood at all.<end>"
KO: "나는 젊을 적 무뢰한 용병이었네. 물론 신관 공부 따윈 눈곱만큼도 하지 않았지.<end>"

## 1330651
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃから、オッサンになってから、<line-break>死に物狂いに勉強してな。<line-break>それこそ、人の３倍は努力した。<line-break>努力は報われ、ま、わしも、<line-break>並以上の神官になれたわけじゃ。<line-break>なんでも、努力はするもんじゃな。<end>"
EN: "So when I was already middle-aged, I studied as if my life depended on it. I put in three times the effort of anyone else. That hard work paid off, and I became a better-than-average priest. Whatever you do, it's worth making the effort.<end>"
KO: "그래서 나이 든 뒤 죽기 살기로 공부했지. 남들 세 배는 노력했어. 노력은 보답을 받아 나도 평균 이상은 되는 신관이 됐지. 뭐든 노력은 하고 볼 일이야.<end>"

## 1330652
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近はいにしえの怪物たちの目覚めが<line-break>多うてな。あちこちから呼び出される。<line-break>封士イオンズ様も仕事が多いわ。<end>"
EN: "Ancient monsters have been awakening with troubling frequency of late, so I am summoned here, there and everywhere. Lord Ions the Sealer is a busy man.<end>"
KO: "요즘 고대 괴물들이 많이 깨어나서 여기저기서 불려 다닌다네. 봉사 이온즈 님도 일이 많아.<end>"

## 1330653
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "商売繁盛で結構なことじゃが、<line-break>気に入らんな。<line-break>封じればすむことではないのに…<line-break>もっと気に入らんのは<line-break>怪物どもを目覚めさせ、その敵意を<line-break>煽っとる奴がいるということじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Business may be booming, but I do not like it. Sealing them away solves nothing... Worse still, someone is awakening these monsters and stoking their hostility.<end>"
KO: "장사가 잘되는 건 좋은 일이지만 마음에 안 드는군. 봉인하면 끝나는 일이 아닌데…. 더 마음에 안 드는 건 괴물들을 깨우고 그 적의를 부추기는 녀석이 있다는 거야.<end>"

## 1330654
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴らがそのようなことをするたび、<line-break>わしの夢…怪物どもとの共存という夢は<line-break>千年単位で遠ざかりよるからな。<line-break>対峙した怪物どもの暗くすさんだ<line-break>心をのぞくだに、痛うて痛うて、<line-break>わしまで泣きたくなってきよる。<end>"
EN: "Every time they do this, my dream of coexisting with the monsters slips millennia farther out of reach. Just glimpsing the dark, ravaged hearts of the monsters I confront hurts so much, it makes me want to weep.<end>"
KO: "놈들이 그런 짓을 할 때마다 내 꿈… 괴물들과 공존한다는 꿈은 천 년씩 멀어져 간다. 맞선 괴물들의 어둡고 황폐한 마음을 들여다보는 것만으로도 가슴이 아파서 나까지 울고 싶어질 지경이야.<end>"

## 1330655
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと法力に優れとるゆえに、<line-break>人はわしを封士様と呼んでくれよる。<end>"
EN: "Because I happen to possess a little more divine power than most, people are kind enough to call me Lord Sealer.<end>"
KO: "법력이 조금 뛰어나다고 사람들이 나를 봉사님이라 부르는구먼.<end>"

## 1330656
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "封士とは、元を正せば、<line-break>世界の凶つ物を封印して回った<line-break>施文院の祖レオニックのあだ名よ。<line-break>封士レオニックは、<line-break>非常に強力な究極の神聖魔法を<line-break>遺跡に封じたという伝説を残しとってな<end>"
EN: "The title “Sealer” was originally a sobriquet given to Leonick, founder of the Inscription Order, who traveled the world sealing away its baleful beings. Legend holds that Leonick the Sealer confined an immensely powerful form of ultimate sacred magic within some ruins.<end>"
KO: "봉사라는 건 원래 세계의 흉물을 봉인하며 돌아다닌 시문원에서 비롯된 것이다.<end>"

## 1330657
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしも傭兵時代に探したもんじゃが…<line-break>まさか、そのレオニックと同じ<line-break>あだ名をもらうことになるとはのう。<end>"
EN: "I searched for it myself in my mercenary days... Never imagined I would one day bear the same sobriquet as Leonick.<end>"
KO: "나도 용병 시절에 찾아다닌 적이 있었는데… 설마 그 레오닉과 같은 별명을 얻게 될 줄이야.<end>"

## 1330658
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、もう少しゆっくりさせてくれ。<line-break>しばらくしたら、神殿に戻って、<line-break>神官様らしく行儀よう座っとるでな。<end>"
EN: "Well, let me rest a little longer. After a while, I'll go back to the temple and sit there all prim and proper, like a priest should.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 조금만 더 쉬게 해 주게. 잠시 뒤엔 신전으로 돌아가 신관답게 얌전히 앉아 있을 테니.<end>"

## 1330659
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、こいつか。こいつはな…。<end>"
EN: "Ah, this one. Well...<end>"
KO: "아아, 이 녀석인가. 이 녀석은 말이지….<end>"

## 1330660
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギー、オレ、イズキヤル。<line-break>イオンズノ、トモ。<end>"
EN: "Ghee. I'm Izkiyal. Ions's friend.<end>"
KO: "기, 나 이즈키얄. 이온즈 친구.<end>"

## 1330661
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>わしの生涯最大の強敵だった。<line-break>　<line-break>なりは小さくしとるが、<line-break>こいつはティラの娘と呼ばれる<line-break>いにしえの闇の聖母の怪物だ。<end>"
EN: "And the greatest foe I ever faced. Small as Izkiyal looks now, this is an ancient Dark Mother monster known as the Daughter of Tira.<end>"
KO: "그리고 내 평생 최대의 강적이었지. 모습은 작아졌지만, 이 녀석은 티라의 딸이라 불리는 고대의 어둠의 성모 괴물이다.<end>"

## 1330662
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつが復活したとき　　　　　　<line-break>わしがこいつと戦い、<line-break>かろうじて勝った。<line-break>わしはこいつに<line-break>とどめを刺そうとして、考えた。<line-break>怪物は悪なのか？　とな。<end>"
EN: "When Izkiyal came back to life, we fought, and I barely won. As I was about to deliver the final blow, a thought came to me: Is a monster evil?<end>"
KO: "이 녀석이 부활했을 때 내가 싸워 간신히 이겼다. 마무리를 지으려다 생각했지. 괴물은 악인가, 하고.<end>"

## 1330663
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしはすぐに否と思うた。　　　　<line-break>人間に悪人も善人もないように、<line-break>怪物だから悪ということはない。<line-break>ならばどうする？<line-break>このまま生かしておけば、こいつは<line-break>傷が癒えた日に里を襲うだろう。<end>"
EN: "I knew at once the answer was no. Just as humans cannot simply be divided into good and evil, being a monster does not make one evil. But then what? If I let Izkiyal live, the village would be attacked as soon as those wounds healed.<end>"
KO: "나는 곧 아니라고 생각했다. 인간에게 악인과 선인이 있듯 괴물이라고 해서 악한 것은 아니다. 그렇다면 어찌해야 하나? 이대로 살려 두면 이놈은 상처가 나은 날 마을을 덮칠 것이다.<end>"

## 1330664
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その前に　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>こいつの牙と爪を見た者が<line-break>こいつに戦いを挑むかもしれぬ。<line-break>争えば憎しみだけが残り、<line-break>封じれば恨みだけが先送りされる。<end>"
EN: "Even before that, someone who saw those fangs and claws might challenge Izkiyal to a fight. Fighting leaves only hatred, while sealing a creature away merely postpones resentment.<end>"
KO: "그 전에 이 녀석의 송곳니와 발톱을 본 자가 싸움을 걸지도 모른다. 싸우면 증오만 남고, 봉인하면 원한만 뒤로 미뤄질 뿐이지.<end>"

## 1330665
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "殺せば、生まれたことの悲しみと<line-break>虚無だけが勝利する。<line-break>わしは答えが出せなんだ。<line-break>じゃから、わしは、<line-break>話しおうてみることにした。<end>"
EN: "If I killed Izkiyal, only the sorrow of having been born and the Void would triumph. I could find no answer. So I decided to try talking things through.<end>"
KO: "죽이면 태어난 슬픔과 허무만이 승리한다. 나는 답을 내지 못했지. 그래서 이야기해 보기로 했다.<end>"

## 1330666
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "傷ついたこいつが眠る洞窟へ何度も　　<line-break>足を運んだ。こいつは怒りと悲しみに<line-break>むしばまれておった。<line-break>こいつの人生は、追われ、傷つけられ、<line-break>封じられ、恨み、殺し、破壊することの<line-break>繰り返しじゃった。<end>"
EN: "Again and again, I went to the cave where the wounded Izkiyal slept. Anger and sorrow were eating away at that creature. Izkiyal's life had been an endless cycle of being hunted, wounded, and sealed away―of hatred, killing, and destruction.<end>"
KO: "상처 입은 이 녀석이 잠든 동굴에 몇 번이나 찾아갔지. 이 녀석은 분노와 슬픔에 좀먹히고 있었네. 이 녀석의 삶은 쫓기고, 상처 입고, 봉인되고, 원망하고, 죽이고, 파괴하는 일의 반복이었지.<end>"

## 1330667
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしは小さくなる魔法を教えた。　　　<line-break>せめて、姿が人の恐怖を誘わなければ、<line-break>この悪い輪から逃れられると思ってな。<line-break>最後には、こいつは<line-break>わしの申し出を受け入れてくれよった。<end>"
EN: "I taught Izkiyal a spell for becoming small. I thought that if that form no longer inspired fear in people, there might at least be a way out of that vicious cycle. In the end, Izkiyal accepted my offer.<end>"
KO: "나는 작아지는 마법을 가르쳐 줬다. 적어도 모습이 사람들에게 공포를 주지 않는다면 이 나쁜 악순환에서 벗어날 수 있으리라 생각했지. 마지막엔 이 녀석도 내 제안을 받아들였어.<end>"

## 1330668
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ソノマエニ、　　　　　　<line-break>ダイブヤリアッタガ、ナ。<end>"
EN: "We fought plenty before that, though.<end>"
KO: "그전에 꽤 한바탕 했지만 말이지.<end>"

## 1330669
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつは今、自分の意志で　　　　<line-break>こうして小さくなっておる。<line-break>　　<line-break>じゃが、その気になればいつでも、<line-break>もとの姿になって、<line-break>この町ぐらい、軽く焼き尽くせる。<end>"
EN: "Izkiyal now takes this small form by choice. But if so inclined, Izkiyal could return to the original form at any time and burn a town this size to ashes with ease.<end>"
KO: "이 녀석은 지금 자기 의지로 이렇게 작아져 있다. 하지만 마음만 먹으면 언제든 원래 모습으로 돌아가 이 도시 정도는 가볍게 태워 버릴 수 있어.<end>"

## 1330670
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギー、ソントキャ、オマエガ、<line-break>オレヲ、タイジシテクレンダロ？<end>"
EN: "Ghee. If that happens, you'll kill me, won't you?<end>"
KO: "기-, 그때는, 네가 나를 퇴치해 주겠지?<end>"

## 1330671
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、そうじゃ。　　<line-break>ま、そういう奴だ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. That's right. Well, that's Izkiyal for you.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그렇다. 뭐, 그런 녀석이지.<end>"

## 1330672
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギー、　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>オレ、コドモタチト、アソブー！<end>"
EN: "Ghee! I'm going to play with the children!<end>"
KO: "기-, 나, 아이들이랑, 놀 거야-!<end>"

## 1330673
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギー、コンドハ、オレガ、オニカ？<end>"
EN: "Ghee, am I it this time?<end>"
KO: "기, 이번엔 내가 귀신인가?<end>"

## 1330674
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん、そーだよ、イズキヤル。<line-break>それ、逃げろ！<end>"
EN: "Yeah, that's right, Izkiyal. Now run!<end>"
KO: "응, 맞아, 이즈키얄. 자, 도망쳐!<end>"

## 1330675
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギー！　ヨシ。マテー！<end>"
EN: "Ghee! Okay! Wait up!<end>"
KO: "기이! 좋아. 기다려!<end>"

## 1330676
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギー、コンドハ、コイツガ、オニカ？<end>"
EN: "Ghee, is this one it this time?<end>"

## 1330678
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1330679
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アルノートゥンでジラーク将軍が<line-break>反旗を翻したんだってね。<end>"
EN: "I hear General Zsilak has rebelled in Alnortun.<end>"
KO: "알노툰에서 지라크 장군이 반기를 들었다면서.<end>"

## 1330680
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人間優先の社会に反抗、決起。<line-break>コーンスが支配するコーンスのための<line-break>世界を作るんだってさ…<end>"
EN: "He's risen up against a society that puts humans first. Says he'll build a world ruled by the Konsu, for the Konsu...<end>"
KO: "인간 우선 사회에 반발해서 봉기. 콘스가 지배하는 콘스를 위한 세계를 만들겠다는군….<end>"

## 1330681
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナッジは参加したんだよね。<end>"
EN: "Nadge joined them, didn't he?<end>"
KO: "나지는 참가했지.<end>"

## 1330682
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "続々とコーンスがアルノートゥンに<line-break>集まってるって。<end>"
EN: "They say Konsu are flocking to Alnortun one after another.<end>"
KO: "콘스가 잇따라 알노툰으로 모이고 있대.<end>"

## 1330683
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もしも自分がコーンスだったら、<line-break>ぼくも参加したかもしれないよ。<end>"
EN: "If I were a Konsu, I might have joined them too.<end>"
KO: "내가 콘스였다면 나도 참가했을지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1330684
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぼくの額に<line-break>つけぼくろがついてるでしょ？<line-break>　<line-break>ルルアンタにも<line-break>他のすべてのリルビーにもついてる…。<line-break>これ…とれないんだ。<end>"
EN: "See the false mole on my forehead? Lulluantha has one too. So does every Lilubee... This... won't come off.<end>"
KO: "내 이마에 붙인 점이 있지? 루루안타도, 다른 모든 릴비도 붙어 있어… 이거… 안 떨어져.<end>"

## 1330685
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つけぼくろがついてるでしょ？<line-break>他のすべてのリルビーにもついてる…。<line-break>　<line-break>これ…とれないんだ。<end>"
EN: "See this false mole? Every Lilubee has one... It... won't come off.<end>"
KO: "붙이는 점이 있지? 다른 모든 릴비에게도 있어…. 이거… 안 떨어져.<end>"

## 1330686
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぼくたちリルビーは人間の子供と<line-break>ほとんど変わらない外見をしてる。<end>"
EN: "We Lilubees look almost exactly like human children.<end>"
KO: "우리 릴비는 인간 아이와 거의 다르지 않은 모습이야.<end>"

## 1330687
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このことを利用して<line-break>ひどい悪事を行ったリルビーが、<line-break>昔、いたんだって。<line-break>人間たちは激怒して、リルビーを<line-break>皆殺しにしようとしたんだ。<line-break>ちょうどダルケニスにしたみたいにね。<end>"
EN: "They say that long ago, a Lilubee took advantage of that resemblance to commit terrible crimes. The humans were furious and tried to exterminate every Lilubee―just as they did to the Darkenith.<end>"
KO: "이걸 이용해 끔찍한 악행을 저지른 릴비가 옛날에 있었대. 인간들은 격노해서 릴비를 몰살하려 했어. 딱 다르케니스에게 했던 것처럼.<end>"

## 1330688
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これを回避するために、リルビーは、<line-break>今後、ニ度とこんなことを<line-break>起こさないことを誓い、<line-break>人間の子供と区別するために、<line-break>すべてのリルビーにつけぼくろを<line-break>埋め込むことで人間と和解したんだ。<end>"
EN: "To avert that, the Lilubees swore nothing like it would ever happen again. They made peace with the humans by agreeing to have a false mole implanted in every Lilubee, so we could be told apart from human children.<end>"
KO: "이를 피하기 위해 릴비는 앞으로 두 번 다시 이런 일을 일으키지 않겠다고 맹세하고, 인간 아이와 구별하기 위해 모든 릴비에게 붙임점을 심는 것으로 인간과 화해했어.<end>"

## 1330689
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…これ、結構、痛いんだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、ぼくが痛がることを知りながら<line-break>これを埋め込まなくっちゃいけなかった<line-break>父さんと母さんの心の方が、<end>"
EN: "This actually hurts quite a bit. But my father and mother knew it would hurt me, and they still had to implant it. The pain they felt must have been...<end>"
KO: "…이거 꽤 아파. 하지만 내가 아파할 걸 알면서도 이걸 심어야 했던 아버지와 어머니의 마음이 더…<end>"

## 1330690
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…たぶん、何千倍も、何万倍も<line-break>痛んだだろうなって、<line-break>ぼく、思うよ。<line-break>リルビーの変わった風習だって、<line-break>今となっちゃ、笑う人間もいるけれど、<line-break>これは、人間優位の社会の風習なんだ。<end>"
EN: "...I think it must have hurt them thousands, even tens of thousands, of times more. Some humans laugh at it now as a strange Lilubee custom, but it's a custom born of a society where humans come first.<end>"
KO: "…아마 수천 배, 수만 배는 더 아팠을 거라고 생각해. 릴비의 이상한 풍습이라고 지금은 비웃는 사람도 있지만, 이건 인간 우위 사회가 만든 풍습이야.<end>"

## 1330691
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "旅好きなせいもあるけど、<line-break>ぼくは子供を持ちたくない。<line-break>種族平等を唱えてたネメアも死んだし。<end>"
EN: "Partly because I love to travel, I suppose, but I don't want children. And Nemea, who called for equality among the races, is dead now.<end>"
KO: "여행을 좋아해서이기도 하지만 난 아이를 갖고 싶지 않아. 종족 평등을 외치던 네메아도 죽었고.<end>"

## 1330692
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは違う<end>"
EN: "That's not true.<end>"
KO: "그건 아니야<end>"

## 1330693
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "平等の世界を作ろう<end>"
EN: "Let's build a world of equality.<end>"
KO: "평등한 세상을 만들자<end>"

## 1330694
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうかな？でも、ぼくたちは<line-break>つけぼくろをつけている。<line-break>それは事実だよ。<line-break>人間と一緒に生きていくために、<line-break>ぼくたちだって、<line-break>つらい目に遭っているんだ。<end>"
EN: "Do you think so? But we do wear these false moles. That's a fact. We've had to suffer too, just so we can live alongside humans.<end>"
KO: "그럴까? 하지만 우리 릴비는 점을 붙이고 있어. 그건 사실이야. 인간과 함께 살아가기 위해 우리도 힘든 일을 겪고 있어.<end>"

## 1330695
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうやって？<line-break>何か、方法があるの？<end>"
EN: "How? Is there some way to do it?<end>"
KO: "어떻게? 무슨 방법이 있는 거야?<end>"

## 1330696
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、いいんだよ、それで。<line-break>そう思ってくれているだけで十分だよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. That's all right. Just knowing you feel that way is enough.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그걸로 됐어. 그렇게 생각해 주는 것만으로 충분해.<end>"

## 1330697
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうやって、１人１人が<line-break>考えてくれなきゃ、<line-break>種族の平等なんて実現しないから、さ。<line-break>じゃね。ありがとう、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Equality among the races can't become a reality unless each person starts thinking that way. See you. Thanks, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 한 사람 한 사람이 생각해 주지 않으면 종족의 평등 같은 건 실현되지 않으니까. 그럼, 고마워, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1330698
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしても入る<end>"
EN: "I have to go in<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이 있어도 들어간다<end>"

## 1330699
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしても入らなくては<line-break>ならないのですか？<end>"
EN: "Do you absolutely have to go in?<end>"
KO: "정말 꼭 들어가야 합니까?<end>"

## 1330700
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかりました<line-break>こちらに古い坑道のような洞窟があります<line-break>そこから中に入れるかも知れません<line-break>こっそり案内してあげましょう<line-break>でも、後悔しても知りませんよ？<end>"
EN: "Very well. There is a cave over here that looks like an old mine. You may be able to get inside through it. I'll secretly show you the way. But don't blame me if you regret it.<end>"
KO: "…알겠습니다. 이쪽에 오래된 갱도 같은 동굴이 있습니다. 거기서 안으로 들어갈 수 있을지도 몰라요. 몰래 안내해 드리죠. 하지만 후회해도 모릅니다?<end>"

## 1330701
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それがいいですよ<end>"
EN: "That's for the best.<end>"
KO: "그게 좋겠어요<end>"

## 1330702
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここを通りたい？<line-break>冗談じゃない！<line-break> <line-break>勝手に人を通したのがばれたら、<line-break>酷い目に遭わされます。<line-break>お引取りを…<end>"
EN: "You want to pass through here? Don't be ridiculous! If anyone finds out I let you through without permission, I'll be in serious trouble. Please leave...<end>"
KO: "여길 지나가고 싶다고? 말도 안 돼! 멋대로 사람을 통과시킨 게 들키면 호되게 당합니다. 돌아가 주십시오….<end>"

## 1330703

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml

JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""

## 1330706
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来たか…。<line-break>アスティアの子よ。<line-break>我を倒さぬかぎり、闇の門は閉じぬ。<end>"
EN: "So you have come, child of Astia. The Dark Gate will not close unless you defeat me.<end>"
KO: "왔는가…. 아스티아의 아이여. 나를 쓰러뜨리지 않는 한 어둠의 문은 닫히지 않는다.<end>"

## 1330707
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "母も戻っては来ぬ。<line-break>お前がここに来るのも必然というワケか。<line-break> <line-break>よかろう。<line-break>このヴァシュタールを滅ぼしてみよ。<end>"
EN: "Nor will Mother return. So it seems your coming here was inevitable. Very well. Let us see you destroy Vashtar.<end>"
KO: "어머니도 돌아오지 않는다. 네가 여기 오는 것도 필연이라는 건가. 좋다. 이 바슈타르를 멸해 보아라.<end>"

## 1330708
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…無駄だ。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>いかなる力を持ってしても<line-break>我を滅することはできん。<line-break>我は不滅の存在。<end>"
EN: "It is futile. No power can destroy me. I am immortal.<end>"
KO: "…소용없다. 어떤 힘으로도 나를 멸할 수 없다. 나는 불멸의 존재다.<end>"

## 1330709
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "永遠の終わりを探し求める<line-break>朽ち果てぬ骸。<line-break>お前の憎しみも良い暇つぶしとなる。<line-break>また、会おう。<line-break>アスティアの子よ。<end>"
EN: "A corpse that will never decay, seeking an end to eternity. Your hatred will make a fine diversion. We shall meet again, child of Astia.<end>"
KO: "영원의 끝을 찾아 헤매는 썩지 않는 시체. 네 증오도 좋은 심심풀이가 되겠군. 또 만나자, 아스티아의 아이여.<end>"

## 1330710
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、これはウルグ様の力…。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>お前は一体！<line-break>まあ…よい。<line-break>お前こそ我が求めていた存在。<end>"
EN: "Th-this is the power of Ulugh... What are you? No matter. You are the one I have sought.<end>"
KO: "이, 이건 울그 님의 힘…. 너는 대체! 뭐… 됐다. 네가 바로 내가 찾던 존재다.<end>"

## 1330711
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、その力で我を撃て。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>闇の中の闇。<line-break>ウルグ様の力であれば我も<line-break>眠ることができよう。<end>"
EN: "Now strike me with that power. Darkness within darkness. With the power of Ulugh, perhaps even I may sleep.<end>"
KO: "자, 그 힘으로 나를 쏴라. 어둠 속의 어둠. 울그 님의 힘이라면 나도 잠들 수 있겠지.<end>"

## 1330712
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さらばだ…アスティアの子よ。<line-break>礼を言うぞ。<end>"
EN: "Farewell, child of Astia. You have my thanks.<end>"
KO: "잘 있어라… 아스티아의 아이여. 감사한다.<end>"

## 1330713
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　神聖暦<value:$24>年<value:$25>月　　　　<line-break>後に多くの吟遊詩人に歌われるこの騒乱の後<line-break>　　ディンガル帝国とロストール王国は　　<line-break>　　　　　和平条約を締結した。　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Sacred Calendar: Year <value:$24>, Month <value:$25>. Following this upheaval, later sung of by many bards, the Dyneskal Empire and the Kingdom of Rostorl concluded a peace treaty.<end>"
KO: "신성력 <value:$24>년 <value:$25>월 후에 수많은 음유시인이 노래하게 될 이 소란 뒤 딩갈 제국과 로스톨 왕국은 평화조약을 체결했다.<end>"

## 1330714
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、<line-break>わたしにはこんなことを言う<line-break>資格はないですけど…<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I know I have no right to say this, but...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨, 제가 이런 말을 할 자격은 없지만…<end>"

## 1330715
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "がんばってください…。<end>"
EN: "Please... keep going.<end>"
KO: "힘내세요….<end>"

## 1330716
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ様は、しばらく<line-break>お忙しいとのことです。<line-break>今は、お引き取りください。<end>"
EN: "Lady Karula will be occupied for some time. Please leave for now.<end>"
KO: "칼라 님은 당분간 바쁘시다고 합니다. 지금은 돌아가 주십시오.<end>"

## 1330717
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ。<value:$28>様で<line-break>ございますね。<line-break>どうぞお通りください！<end>"
EN: "Yes, you must be <value:$28>. Please, go on in!<end>"
KO: "예. <value:$28> 님이시군요. 부디 지나가십시오!<end>"

## 1330718
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様！<line-break>将軍閣下からご伝言がございます。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! I have a message for you from the General.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님! 장군 각하께서 전언을 보내셨습니다.<end>"

## 1330719
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "トンガリ頭がうるさいので<line-break>アルノートゥンまでデートに<line-break>行ってきます。<line-break>なんか、やな予感がするので<line-break>大人しく待っているように。<line-break>カルラ<end>"
EN: "Pointy-Head won't stop pestering me, so I'm off to Alnortun for a date. I've got a bad feeling about this, so stay put and wait for me. Karula<end>"
KO: "뾰족머리가 시끄러워서 알노툰까지 데이트하러 갑니다. 왠지 불길한 예감이 드니 얌전히 기다리고 있도록. 카를라<end>"

## 1330720
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "以上であります。<end>"
EN: "That is all.<end>"
KO: "이상입니다.<end>"

## 1330721
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僭越ですが…<value:$28>様。<line-break>私も何か嫌な予感がします。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me for saying so, <value:$28>, but I have a bad feeling about this as well.<end>"
KO: "외람되지만… <value:$28>님. 저도 왠지 불길한 예감이 듭니다.<end>"

## 1330722
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なにとぞ、<line-break><value:$28>様のお力を<line-break>カルラ様にお貸しください。<end>"
EN: "Please, <value:$28>. Lady Karula needs your help.<end>"
KO: "부디 <value:$28> 님의 힘을 카를라 님께 빌려주십시오.<end>"

## 1330723
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将軍の留守を預かるのが我らが仕事だ。<line-break>ロセン総督府にスキなぞない。<line-break>わかったら、去るがよかろう。<end>"
EN: "Our duty is to hold the fort while the General is away. The Rocen Governor-General's Office has no vulnerabilities. If you understand, be on your way.<end>"
KO: "장군의 부재를 지키는 것이 우리 임무다. 로센 총독부에는 빈틈 따윈 없어. 알았으면 물러가라.<end>"

## 1330724
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが<line-break><value:$2B>の<line-break><value:$28>でしたか。<line-break>カルラ様がお待ちです。<line-break>どうぞお通りください。<end>"

KO: "당신이 <value:$2B>의 <value:$28>이시군요. 카를라 님이 기다리고 계십니다. 들어가십시오.<end>"

## 1330725
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "너는… 아니, 실례했습니다. 카를라 님이 기다리고 계십니다. 들어가십시오.<end>"

## 1330735
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は<value:$28>様なら<line-break>閣下に追いつけると信じております。<line-break>我が軍の勝利のため、<line-break>一刻も早く将軍のもとへ！<end>"
EN: "I believe you can catch up with the General, <value:$28>. For our army's victory, hurry to her side at once!<end>"
KO: "저는 <value:$28>님이라면 각하를 따라잡을 수 있다고 믿습니다. 우리 군의 승리를 위해 한시라도 빨리 장군님께로!<end>"

## 1330736
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセン総督府に用か？<line-break>カルラ将軍なら、あいにく、<line-break>遠征中である。<end>"
EN: "Do you have business at the Rocen Governor-General's Office? Unfortunately, General Karula is away on campaign.<end>"
KO: "로센 총독부에 볼일인가? 카를라 장군은 공교롭게도 원정 중이다.<end>"

## 1330737
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、お主が<value:$28>か？<line-break>なるほど堂々とした面構え。<line-break>カルラ様がお待ちだ。通れ。<end>"
EN: "Y-you're <value:$28>? I see. You certainly look the part. Lady Karula is expecting you. Go on through.<end>"
KO: "네, 네놈이 <value:$28>인가? 과연 당당한 얼굴이군. 카를라 님이 기다리신다. 지나가라.<end>"

## 1330738
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、お主は！　いや…<line-break>カルラ様がお待ちです。<end>どうぞ……<end>"
EN: "Y-you! No... Lady Karula is expecting you.<end>Go on...<end>"
KO: "너, 너는! 아니… 카를라 님이 기다리고 계신다.<end>들어가라……<end>"

## 1330739
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "너, 너는! 아니… 카를라 님이 기다리고 계신다.<end>들어가라……<end>"

## 1330740
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将軍閣下はお忙しい。<line-break>結果を出すまでお会いには<line-break>ならぬとのことだ。<end>"
EN: "The General is busy. You will not be received until you have produced results.<end>"
KO: "장군 각하는 바쁘시다. 성과를 내기 전에는 만나지 않겠다고 하셨다.<end>"

## 1330741
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "카를라 님은 당분간 바쁘시다고 합니다. 돌아가 주십시오.<end>"

## 1330742
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Lady Karula will be occupied for some time. Please leave.<end>"
KO: "카를라 님은 당분간 바쁘시다고 합니다. 돌아가 주십시오.<end>"

## 1330743
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王…様、<line-break>わたしは…<line-break>もう戦えません。<end>"
EN: "Dragon King... I... cannot fight anymore.<end>"
KO: "용왕…님, 저는… 이제 싸울 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 1330744
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　ならばノエルよ…　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　　汝のもつ<line-break>　　　　　　　無限のソウルは、<line-break>　　　　　この竜王が役立てよう…<end>"
EN: "Then, Noel... this Dragon King shall put your Infinite Soul to use...<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 노엘이여… 그대가 지닌 무한의 소울은 이 용왕이 유용하게 쓰리라…<end>"

## 1330745
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　いまひとりの　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者…<line-break>　　　　　　　<value:$28>よ…<line-break>　　　　　　　　聞くがよい…<end>"
EN: "Another Bearer of the Infinite Soul... <value:$28>... listen well...<end>"
KO: "또 한 명의 무한의 소울을 지닌 자… <value:$28>여… 들어라….<end>"

## 1330746
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　汝の力は強大すぎる…　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　世界の均衡を崩しかねぬ…<line-break>　　　　　　ゆえに、我みずから<line-break>　　　　　その強大な力を封じよう…<end>"
EN: "Your power is too great... It could upset the Balance of the World... Therefore, I shall seal away that immense power myself...<end>"
KO: "그대의 힘은 너무 강대하다… 세계의 균형을 무너뜨릴 수도 있다… 그러므로 내가 직접 그 강대한 힘을 봉인하겠다…<end>"

## 1330747
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　さぁ、くるがよい…　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　汝の前に開けし門が…<line-break>　　　　　　　汝を、我が前へと<line-break>　　　　　　　　呼びよせん…<end>"
EN: "Come, then... The gate that has opened before you... shall summon you into my presence...<end>"
KO: "자, 오너라…. 그대 앞에 열린 문이 그대를 내 앞으로 불러오리라….<end>"

## 1330748
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（早く脱出しなければ…）<end>"
EN: "(I have to get out of here, quickly...)<end>"
KO: "(빨리 탈출해야 해…)<end>"

## 1330749
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…終わったか。<line-break>人類の革新を急がねば、<line-break>この悲しみは繰り返される。<line-break>人は…<line-break>人は強くあらねばならんのだ。<end>"
EN: "So... it's over. Unless Humanity's Evolution is hastened, this sorrow will only repeat itself. People... People must be strong.<end>"
KO: "…끝났나. 인류의 혁신을 서두르지 않으면 이 슬픔은 반복된다. 인간은… 인간은 강해야 한다.<end>"

## 1330750
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふっ…そうか。<end>"
EN: "Hmph... I see.<end>"
KO: "훗… 그런가.<end>"

## 1330751
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、ぬかったわ…。<end>"
EN: "Damn... I was careless...<end>"
KO: "큭, 방심했군….<end>"

## 1330752
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よもや…円卓騎士マゴスの支配を<line-break>はねのけるとは…。<line-break>　<line-break>フッ…。どうやらわしも…<line-break>ここまでのようじゃ…。<line-break>あとは…シャリに…託すとしよう…。<line-break>邪眼帝…バロル様の復活を…な…。<end>"
EN: "Never did I imagine... that you would throw off the control of Magos, Knight of the Round Table... Hmph. It seems this is... the end for me. I shall leave the rest... to Shalli... the resurrection of Lord Balor... the Evil-Eyed Emperor...<end>"
KO: "설마… 원탁기사 마고스의 지배를 떨쳐 내다니…. 훗…. 아무래도 나도… 여기까지인 모양이군…. 나머지는… 샤리에게… 맡기도록 하지…. 사안제… 발로르 님의 부활을… 말이다….<end>"

## 1330753
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、ぬかったわ…。<end>"
EN: "Damn... I was careless...<end>"
KO: "큭, 방심했군….<end>"

## 1330754
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よもや…円卓騎士マゴスを…<line-break>もってしても…滅ぼせぬとは…。<line-break>さすがは…無限のソウルの持ち主…。<line-break>フッ…。どうやらわしも…<line-break>ここまでのようじゃ…。<line-break>あとは…シャリに…託すとしよう…。<line-break>邪眼帝…バロル様の復活を…な…。<end>"
EN: "Never did I imagine... that even Magos, Knight of the Round Table... would fail to destroy you. As expected... of one who bears the Infinite Soul... Hmph. It seems this is... the end for me. I shall leave the rest... to Shalli... the resurrection of Lord Balor... the Evil-Eyed Emperor...<end>"
KO: "설마… 원탁기사 마고스까지… 가지고도… 멸하지 못하다니…. 역시… 무한의 소울을 지닌 자…. 후…. 나도… 여기까지인 모양이군…. 나머지는… 샤리에게… 맡기도록 하지…. 사안제… 발로르 님의 부활을… 말이다….<end>"

## 1330755
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう誰にも止められないよ。<line-break>強大な闇の巨人が世界中を襲ってるのさ。<line-break>でも、これはほんの序の口<line-break>ここからが本番だ。<end>"
EN: "No one can stop it now. Mighty Giants of Darkness are attacking all over the world. But that's only the beginning. Now the real show starts.<end>"
KO: "이제 아무도 못 막아. 강대한 어둠의 거인이 세계 곳곳을 습격하고 있어. 하지만 이건 시작에 불과해. 이제부터가 본론이야.<end>"

## 1330756
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう誰にも止められないよ。<line-break>強大な闇の巨人が世界中を<line-break>襲いはじめたんだ。<line-break>でも、これはほんの序の口。<line-break>ここからが本番さ。<line-break>　<line-break>だけど、本番なのに弱ったな。<line-break>エルファスが君の仲間になって<line-break>舞台を降りちゃったからね。<line-break>しょうがないや、<line-break>エルファスの代役を<line-break>立てさせてもらうとするかな。<line-break>破壊の化身、魔王バロルをね。<end>"
EN: "No one can stop it now. Mighty Giants of Darkness have begun attacking all over the world. But that's only the beginning. Now the real show starts. Though I've got a problem: Elfas joined you and left the stage just before the main event. Oh well. I'll simply cast a replacement for him: the Embodiment of Destruction, Balor, Demon King.<end>"
KO: "이제 아무도 막을 수 없어. 강대한 어둠의 거인들이 온 세계를 습격하기 시작했거든. 하지만 이건 겨우 시작이야. 이제부터가 본편이지. 그런데 본편인데 곤란하게 됐네. 엘파스가 네 동료가 돼서 무대에서 내려가 버렸잖아. 어쩔 수 없지. 엘파스 대신 대역을 세워야겠어. 파괴의 화신, 마왕 발로르를 말이야.<end>"

## 1330757
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の巨人をこれだけ<line-break>呼び寄せることができたから<line-break>バロルも復活できた。<line-break>それもすべて、<line-break>強大な闇の力の持ち主である<line-break>闇の王女がいればこそだけどね。<end>"
EN: "Summoning this many Giants of Darkness made it possible to revive Balor as well. Of course, none of it would have been possible without the Princess of Darkness and her tremendous dark power.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 거인을 이만큼 불러모을 수 있었기에 발로르도 부활할 수 있었어. 그것도 모두 강대한 어둠의 힘을 지닌 어둠의 왕녀가 있었기 때문이지만.<end>"

## 1330758
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう、<value:$28>。<line-break>彼女を闇に突き落とした<line-break>最大の功労者は君だよ。アハハハ！<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. No one did more than you to cast her into darkness. Ahahaha!<end>"
KO: "고마워, <value:$28>. 그녀를 어둠에 빠뜨린 최대 공로자는 너야. 아하하하!<end>"

## 1330759
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec133-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我ハ己の欲するままに…世界ヲ支配し…<line-break>　　　破壊シ…消滅させるのみ…。<end>"
EN: "I shall do as I desire: rule the world... destroy it... erase it from existence. Nothing more...<end>"
KO: "나는 내가 원하는 대로… 세계를 지배하고… 파괴하고… 소멸시킬 뿐….<end>"
